<!--
Focus Notes v1.16.3 corrects RAD command keyword forms, hardens diagnostics, and keeps model-specific command and port-reference behavior.
Focus Notes - SecureCRT Toolkit
Standalone offline Chrome work aid; no external scripts, browser storage, or network calls.
Build version and date come from the HTML data attributes below.
v1.12.39 removes only proven-dead or overridden CSS while preserving approved behavior and visuals.
v1.12.40 restores a multiline Issue workspace with a native nine-preset dropdown while preserving custom typing and existing click behavior.
v1.12.41 hardens the permanent Start New / Copy Notes action bar with full-size hit targets and direct native click bindings.
v1.12.42 preserves selected Issue text during preset insertion, blocks action-bar click-through, reserves keyboard focus clearance, enlarges secondary hit targets, and improves workflow heading contrast.
v1.12.43 simplifies visible command names, shortens builder headers to CER/CES/NID, and removes the retired command-review/import workflow while retaining Tool Self-Test.
v1.12.44 removes the stale Start New reference to saved review commands after that workflow was retired.
v1.12.45 makes the full CER/CES/NID headers clickable with centered labels and adds privacy-safe copyable self-test diagnostics.
v1.12.46 hardens calculator startup for managed Chrome, removes CER/CES/NID header dots, compacts Tool Self-Test, and improves calculator/target diagnostics.
v1.12.47 upgrades Tool Self-Test to Focus Notes Diagnostics with local-only quick/full diagnostics, state-safe checks, measurements, and richer privacy-safe copy reports.
v1.12.48 improves scanability and scalability with clearer group hierarchy, a centered 720px content cap, responsive Quick Scratch sizing, quieter bottom-positioned Diagnostics, and accessibility refinements.
v1.12.49 removes five proven-dead Diagnostics CSS declarations while preserving v1.12.48 layout and behavior.
v1.13.0 packages the toolkit as executable HTML, upgrades copy targets to native buttons, and removes duplicate calculator startup work without changing the command library or approved layout.
v1.13.1 keeps native copy buttons, restores the independent managed-Chrome calculator startup path as the sole startup owner, removes redundant native-button key handlers, and preserves approved copy-control visuals.
v1.13.2 stabilizes Quick Scratch against viewport-driven reflow, matches its default height to Caller Identity, and adds state-safe auto-grow/shrink behavior to Quick Scratch, Issue, and Troubleshooting.
v1.13.3 adds a compact CES NID IP lookup workflow and a read-only CER route-by-interface lookup without duplicating the existing MAC-table command.
v1.13.4 prevents Quick Scratch keyboard editing from moving the workflow below it by restoring the intended scroll position after browser scroll anchoring and non-input key actions complete.
v1.13.5 replaces key-by-key Quick Scratch scroll compensation with visual anchoring so the workflow below Quick Scratch stays at the same on-screen position while typing, editing, pasting, or auto-growing.
v1.13.6 preserves the Start New working scroll position while Quick Scratch grows, keeping Ticket Template tucked behind the sticky scratchpad and Caller Identity directly below it.
v1.13.7 removes the Ticket Template box and converts Next Step / Waiting On to the same preset-plus-multiline workflow pattern as Issue while preserving the five approved Next Step presets and custom text.
v1.13.8 fixes false-positive export privacy diagnostics for preset selects, keeps Issue/Next Step diagnostic probes state-safe with auto-grow restoration, hardens saved-copy reset for both presets, and removes two proven-redundant CSS declarations without changing workflow behavior.
v1.13.9 separates Quick Scratch's "Not Copied" privacy cue from its title while preserving note content and the command library.
v1.13.10 tunes the desktop widget for easier scanning with clearer section labels, stronger placeholder contrast, and visible command-panel disclosure cues.
v1.13.11 removes the Quick Scratch hide control; the desktop scratchpad stays expanded and aligned with Caller Identity.
v1.13.12 gives Next Step / Waiting On its own muted-violet section accent so it is visually distinct from Troubleshooting without changing workflow behavior.
-->

<script id="diagnosticRuntimeCaptureScript">
(function () {
    "use strict";

    var runtimeErrors = [];

    function cleanDiagnosticText(value) {
        return String(value == null ? "" : value).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 1200);
    }

    function addRuntimeError(type, message, _origin, line, column) {
        runtimeErrors.push({
            type: type,
            message: cleanDiagnosticText(message) || "Unknown runtime error",
            line: Number(line) || 0,
            column: Number(column) || 0
        });
        if (runtimeErrors.length > 25) runtimeErrors.shift();
    }

    window.addEventListener("error", function (event) {
        addRuntimeError(
            "JavaScript error",
            event.message || (event.error && event.error.message),
            event.filename,
            event.lineno,
            event.colno
        );
    });

    window.addEventListener("unhandledrejection", function (event) {
        var reason = event.reason;
        addRuntimeError(
            "Unhandled promise rejection",
            reason && reason.message ? reason.message : reason,
            "",
            0,
            0
        );
    });

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS = Object.freeze({
        list: function () { return runtimeErrors.slice(); }
    });
})();
</script>

<script>
    /* ADDRESS_NORMALIZER_START */
    const ADDRESS_STATE_MAP = Object.freeze(    {
        "alabama": "AL", "al": "AL", "alaska": "AK", "ak": "AK",
        "arizona": "AZ", "az": "AZ", "arkansas": "AR", "ar": "AR",
        "california": "CA", "ca": "CA", "colorado": "CO", "co": "CO",
        "connecticut": "CT", "ct": "CT", "delaware": "DE", "de": "DE",
        "district of columbia": "DC", "dc": "DC",
        "florida": "FL", "fl": "FL", "georgia": "GA", "ga": "GA",
        "hawaii": "HI", "hi": "HI", "idaho": "ID", "id": "ID",
        "illinois": "IL", "il": "IL", "indiana": "IN", "in": "IN",
        "iowa": "IA", "ia": "IA", "kansas": "KS", "ks": "KS",
        "kentucky": "KY", "ky": "KY", "louisiana": "LA", "la": "LA",
        "maine": "ME", "me": "ME", "maryland": "MD", "md": "MD",
        "massachusetts": "MA", "ma": "MA", "michigan": "MI", "mi": "MI",
        "minnesota": "MN", "mn": "MN", "mississippi": "MS", "ms": "MS",
        "missouri": "MO", "mo": "MO", "montana": "MT", "mt": "MT",
        "nebraska": "NE", "ne": "NE", "nevada": "NV", "nv": "NV",
        "new hampshire": "NH", "nh": "NH", "new jersey": "NJ", "nj": "NJ",
        "new mexico": "NM", "nm": "NM", "new york": "NY", "ny": "NY",
        "north carolina": "NC", "nc": "NC", "north dakota": "ND", "nd": "ND",
        "ohio": "OH", "oh": "OH", "oklahoma": "OK", "ok": "OK",
        "oregon": "OR", "or": "OR", "pennsylvania": "PA", "pa": "PA",
        "rhode island": "RI", "ri": "RI", "south carolina": "SC", "sc": "SC",
        "south dakota": "SD", "sd": "SD", "tennessee": "TN", "tn": "TN",
        "texas": "TX", "tx": "TX", "utah": "UT", "ut": "UT",
        "vermont": "VT", "vt": "VT", "virginia": "VA", "va": "VA",
        "washington": "WA", "wa": "WA", "west virginia": "WV", "wv": "WV",
        "wisconsin": "WI", "wi": "WI", "wyoming": "WY", "wy": "WY"
    });
    const ADDRESS_SUFFIX_MAP = Object.freeze(    {
        "alley": "Aly", "aly": "Aly", "avenue": "Ave", "ave": "Ave",
        "boulevard": "Blvd", "blvd": "Blvd", "circle": "Cir", "cir": "Cir",
        "court": "Ct", "ct": "Ct", "cove": "Cv", "cv": "Cv", "drive": "Dr", "dr": "Dr",
        "expressway": "Expy", "expy": "Expy", "highway": "Hwy", "hwy": "Hwy",
        "lane": "Ln", "ln": "Ln", "loop": "Loop", "parkway": "Pkwy", "pkwy": "Pkwy",
        "place": "Pl", "pl": "Pl", "road": "Rd", "rd": "Rd",
        "square": "Sq", "sq": "Sq", "street": "St", "st": "St",
        "terrace": "Ter", "ter": "Ter", "trail": "Trl", "trl": "Trl",
        "turnpike": "Tpke", "tpke": "Tpke", "way": "Way"
    });
    const ADDRESS_DIRECTION_MAP = Object.freeze(    {
        "north": "N", "n": "N", "south": "S", "s": "S",
        "east": "E", "e": "E", "west": "W", "w": "W",
        "northeast": "NE", "ne": "NE", "northwest": "NW", "nw": "NW",
        "southeast": "SE", "se": "SE", "southwest": "SW", "sw": "SW"
    });

    function normalizeServiceAddress(rawValue) {



        function titleToken(token) {
            if (!token) return token;
            if (/^\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)$/i.test(token)) return token.toLowerCase();
            if (/^[A-Z]*\d[A-Z0-9-]*$/i.test(token)) return token.toUpperCase();
            return token.split("-").map(function (part) {
                return part.split("'").map(function (piece) {
                    if (!piece) return piece;
                    return piece.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + piece.slice(1).toLowerCase();
                }).join("'");
            }).join("-");
        }

        function normalizeStreet(street) {
            const tokens = street.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
            let suffixIndex = -1;

            for (let i = 0; i < tokens.length; i += 1) {
                const key = tokens[i].toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                if (ADDRESS_SUFFIX_MAP[key]) suffixIndex = i;
            }

            return tokens.map(function (token, index) {
                const key = token.toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                if (ADDRESS_DIRECTION_MAP[key]) return ADDRESS_DIRECTION_MAP[key];
                if (index === suffixIndex && ADDRESS_SUFFIX_MAP[key]) return ADDRESS_SUFFIX_MAP[key];
                if (["us", "sr", "cr", "fm"].includes(key)) return key.toUpperCase();
                return titleToken(token);
            }).join(" ");
        }

        function normalizeCity(city) {
            const smallWords = new Set(["and", "at", "by", "for", "in", "of", "on", "the"]);
            return city.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(function (token, index) {
                const key = token.toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                if (index > 0 && smallWords.has(key)) return key;
                if (["nyc", "dc"].includes(key)) return key.toUpperCase();
                return titleToken(token);
            }).join(" ");
        }

        function splitStreetAndCity(prefix) {
            const commaParts = prefix.split(",").map(function (part) { return part.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
            if (commaParts.length >= 2) {
                let streetIndex = commaParts.findIndex(function (part) {
                    return /^\d/.test(part) && part.split(/\s+/).some(function (token) {
                        return Boolean(ADDRESS_SUFFIX_MAP[token.toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "")]);
                    });
                });
                if (streetIndex < 0) streetIndex = 0;
                return { street: commaParts[streetIndex], city: commaParts[commaParts.length - 1] };
            }

            const tokens = prefix.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
            const suffixIndexes = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < tokens.length; i += 1) {
                const key = tokens[i].toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                if (ADDRESS_SUFFIX_MAP[key]) suffixIndexes.push(i);
            }

            if (suffixIndexes.length) {
                // The street suffix is normally the first suffix-like cluster after the house
                // number. Keeping consecutive suffix words together preserves names such as
                // "Palm Cove Loop", while preventing city names such as "Temple Terrace" or
                // "Green Cove Springs" from being mistaken for part of the street.
                let streetEnd = suffixIndexes[0];
                for (let i = 1; i < suffixIndexes.length; i += 1) {
                    if (suffixIndexes[i] !== streetEnd + 1) break;
                    streetEnd = suffixIndexes[i];
                }

                const selectedKey = (tokens[streetEnd] || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                const nextToken = tokens[streetEnd + 1] || "";
                const routeContext = tokens.slice(Math.max(0, streetEnd - 2), streetEnd).map(function (token) {
                    return token.toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                });
                const routeNumberFollows = /^\d+[A-Za-z-]*$/.test(nextToken) && (
                    ["highway", "hwy"].includes(selectedKey) ||
                    routeContext.some(function (token) {
                        return ["state", "county", "route", "us", "sr", "cr", "fm"].includes(token);
                    })
                );
                if (routeNumberFollows) streetEnd += 1;

                const directionKey = (tokens[streetEnd + 1] || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, "");
                if (ADDRESS_DIRECTION_MAP[directionKey]) streetEnd += 1;

                return {
                    street: tokens.slice(0, streetEnd + 1).join(" "),
                    city: tokens.slice(streetEnd + 1).join(" ")
                };
            }
            return { street: prefix, city: "" };
        }

        let value = String(rawValue == null ? "" : rawValue)
            .replace(/\u00a0/g, " ")
            .replace(/[\r\n]+/g, ", ")
            .trim();

        if (!value) return "";

        value = value
            .replace(/^(?:(?:service|customer|site|physical|billing|mailing|location)\s+)?address\s*[:\-]\s*/i, "")
            .replace(/^(?:service|customer|site|location)\s*[:\-]\s*/i, "")
            .replace(/\b[A-Z0-9._%+\-]+@[A-Z0-9.\-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}\b/gi, " ")
            .replace(/(^|[^\d])(?:\+?1[\s.\-]?)?\(?\d{3}\)?[\s.\-]\d{3}[\s.\-]\d{4}\b/g, "$1")
            .replace(/\s*(?:\||;)\s*.*$/, "")
            .replace(/(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)\s+(?:near|behind|across\s+from|beside|adjacent\s+to|next\s+to)\b.*$/i, "$1")
            .replace(/,\s*(?:near|behind|across\s+from|beside|adjacent\s+to|next\s+to)\b.*$/i, "")
            .replace(/\b(?:next\s+to|behind|across\s+from|beside|adjacent\s+to)\b.*$/i, "")
            .replace(/\b(?:phone|telephone|tel|contact|gate\s+code|directions?|notes?|landmark)\s*[:\-].*$/i, "");

        const secondaryPatterns = [
            /\b(?:apt|apartment|ste|suite|unit|bldg|building|room|rm|dept|department|lot|trailer|trlr|hangar|hngr|space|spc)\.?\s*(?:no\.?\s*)?#?\s*[A-Z0-9-]+\b/gi,
            /\b\d+(?:st|nd|rd|th)\s+floor\b/gi,
            /\bfloor\s+[A-Z0-9-]+\b/gi,
            /(?:^|[\s,])#\s*[A-Z0-9-]+\b/gi
        ];
        secondaryPatterns.forEach(function (pattern) {
            value = value.replace(pattern, " ");
        });

        value = value
            .replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9\s,.'\-]/g, " ")
            .replace(/\s*,\s*/g, ", ")
            .replace(/(?:,\s*){2,}/g, ", ")
            .replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ")
            .replace(/^,\s*|,\s*$/g, "")
            .trim();

        const stateKeys = Object.keys(ADDRESS_STATE_MAP).sort(function (a, b) { return b.length - a.length; });
        const statePattern = stateKeys.map(function (key) {
            return key.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
        }).join("|");
        const stateZipRegex = new RegExp("(?:,?\\s+)(" + statePattern + ")\\s+(\\d{5}(?:-\\d{4})?)\\s*$", "i");
        const match = value.match(stateZipRegex);

        if (!match) {
            return value.split(",").map(function (part, index) {
                return index === 0 ? normalizeStreet(part.trim()) : normalizeCity(part.trim());
            }).filter(Boolean).join(", ");
        }

        const state = ADDRESS_STATE_MAP[match[1].toLowerCase()];
        const zip = match[2];
        const prefix = value.slice(0, match.index).replace(/,\s*$/, "").trim();
        const parts = splitStreetAndCity(prefix);
        const street = normalizeStreet(parts.street);
        const city = normalizeCity(parts.city);

        if (street && city) return street + ", " + city + ", " + state + " " + zip;
        if (street) return street + ", " + state + " " + zip;
        return state + " " + zip;
    }

    function formatAddressField(field) {
        if (!field) return "";
        const formatted = normalizeServiceAddress(field.value);
        field.value = formatted;
        return formatted;
    }
/* ADDRESS_NORMALIZER_END */

    function clearRegionalCopyFeedback() {
        var directory = document.getElementById("regionalSupportDirectory");
        if (!directory) return;
        directory.querySelectorAll("[data-copy-value]").forEach(function (element) {
            if (element._focusNotesCopyTimer) clearTimeout(element._focusNotesCopyTimer);
            if (typeof element._focusNotesCopyOriginal === "string") element.innerText = element._focusNotesCopyOriginal;
            if (typeof element._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria === "string") element.setAttribute("aria-label", element._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria);
            element.style.background = "";
            element.style.color = "";
            element._focusNotesCopyTimer = 0;
            delete element._focusNotesCopyOriginal;
            delete element._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria;
        });
    }

    function formatRegionalSupportManagementArea(value) {
        return String(value || "").split(/,\s*/).filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    }

    function formatRegionalSupportHeaderRegion(record) {
        if (!record) return "";
        return record.region === "New York City (NYC)" ? "NYC" : record.region;
    }

    function formatRegionalSupportTime(value) {
        return String(value || "")
            .replace(/\b(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?(am|pm)\b/gi, function (_, hour, minutes, period) {
                return hour + ":" + (minutes || "00") + " " + period.toUpperCase();
            })
            .replace(/\s*-\s*/g, "–")
            .replace(/Sat-Sun/gi, "SAT–SUN");
    }

    function createRegionalContactRow(label, value, copyValue, copyLabel, options) {
        options = options || {};
        var kind = options.kind || "text";
        var row = document.createElement("div");
        row.className = "regional-contact-row regional-contact-row-" + kind + (copyValue ? " has-copy" : "");
        if (options.emphasis) row.classList.add("regional-contact-row-" + kind + "-" + options.emphasis);

        var copy = document.createElement("div");
        copy.className = "regional-contact-copy";

        var labelNode = document.createElement("span");
        labelNode.className = "regional-contact-label";
        labelNode.textContent = label;
        copy.appendChild(labelNode);

        if (value) {
            var valueNode = document.createElement("span");
            valueNode.className = "regional-contact-value";
            valueNode.textContent = value;
            copy.appendChild(valueNode);
        }

        row.appendChild(copy);

        if (copyValue) {
            var button = document.createElement("button");
            button.type = "button";
            button.className = "copy-pill";
            button.setAttribute("aria-label", copyLabel);
            button.setAttribute("data-copy-value", copyValue);
            button.setAttribute("data-copy-feedback-full", "true");
            button.textContent = options.buttonText || "COPY";
            row.appendChild(button);
        }

        return row;
    }

    function createRegionalHoursRow(hours) {
        var row = document.createElement("div");
        row.className = "regional-contact-row regional-contact-row-hours";

        var copy = document.createElement("div");
        copy.className = "regional-contact-copy";

        var label = document.createElement("span");
        label.className = "regional-contact-label";
        label.textContent = "Hours";
        copy.appendChild(label);

        if (hours === "24/7") {
            var alwaysOpen = document.createElement("span");
            alwaysOpen.className = "regional-hours-value is-always-open";
            alwaysOpen.textContent = "24/7";
            copy.appendChild(alwaysOpen);
        } else if (/Sat-Sun/i.test(hours)) {
            var parts = String(hours).split(/,\s*(?=Sat-Sun)/i);
            var lines = document.createElement("div");
            lines.className = "regional-hours-lines";

            [["Daily", parts[0]], ["Sat–Sun", (parts[1] || "").replace(/^Sat-Sun\s*/i, "")]].forEach(function (item) {
                var line = document.createElement("div");
                line.className = "regional-hours-line";

                var lineLabel = document.createElement("span");
                lineLabel.className = "regional-hours-line-label";
                lineLabel.textContent = item[0];

                var lineValue = document.createElement("span");
                lineValue.className = "regional-hours-line-value";
                lineValue.textContent = formatRegionalSupportTime(item[1]);

                line.appendChild(lineLabel);
                line.appendChild(lineValue);
                lines.appendChild(line);
            });
            copy.appendChild(lines);
        } else {
            var value = document.createElement("span");
            value.className = "regional-hours-value";
            value.textContent = formatRegionalSupportTime(hours);
            copy.appendChild(value);
        }

        row.appendChild(copy);
        return row;
    }

    function setRegionalFieldGroupsAvailable(available) {
        var groups = [
            [document.getElementById("rocContactDetails"), document.getElementById("rocContactPurpose"), "Phone"],
            [document.getElementById("nfsContactDetails"), document.getElementById("nfsContactPurpose"), "Hours + Email"]
        ];
        groups.forEach(function (entry) {
            var details = entry[0];
            var purpose = entry[1];
            if (!details || !purpose) return;
            details.classList.toggle("is-unavailable", !available);
            details.setAttribute("aria-disabled", available ? "false" : "true");
            purpose.textContent = available ? entry[2] : "Select Region";
            if (!available) details.open = false;
        });
    }

    function renderRegionalSupportContact() {
        var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        var summaryRegion = document.getElementById("regionalSupportSummaryRegion");
        var shared = document.getElementById("regionalSupportShared");
        var managementArea = document.getElementById("regionalSupportManagementArea");
        var rocBody = document.getElementById("rocContactBody");
        var nfsBody = document.getElementById("nfsContactBody");
        if (!select || !summaryRegion || !shared || !managementArea || !rocBody || !nfsBody) return null;

        clearRegionalCopyFeedback();
        var record = REGIONAL_SUPPORT_DATA[select.value] || null;
        select.classList.toggle("has-selection", !!record);

        if (!record) {
            summaryRegion.textContent = "";
            summaryRegion.hidden = true;
            shared.hidden = true;
            managementArea.textContent = "";
            rocBody.innerHTML = "";
            nfsBody.innerHTML = "";
            setRegionalFieldGroupsAvailable(false);
            return null;
        }

        summaryRegion.textContent = formatRegionalSupportHeaderRegion(record);
        summaryRegion.hidden = false;
        shared.hidden = false;
        managementArea.textContent = formatRegionalSupportManagementArea(record.managementArea);
        setRegionalFieldGroupsAvailable(true);

        rocBody.innerHTML = "";
        rocBody.appendChild(createRegionalContactRow(
            "Phone",
            "",
            record.roc.phone,
            "Copy " + record.region + " ROC phone " + record.roc.phone,
            { kind: "phone", buttonText: record.roc.phone }
        ));

        nfsBody.innerHTML = "";
        nfsBody.appendChild(createRegionalHoursRow(record.nfs.hours));
        record.nfs.emails.forEach(function (email) {var visibleLabel = email.label === "Email" ? "Email" : email.label + " Email";
            var emphasis = email.label === "Primary" ? "primary" : (email.label === "Secondary" ? "secondary" : "standard");
            nfsBody.appendChild(createRegionalContactRow(
                visibleLabel,
                email.address,
                email.address,
                "Copy " + record.region + " NFS " + visibleLabel + " " + email.address,
                { kind: "email", buttonText: "COPY", emphasis: emphasis }
            ));
        });

        return record;
    }
    window.renderRegionalSupportContact = renderRegionalSupportContact;

    function focusRegionalSupportRegion() {
        var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        if (!select) return;
        try { select.focus({ preventScroll: false }); }
        catch (error) { select.focus(); }
        select.classList.remove("regional-support-select-prompt");
        void select.offsetWidth;
        select.classList.add("regional-support-select-prompt");
        if (select._regionalPromptTimer) clearTimeout(select._regionalPromptTimer);
        select._regionalPromptTimer = setTimeout(function () {
            select.classList.remove("regional-support-select-prompt");
            select._regionalPromptTimer = 0;
        }, 900);
    }

    function handleRegionalFieldGroupSummaryClick(event) {
        var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        if (select && !select.value) {
            event.preventDefault();
            focusRegionalSupportRegion();
        }
    }

    function handleRegionalFieldGroupToggle(details, otherDetails) {
        var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        if (!details.open) return;
        if (!select || !select.value) {
            details.open = false;
            focusRegionalSupportRegion();
            return;
        }
        if (otherDetails) otherDetails.open = false;
    }

    function resetRegionalSupportUi() {
        var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        if (select) {
            select.value = "";
            select.classList.remove("has-selection", "regional-support-select-prompt");
            if (select._regionalPromptTimer) clearTimeout(select._regionalPromptTimer);
            select._regionalPromptTimer = 0;
        }
        renderRegionalSupportContact();
        ["regionalSupportDirectory", "rocContactDetails", "nfsContactDetails"].forEach(function (id) {
            var details = document.getElementById(id);
            if (details) details.open = false;
        });
    }
    window.resetRegionalSupportUi = resetRegionalSupportUi;

    function initializeRegionalSupportDirectory() {
        var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        var rocDetails = document.getElementById("rocContactDetails");
        var nfsDetails = document.getElementById("nfsContactDetails");
        renderRegionalSupportContact();
        if (!select || !rocDetails || !nfsDetails || rocDetails.dataset.regionalDirectoryInitialized === "true") return;

        rocDetails.dataset.regionalDirectoryInitialized = "true";
        nfsDetails.dataset.regionalDirectoryInitialized = "true";
        rocDetails.querySelector(":scope > summary").addEventListener("click", handleRegionalFieldGroupSummaryClick);
        nfsDetails.querySelector(":scope > summary").addEventListener("click", handleRegionalFieldGroupSummaryClick);
        rocDetails.addEventListener("toggle", function () { handleRegionalFieldGroupToggle(rocDetails, nfsDetails); });
        nfsDetails.addEventListener("toggle", function () { handleRegionalFieldGroupToggle(nfsDetails, rocDetails); });
    }
    window.initializeRegionalSupportDirectory = initializeRegionalSupportDirectory;

    function fallbackCopy(text) {
        const ta = document.createElement("textarea");
        ta.value = String(text == null ? "" : text);
        ta.setAttribute("readonly", "");
        ta.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
        ta.style.position = "fixed";
        ta.style.opacity = "0";
        ta.style.left = "-9999px";
        ta.style.top = "0";
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.focus();
        ta.select();

        let success = false;
        try {
            success = typeof document.execCommand === "function" && document.execCommand("copy");
        } catch (error) {
            success = false;
        }

        document.body.removeChild(ta);
        return success;
    }

    function clearManualCopyFallback(restoreFocus) {
        const overlay = document.getElementById("manualCopyFallback");
        if (!overlay) return;
        const textarea = document.getElementById("manualCopyText");
        const note = document.getElementById("manualCopyMessage");
        if (textarea) textarea.value = "";
        if (note) note.textContent = "";
        overlay.hidden = true;
        overlay.style.display = "none";
        if (restoreFocus !== false && overlay._focusReturn && document.contains(overlay._focusReturn)) {
            try { overlay._focusReturn.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }
            catch (error) { overlay._focusReturn.focus(); }
        }
        overlay._focusReturn = null;
    }

    function showManualCopyFallback(text, message) {
        let overlay = document.getElementById("manualCopyFallback");

        if (!overlay) {
            overlay = document.createElement("div");
            overlay.id = "manualCopyFallback";
            overlay.setAttribute("role", "dialog");
            overlay.setAttribute("aria-modal", "true");
            overlay.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", "manualCopyTitle");
            overlay.hidden = true;
            overlay.style.cssText = "position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10000;background:rgba(0,0,0,.82);padding:24px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;";

            const panel = document.createElement("div");
            panel.style.cssText = "width:min(760px,100%);max-height:90vh;overflow:auto;background:#171f25;border:1px solid #6fa8c7;border-radius:10px;padding:16px;color:#e8f0f3;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.55);";

            const title = document.createElement("h2");
            title.id = "manualCopyTitle";
            title.textContent = "Manual Copy";
            title.style.cssText = "margin:0 0 8px;font-size:1rem;";

            const note = document.createElement("p");
            note.id = "manualCopyMessage";
            note.style.cssText = "margin:0 0 10px;color:#a8b6bd;font-size:.88rem;";

            const textarea = document.createElement("textarea");
            textarea.id = "manualCopyText";
            textarea.setAttribute("readonly", "");
            textarea.setAttribute("aria-label", "Text to copy manually");
            textarea.style.cssText = "width:100%;min-height:240px;resize:vertical;font-family:var(--font-mono);";

            const close = document.createElement("button");
            close.type = "button";
            close.textContent = "Close";
            close.style.cssText = "margin-top:10px;width:100%;";
            close.addEventListener("click", function () { clearManualCopyFallback(true); });

            overlay.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
                if (event.key === "Escape") {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    clearManualCopyFallback(true);
                    return;
                }
                if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
                const focusable = [textarea, close];
                const currentIndex = focusable.indexOf(document.activeElement);
                const nextIndex = event.shiftKey
                    ? (currentIndex <= 0 ? focusable.length - 1 : currentIndex - 1)
                    : (currentIndex >= focusable.length - 1 ? 0 : currentIndex + 1);
                event.preventDefault();
                focusable[nextIndex].focus();
            });

            panel.appendChild(title);
            panel.appendChild(note);
            panel.appendChild(textarea);
            panel.appendChild(close);
            overlay.appendChild(panel);
            document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        }

        const note = document.getElementById("manualCopyMessage");
        const textarea = document.getElementById("manualCopyText");
        overlay._focusReturn = document.activeElement;
        if (note) note.textContent = message || "Clipboard access was blocked. Press Ctrl+C to copy the selected text.";
        if (textarea) textarea.value = String(text == null ? "" : text);

        overlay.hidden = false;
        overlay.style.display = "flex";
        if (textarea) {
            textarea.focus();
            textarea.select();
        }
        return false;
    }

    function copyText(text) {
        const copyValue = String(text == null ? "" : text);

        if (!copyValue.trim()) {
            return Promise.resolve(false);
        }

        const useCompatibilityFallback = function () {
            if (fallbackCopy(copyValue)) {
                clearManualCopyFallback(false);
                return true;
            }
            return showManualCopyFallback(copyValue, "Clipboard access was blocked. Press Ctrl+C to copy the selected text.");
        };

        if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
            return navigator.clipboard.writeText(copyValue)
                .then(() => {
                    clearManualCopyFallback(false);
                    return true;
                })
                .catch(() => useCompatibilityFallback());
        }

        return Promise.resolve(useCompatibilityFallback());
    }

    function forceCopy(text) {
        return copyText(text);
    }

    function showStatus(msg) {
        const s = document.getElementById('status');
        if (!s) return;
        s.innerText = String(msg == null ? "" : msg);
        s.style.opacity = "1";
        setTimeout(function () { s.style.opacity = "0"; }, 2000);
    }

    function trackerSelectionKeys(field) {
        if (!field) return null;
        if (field.id === "issue") return ["_focusNotesIssueSelectionStart", "_focusNotesIssueSelectionEnd"];
        if (field.id === "nextStep") return ["_focusNotesNextStepSelectionStart", "_focusNotesNextStepSelectionEnd"];
        return null;
    }

    function rememberTrackerSelection(field) {
        var keys = trackerSelectionKeys(field);
        if (!keys || typeof field.selectionStart !== "number" || typeof field.selectionEnd !== "number") return;
        field[keys[0]] = field.selectionStart;
        field[keys[1]] = field.selectionEnd;
    }

    function rememberIssueSelection(field) {
        rememberTrackerSelection(field);
    }

    function rememberNextStepSelection(field) {
        rememberTrackerSelection(field);
    }

    function focusTrackerWithoutScroll(field, caret) {
        if (!field) return;
        try {
            field.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        } catch (error) {
            field.focus();
        }
        if (typeof field.setSelectionRange === "function" && typeof caret === "number") {
            field.setSelectionRange(caret, caret);
        }
    }

    function applyTrackerPreset(select, fieldId) {
        var field = document.getElementById(fieldId);
        var keys = trackerSelectionKeys(field);
        if (!field || !select || !keys) return false;

        var preset = String(select.value || "").trim();
        if (!preset) return false;

        var current = String(field.value || "");
        var caret = 0;

        if (!current.trim()) {
            field.value = preset;
            caret = preset.length;
        } else {
            var start = Number.isInteger(field[keys[0]]) ? field[keys[0]] : current.length;
            start = Math.max(0, Math.min(start, current.length));

            var before = current.slice(0, start);
            var after = current.slice(start);
            var prefix = before && !/\n$/.test(before) ? "\n" : "";
            var suffix = after && !/^\n/.test(after) ? "\n" : "";

            field.value = before + prefix + preset + suffix + after;
            caret = before.length + prefix.length + preset.length;
        }

        select.value = "";
        field[keys[0]] = caret;
        field[keys[1]] = caret;
        focusTrackerWithoutScroll(field, caret);
        field.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
        return true;
    }

    function applyIssuePreset(select) {
        return applyTrackerPreset(select, "issue");
    }

    function applyNextStepPreset(select) {
        return applyTrackerPreset(select, "nextStep");
    }

    window.applyIssuePreset = applyIssuePreset;
    window.applyNextStepPreset = applyNextStepPreset;

    function copyNotes() {
        const name = formatCallerNameField(document.getElementById('callerName')).trim();
        const itCompany = formatITCompanyField(document.getElementById('itCompany')).trim();
        const acct = normalizeAccountNumberField(document.getElementById('accountNum')).trim();
        const ph = document.getElementById('callbackNum').value.trim();
        const em = document.getElementById('email').value.trim();
        const addressField = document.getElementById('address');
        const ad = formatAddressField(addressField).trim();
        const iss = document.getElementById('issue').value.trim();
        const act = document.getElementById('actions').value.trim();
        const next = document.getElementById('nextStep').value.trim();

        let notesArray = [];

        if (name) notesArray.push(`CALLER: ${name}`);
        if (itCompany) notesArray.push(`IT COMPANY: ${itCompany}`);
        if (acct) notesArray.push(`ACCT: ${acct}`);
        if (ph) notesArray.push(`PHONE: ${ph}`);
        if (em) notesArray.push(`EMAIL: ${em}`);
        if (ad) notesArray.push(`ADDRESS: ${ad}`);

        if (iss) {
            if (notesArray.length > 0) notesArray.push(`-------------------`);
            notesArray.push(`ISSUE: ${iss}`);
        }

        if (act) {
            if (notesArray.length > 0) notesArray.push(`-------------------`);
            notesArray.push(`ACTIONS: ${act}`);
        }

        if (next) {
            if (notesArray.length > 0) notesArray.push(`-------------------`);
            notesArray.push(`NEXT STEP: ${next}`);
        }

        if (notesArray.length === 0) return showStatus("⚠ No Notes to Copy");

        forceCopy(notesArray.join('\n')).then((copied) => showStatus(copied ? "✓ Notes Copied" : "⚠ Copy Manually"));
    }

    function quickCopy(txt, el) {
        if (!el || typeof el !== "object" || !el.setAttribute) {
            return Promise.resolve(false);
        }

        return forceCopy(txt).then((copied) => {
            if (!copied) return false;

            if (typeof el._focusNotesCopyOriginal !== "string") {
                el._focusNotesCopyOriginal = el.innerText;
                el._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria = el.getAttribute("aria-label") || "";
            }
            if (el._focusNotesCopyTimer) clearTimeout(el._focusNotesCopyTimer);

            el.innerText = el.hasAttribute("data-copy-feedback-full") ? "✓ COPIED" : "✓";
            el.setAttribute("aria-label", "Copied");
            el.style.background = "#ffffff";
            el.style.color = "#111619";

            el._focusNotesCopyTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                el.innerText = el._focusNotesCopyOriginal;
                if (el._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria) el.setAttribute("aria-label", el._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria);
                else el.removeAttribute("aria-label");
                el.style.background = "";
                el.style.color = "";
                el._focusNotesCopyTimer = 0;
                delete el._focusNotesCopyOriginal;
                delete el._focusNotesCopyOriginalAria;
            }, 1000);
            return true;
        });
    }


    function collapseExpandableSections() {
        document.querySelectorAll('details[open]').forEach(function (details) {
            details.open = false;
        });


        [["cerCommandBuilder", "cerBuilderBody", "cerBuilderToggle"],
         ["cesCommandBuilder", "cesBuilderBody", "cesBuilderToggle"],
         ["nidCommandBuilder", "nidBuilderBody", "nidBuilderToggle"]].forEach(function (group) {
            var panel = document.getElementById(group[0]);
            var body = document.getElementById(group[1]);
            var toggle = document.getElementById(group[2]);
            if (panel) panel.classList.remove("is-open");
            if (body) body.setAttribute("hidden", "");
            if (toggle) {
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                toggle.textContent = "Show";
            }
        });

        var selfTestCard = document.getElementById("selfTestCard");
        var selfTestBody = document.getElementById("selfTestBody");
        var selfTestToggle = document.getElementById("selfTestToggle");
        if (selfTestCard) selfTestCard.classList.remove("is-expanded");
        if (selfTestBody) selfTestBody.hidden = true;
        if (selfTestToggle) {
            selfTestToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            selfTestToggle.textContent = "Show";
        }

        clearManualCopyFallback(false);
    }

    var BUSINESS_ACRONYMS = Object.freeze({
        "at&t": "AT&T",
        aws: "AWS",
        cdw: "CDW",
        dsr: "DSR",
        ibm: "IBM",
        isp: "ISP",
        it: "IT",
        llc: "LLC",
        llp: "LLP",
        lp: "LP",
        msp: "MSP",
        ntt: "NTT",
        pc: "PC",
        plc: "PLC",
        us: "US",
        usa: "USA"
    });

    function normalizeBusinessPart(part) {
        var lower = String(part == null ? "" : part).toLocaleLowerCase();
        var acronym = BUSINESS_ACRONYMS[lower];
        if (acronym) return acronym;

        return lower.replace(/(^|['’])(\p{L})/gu, function (match, separator, letter, offset, source) {
            if (separator && source.slice(offset) === separator + "s") return separator + "s";
            return separator + letter.toLocaleUpperCase();
        });
    }

    function normalizeBusinessToken(token) {
        var text = String(token == null ? "" : token);
        if (!text) return "";

        var leadingMatch = text.match(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}&]+/u);
        var leading = leadingMatch ? leadingMatch[0] : "";
        var remainder = text.slice(leading.length);
        var trailingMatch = remainder.match(/[^\p{L}\p{N}&]+$/u);
        var trailing = trailingMatch ? trailingMatch[0] : "";
        var core = trailing ? remainder.slice(0, -trailing.length) : remainder;
        var acronym = BUSINESS_ACRONYMS[core.toLocaleLowerCase()];

        if (acronym) return leading + acronym + trailing;

        var titleCased = core.split("-").map(normalizeBusinessPart).join("-");
        return leading + titleCased + trailing;
    }

    function normalizeBusinessName(value, preserveSpacing) {
        var text = String(value == null ? "" : value);
        if (!preserveSpacing) text = text.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        if (!text) return "";
        return text.replace(/\S+/g, normalizeBusinessToken);
    }

    function formatITCompanyField(field, preserveSpacing) {
        if (!field) return "";

        var selectionStart = typeof field.selectionStart === "number" ? field.selectionStart : null;
        var selectionEnd = typeof field.selectionEnd === "number" ? field.selectionEnd : null;
        var selectionDirection = field.selectionDirection || "none";
        var normalized = normalizeBusinessName(field.value, !!preserveSpacing);

        if (field.value !== normalized) {
            field.value = normalized;
            if (selectionStart !== null && selectionEnd !== null && document.activeElement === field) {
                try {
                    field.setSelectionRange(selectionStart, selectionEnd, selectionDirection);
                } catch (error) {
                    // Some input environments do not expose selection ranges.
                }
            }
        }
        return normalized;
    }

    function normalizeCallerName(value) {
        var suffixes = {
            jr: "Jr.",
            sr: "Sr.",
            ii: "II",
            iii: "III",
            iv: "IV",
            v: "V"
        };
        var cleaned = String(value == null ? "" : value).trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
        if (!cleaned) return "";

        var tokens = cleaned.split(" ");
        return tokens.map(function (token, index) {
            var suffixKey = token.replace(/[.,]/g, "").toLowerCase();
            if (index === tokens.length - 1 && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(suffixes, suffixKey)) {
                return suffixes[suffixKey];
            }

            return token.toLocaleLowerCase().replace(/(^|[-'’])(\p{L})/gu, function (match, separator, letter) {
                return separator + letter.toLocaleUpperCase();
            });
        }).join(" ");
    }

    function formatCallerNameField(field) {
        if (!field) return "";
        var normalized = normalizeCallerName(field.value);
        if (field.value !== normalized) field.value = normalized;
        return normalized;
    }

    function normalizeAccountNumberField(field) {
        if (!field) return "";

        var selectionStart = typeof field.selectionStart === "number" ? field.selectionStart : null;
        var selectionEnd = typeof field.selectionEnd === "number" ? field.selectionEnd : null;
        var selectionDirection = field.selectionDirection || "none";
        var normalized = String(field.value == null ? "" : field.value).replace(/[a-z]/g, function (letter) {
            return letter.toUpperCase();
        });

        if (field.value !== normalized) {
            field.value = normalized;
            if (selectionStart !== null && selectionEnd !== null && document.activeElement === field) {
                try {
                    field.setSelectionRange(selectionStart, selectionEnd, selectionDirection);
                } catch (error) {
                    // Some input environments do not expose selection ranges.
                }
            }
        }

        return normalized;
    }

    function countAccountLettersAndNumbers(value) {
        var matches = String(value == null ? "" : value).match(/[A-Za-z0-9]/g);
        return matches ? matches.length : 0;
    }

    function updateAccountNumCount() {
        var field = document.getElementById("accountNum");
        var counter = document.getElementById("accountNumCount");
        if (!field || !counter) return 0;

        var count = countAccountLettersAndNumbers(field.value);
        counter.textContent = String(count);
        counter.setAttribute("aria-label", count + (count === 1 ? " letter or number entered" : " letters and numbers entered"));
        return count;
    }

    var AUTO_GROW_TEXTAREA_IDS = Object.freeze(["brainDump", "issue", "actions", "nextStep"]);
    var autoGrowResizeFrame = 0;
    var quickScratchScrollFrame = 0;
    var quickScratchScrollSettleFrame = 0;

    function numericStyleValue(style, property) {
        var value = parseFloat(style && style[property]);
        return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
    }

    function textareaBorderHeight(field) {
        var style = window.getComputedStyle(field);
        return numericStyleValue(style, "borderTopWidth") + numericStyleValue(style, "borderBottomWidth");
    }

    function updateQuickScratchFocusClearance() {
        var container = document.querySelector(".container");
        var sticky = document.querySelector(".sticky-wrapper");
        if (!container || !sticky) return 0;
        if (window.getComputedStyle(sticky).position !== "sticky") {
            container.style.scrollPaddingTop = "12px";
            return 12;
        }
        var clearance = Math.ceil(sticky.getBoundingClientRect().height + 24);
        container.style.scrollPaddingTop = clearance + "px";
        return clearance;
    }

    function quickScratchDefaultTextareaHeight() {
        var scratch = document.querySelector(".scratch-zone");
        var label = scratch ? scratch.querySelector(".zone-label") : null;
        var headingRow = scratch ? scratch.querySelector(".scratch-heading-row") : null;
        var field = document.getElementById("brainDump");
        var caller = document.getElementById("callerIdentitySection");
        if (!scratch || !label || !field || !caller) return 0;

        var callerHeight = caller.getBoundingClientRect().height;
        var scratchStyle = window.getComputedStyle(scratch);
        var heading = headingRow || label;
        var headingStyle = window.getComputedStyle(heading);
        var overhead =
            numericStyleValue(scratchStyle, "paddingTop") +
            numericStyleValue(scratchStyle, "paddingBottom") +
            numericStyleValue(scratchStyle, "borderTopWidth") +
            numericStyleValue(scratchStyle, "borderBottomWidth") +
            heading.getBoundingClientRect().height +
            numericStyleValue(headingStyle, "marginBottom");

        scratch.style.minHeight = callerHeight + "px";
        scratch.style.height = "auto";
        var fieldDefault = Math.max(44, callerHeight - overhead);
        field.dataset.focusNotesDefaultHeight = String(fieldDefault);
        field.style.minHeight = fieldDefault + "px";
        return fieldDefault;
    }

    function autoGrowDefaultHeight(field) {
        if (!field) return 0;
        if (field.id === "brainDump") {
            var scratchDefault = parseFloat(field.dataset.focusNotesDefaultHeight);
            if (Number.isFinite(scratchDefault) && scratchDefault > 0) return scratchDefault;
        }
        var style = window.getComputedStyle(field);
        var minHeight = parseFloat(style.minHeight);
        if (Number.isFinite(minHeight) && minHeight > 0) return minHeight;
        return field.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0;
    }

    function rememberQuickScratchScrollPosition(field) {
        if (!field || field.id !== "brainDump") return;
        if (typeof field._focusNotesScrollTop === "number" && Number.isFinite(field._focusNotesScrollTop)) return;
        var container = document.querySelector(".container");
        if (!container) return;
        field._focusNotesScrollTop = container.scrollTop;
    }

    function restoreQuickScratchScrollPosition(field) {
        if (!field || field.id !== "brainDump") return;
        var container = document.querySelector(".container");
        var targetScrollTop = Number(field._focusNotesScrollTop);
        if (!container || !Number.isFinite(targetScrollTop)) return;

        function restoreNow() {
            if (Math.abs(container.scrollTop - targetScrollTop) > 0.25) {
                container.scrollTop = targetScrollTop;
            }
        }

        restoreNow();
        if (quickScratchScrollFrame) window.cancelAnimationFrame(quickScratchScrollFrame);
        if (quickScratchScrollSettleFrame) window.cancelAnimationFrame(quickScratchScrollSettleFrame);
        quickScratchScrollFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            quickScratchScrollFrame = 0;
            restoreNow();
            quickScratchScrollSettleFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                quickScratchScrollSettleFrame = 0;
                restoreNow();
                clearQuickScratchScrollPosition(field);
            });
        });
    }

    function clearQuickScratchScrollPosition(field) {
        if (!field || field.id !== "brainDump") return;
        delete field._focusNotesScrollTop;
    }

    function resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, preserveFollowingPosition) {
        if (!field || AUTO_GROW_TEXTAREA_IDS.indexOf(field.id) === -1) return 0;

        var isScratch = field.id === "brainDump";
        var defaultHeight = autoGrowDefaultHeight(field);

        field.style.height = "0px";
        var desiredHeight = Math.max(defaultHeight, field.scrollHeight + textareaBorderHeight(field));
        field.style.height = desiredHeight + "px";

        if (isScratch) {
            updateQuickScratchFocusClearance();
            if (preserveFollowingPosition !== false) restoreQuickScratchScrollPosition(field);
        }
        return field.getBoundingClientRect().height;
    }

    function resizeAutoGrowFields(preserveFollowingPosition) {
        quickScratchDefaultTextareaHeight();
        AUTO_GROW_TEXTAREA_IDS.forEach(function (id) {
            resizeAutoGrowTextarea(document.getElementById(id), preserveFollowingPosition);
        });
        updateQuickScratchFocusClearance();
    }

    function initializeAutoGrowTextareas() {
        resizeAutoGrowFields(false);
        window.addEventListener("resize", function () {
            if (autoGrowResizeFrame) window.cancelAnimationFrame(autoGrowResizeFrame);
            autoGrowResizeFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                autoGrowResizeFrame = 0;
                resizeAutoGrowFields(true);
            });
        });
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_AUTO_GROW = Object.freeze({
        resize: resizeAutoGrowTextarea,
        resizeAll: resizeAutoGrowFields,
        syncScratchDefault: quickScratchDefaultTextareaHeight
    });

    function positionCallerUnderQuickScratch() {
        var scrollContainer = document.querySelector(".container");
        var stickyWrapper = document.querySelector(".sticky-wrapper");
        var callerSection = document.getElementById("callerIdentitySection");
        var callerName = document.getElementById("callerName");

        if (!scrollContainer || !stickyWrapper || !callerSection) return;

        var containerStyle = window.getComputedStyle(scrollContainer);
        var sectionGap = parseFloat(containerStyle.rowGap || containerStyle.gap) || 0;
        var stickyBottom = stickyWrapper.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
        var callerTop = callerSection.getBoundingClientRect().top;
        var targetTop = Math.max(
            0,
            scrollContainer.scrollTop + callerTop - (stickyBottom + sectionGap)
        );

        scrollContainer.scrollTo({ top: targetTop, behavior: "smooth" });

        if (callerName) {
            try {
                callerName.focus({ preventScroll: true });
            } catch (error) {
                callerName.focus();
                scrollContainer.scrollTo({ top: targetTop, behavior: "smooth" });
            }
        }
    }

    var TEMPORARY_FIELD_IDS = Object.freeze([
        "brainDump", "callerName", "itCompany", "accountNum",
        "callbackNum", "email", "address", "issue", "issuePreset", "actions", "nextStep", "nextStepPreset", "speedInput"
    ]);

    function clearTemporaryFieldValues(root) {
        var scope = root && typeof root.querySelector === "function" ? root : document;
        TEMPORARY_FIELD_IDS.forEach(function (id) {
            var field = scope.querySelector("#" + id);
            if (field) field.value = "";
        });
    }

    function resetForm() {
        if (!confirm("Clear temporary work fields and start new?")) return;

        clearTemporaryFieldValues(document);
        updateAccountNumCount();

        var result = document.getElementById("calcResult");
        if (result) {
            result.style.display = "none";
            result.innerHTML = "";
        }

        var interfaceProfile = document.getElementById("interfaceProfile");
        if (interfaceProfile) interfaceProfile.value = "auto";

        if (window.FOCUS_NOTES_CASE_MEMORY) window.FOCUS_NOTES_CASE_MEMORY.clear();
        if (window.resetNidCommandBuilder) window.resetNidCommandBuilder();
        if (window.resetCerCommandBuilder) window.resetCerCommandBuilder();
        if (window.resetCesCommandBuilder) window.resetCesCommandBuilder();
        if (window.collapseAllCommandBuilders) window.collapseAllCommandBuilders();

        resetRegionalSupportUi();
        collapseExpandableSections();
        resizeAutoGrowFields(false);
        showStatus("✓ Ready for New Caller");

        window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            window.requestAnimationFrame(positionCallerUnderQuickScratch);
        });
    }

    function calculatePolicer() {
        const speed = parseFloat(document.getElementById('speedInput').value);
        const profile = document.getElementById('interfaceProfile') ? document.getElementById('interfaceProfile').value : "auto";
        const resultBox = document.getElementById('calcResult');

        if (!speed || isNaN(speed) || speed <= 0) {
            resultBox.style.display = "none";
            return;
        }

        let cir = Math.round(speed * 1000000);
        let eir = 0;
        let ebs = 0;

        if (speed === 1000) {
            cir = 999616000;
            eir = 384000;
            ebs = 512;
        }

        const cbsInfo = getDefaultCbs(speed, profile);
        const cbsKb = cbsInfo.kb;
        const fiveMsKb = burstKbFromMs(speed, 5);
        const tenMsKb = burstKbFromMs(speed, 10);
        const profileWarning = getPolicerProfileWarning(speed, profile);

        resultBox.style.display = "block";
        resultBox.innerHTML = `
            ${profileWarning ? `<div class="calc-warning" role="alert">${profileWarning}</div>` : ""}
            <div class="calc-result-row">CIR <button type="button" class="calc-copy-value" aria-label="Copy CIR" data-copy="${plainNumber(cir)}">${plainNumber(cir)}</button></div>
            <div class="calc-result-row">CBS <button type="button" class="calc-copy-value" aria-label="Copy CBS" data-copy="${plainNumber(cbsKb)}">${plainNumber(cbsKb)}</button></div>
            <div class="calc-result-row">EIR <button type="button" class="calc-copy-value" aria-label="Copy EIR" data-copy="${plainNumber(eir)}">${plainNumber(eir)}</button></div>
            <div class="calc-result-row">EBS <button type="button" class="calc-copy-value" aria-label="Copy EBS" data-copy="${plainNumber(ebs)}">${plainNumber(ebs)}</button></div>
            <div class="calc-result-divider"></div>
            <div class="calc-result-row">5 ms <button type="button" class="calc-copy-value" aria-label="Copy 5 ms CBS" data-copy="${plainNumber(fiveMsKb)}">${plainNumber(fiveMsKb)}</button></div>
            <div class="calc-result-row">10 ms <button type="button" class="calc-copy-value" aria-label="Copy 10 ms CBS" data-copy="${plainNumber(tenMsKb)}">${plainNumber(tenMsKb)}</button></div>`;

        resultBox.querySelectorAll('.calc-copy-value').forEach(function (el) {
            el.addEventListener('click', function () { quickCopy(el.dataset.copy, el); });
        });
    }

    function initializePolicerCalculator() {
        var speedInput = document.getElementById("speedInput");
        var resultBox = document.getElementById("calcResult");
        if (speedInput && resultBox) calculatePolicer();
    }

    function initializePolicerCalculatorIndependently() {
        var start = function () { initializePolicerCalculator(); };
        if (document.getElementById("speedInput") && document.getElementById("calcResult")) {
            start();
            return;
        }
        if (document.readyState === "loading") {
            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", start, { once: true });
        } else {
            start();
        }
    }

    function getPolicerProfileWarning(speed, profile) {
        if (profile === "auto" && speed >= 10000) {
            return "Select the actual service / interface type for 10G+ service.";
        }
        if (profile === "lag40" && (speed < 10000 || speed > 40000)) {
            return "The entered speed and selected interface profile do not match.";
        }
        if (profile === "native100_40_60" && (speed < 40000 || speed > 60000)) {
            return "The entered speed and selected interface profile do not match.";
        }
        if (profile === "native100_70_90" && (speed < 70000 || speed > 90000)) {
            return "The entered speed and selected interface profile do not match.";
        }
        return "";
    }

    function getDefaultCbs(speed, profile) {
        if (profile === "lag40") {
            return { kb: 15360, label: "10G-30G LAG up to 40G", note: "Use when the service rides on a LAG/bundled 10G interface. Check device max before changing live config." };
        }

        if (profile === "native40100") {
            return { kb: 37500, label: "Native 40G / 100G Interface", note: "Use when the handoff/interface is truly native 40G or 100G. Check device max before changing live config." };
        }

        if (profile === "native100_40_60") {
            return { kb: 75000, label: "Native 100G: 40G-60G Service", note: "Use for native 100G interface cases in the 40G-60G service range. Check device max before changing live config." };
        }

        if (profile === "native100_70_90") {
            return { kb: 96256, label: "Native 100G: 70G-90G Service", note: "Use for native 100G interface cases in the 70G-90G service range. Check device max before changing live config." };
        }

        if (speed <= 200) {
            return { kb: 512, label: "Standard Service", note: "Spectrum updated default CBS from Burst/Y.1564 guidance. This is the normal choice unless the circuit is a big 10G/40G/100G interface case." };
        }

        if (speed <= 1000) {
            return { kb: 1024, label: "Standard Service", note: "Spectrum updated default CBS from Burst/Y.1564 guidance. This is the normal choice unless the circuit is a big 10G/40G/100G interface case." };
        }

        if (speed < 10000) {
            return { kb: 2048, label: "Standard Service", note: "Spectrum updated default CBS for 1001+ Mbps standard service. For 10G+ service, choose the matching Service / Interface Type if known." };
        }

        return { kb: 15360, label: "10G+ needs interface type", note: "10G+ circuits depend on the service/interface type. Choose LAG/native profile if known and check device max." };
    }

    function plainNumber(value) {
        return String(Math.round(Number(value) || 0));
    }

    function burstKbFromMs(speedMbps, ms) {
        // Spectrum formula: CIR bps x burst seconds / 8 = CBS bytes.
        // Output is decimal KB because the Spectrum reference shows 250000 bytes as 250 KB.
        const bytes = (speedMbps * 1000000) * (ms / 1000) / 8;
        return Math.round(bytes / 1000);
    }

    initializePolicerCalculatorIndependently();

</script>


<script>
(function () {
    "use strict";

    var caseValueMemory = Object.create(null);

    function clearCaseValueMemory() {
        Object.keys(caseValueMemory).forEach(function (scopeKey) {
            delete caseValueMemory[scopeKey];
        });
    }

    function snapshotCaseValueMemory() {
        var snapshot = {};
        Object.keys(caseValueMemory).forEach(function (scopeKey) {
            snapshot[scopeKey] = Object.assign({}, caseValueMemory[scopeKey]);
        });
        return snapshot;
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_CASE_MEMORY = Object.freeze({
        clear: clearCaseValueMemory,
        snapshot: snapshotCaseValueMemory
    });

    function field(key, label, placeholder) {
        return { key: key, label: label || labelFromKey(key), placeholder: placeholder || "" };
    }

    var READ_ONLY_COMMAND_META = Object.freeze({ readOnly: true });

    function task(name, cmd, fields, description, meta) {
        if (description && typeof description === "object" && !meta) {
            meta = description;
            description = "";
        }
        return {
            name: name,
            cmd: cmd,
            fields: fields || [],
            description: description || "",
            meta: meta || READ_ONLY_COMMAND_META
        };
    }

    function labelFromKey(key) {
        return String(key || "Value")
            .toLowerCase()
            .split("_")
            .map(function (part) { return part.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + part.slice(1); })
            .join(" ");
    }

    function extractFieldsFromCommand(cmd, definedFields) {
        var fieldsByKey = {};
        (definedFields || []).forEach(function (item) { fieldsByKey[item.key] = item; });

        var seen = {};
        var result = [];
        var match;
        var tokenPattern = /\{([A-Z0-9_]+)\}/g;

        while ((match = tokenPattern.exec(cmd || "")) !== null) {
            var key = match[1];
            if (seen[key]) continue;
            seen[key] = true;
            result.push(fieldsByKey[key] || field(key, labelFromKey(key), "Enter " + labelFromKey(key)));
        }

        return result;
    }

    function taskRank(item) {
        var name = item.name || "";
        if (name.indexOf("[SYSTEM]") === 0) return 1;
        if (name.indexOf("[FIND CIRCUIT / VLAN]") === 0) return 2;
        if (name.indexOf("[INTERFACE]") === 0) return 3;
        if (name.indexOf("[L1]") === 0) return 10;
        if (name.indexOf("[L1/L2]") === 0) return 15;
        if (name.indexOf("[L2]") === 0) return 20;
        if (name.indexOf("[L3") === 0) return 30;
        if (name.indexOf("[QoS]") === 0) return 40;
        if (name.indexOf("[SYS]") === 0) return 50;
        return 60;
    }

    function sortTasks(list) {
        return list.map(function (item, index) {
            item._originalOrder = index;
            item.fields = extractFieldsFromCommand(item.cmd, item.fields);
            if (!item.meta) item.meta = READ_ONLY_COMMAND_META;
            return item;
        }).sort(function (a, b) {
            return taskRank(a) - taskRank(b) || a._originalOrder - b._originalOrder;
        }).map(function (item) {
            delete item._originalOrder;return item;
        });
    }

    function applyMeta(list, meta) {
        return list.map(function (item) {
            item.meta = meta;
            return item;
        });
    }

    }

    Object.keys(COMMAND_DB).forEach(function (builderType) {
        Object.keys(COMMAND_DB[builderType].devices).forEach(function (deviceName) {
            var tasks = COMMAND_DB[builderType].devices[deviceName].tasks || [];
            COMMAND_DB[builderType].devices[deviceName].tasks = tasks.map(function (item) {
                item.fields = extractFieldsFromCommand(item.cmd, item.fields || []);
                if (!item.meta) item.meta = READ_ONLY_COMMAND_META;
                return item;
            });
        });
    });

    window.COMMAND_DB = COMMAND_DB;

    function option(value, label) {
        var opt = document.createElement("option");
        opt.value = value;
        opt.textContent = label || value;
        return opt;
    }

    function splitCommandSequence(commandText) {
        return String(commandText || "")
            .split(/\r?\n/)
            .map(function (line) { return line.trim(); })
            .filter(Boolean);
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_COMMAND_SEQUENCE = Object.freeze({
        split: splitCommandSequence
    });

    var JUNOS_OUTPUT_FILTERS = Object.freeze({
        match: Object.freeze({ operator: "match", input: "regex", placeholder: "Text or regex" }),
        except: Object.freeze({ operator: "except", input: "regex", placeholder: "Text or regex" }),
        find: Object.freeze({ operator: "find", input: "regex", placeholder: "Text or regex" }),
        count: Object.freeze({ operator: "count", input: "none" }),
        last: Object.freeze({ operator: "last", input: "positive", placeholder: "Lines", min: 1 }),
        "no-more": Object.freeze({ operator: "no-more", input: "none" }),
        trim: Object.freeze({ operator: "trim", input: "nonnegative", placeholder: "Columns", min: 0 }),
        refresh: Object.freeze({ operator: "refresh", input: "range", placeholder: "Seconds", min: 1, max: 604800 }),
        hold: Object.freeze({ operator: "hold", input: "none" })
    });

    var JUNOS_INTERFACE_DETAILS = Object.freeze(["terse", "brief", "detail", "extensive", "media", "statistics"]);
    var CISCO_OUTPUT_FILTERS = Object.freeze({
        include: Object.freeze({ operator: "include", input: "regex", placeholder: "Case-sensitive text or regex" }),
        exclude: Object.freeze({ operator: "exclude", input: "regex", placeholder: "Case-sensitive text or regex" }),
