    });

    var CISCO_FILTER_DEVICES = Object.freeze({
        "ASR920": true,
        "ME3400": true,
        "ME3600": true
    });


    function isJunosDevice(deviceName) {
        return deviceName === "Juniper EX" || deviceName === "Juniper" || deviceName === "Juniper MX" || deviceName === "Juniper QFX" || deviceName === "Juniper ACX";
    }

    function isJunosOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, commandText) {
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        return isJunosDevice(deviceName) && command.indexOf("\n") === -1 && /^show(?:\s|$)/i.test(command) && command.indexOf("|") === -1;
    }

    function parseJunosInterfaceCommand(commandText) {
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        if (command.indexOf("\n") !== -1 || command.indexOf("|") !== -1) return null;
        var tokens = command.split(/\s+/);
        if (tokens.length < 2 || tokens[0].toLowerCase() !== "show" || tokens[1].toLowerCase() !== "interfaces") return null;
        var rest = tokens.slice(2);
        if (rest.length > 2) return null;

        var subject = "";
        var detail = "";
        if (rest.length === 1) {
            if (JUNOS_INTERFACE_DETAILS.indexOf(rest[0].toLowerCase()) !== -1) detail = rest[0].toLowerCase();
            else subject = rest[0];
        } else if (rest.length === 2) {
            subject = rest[0];
            detail = rest[1].toLowerCase();
            if (JUNOS_INTERFACE_DETAILS.indexOf(detail) === -1) return null;
        }

        if (/^(?:descriptions|diagnostics|queue)$/i.test(subject)) return null;
        return { subject: subject, detail: detail };
    }

    function isJunosInterfaceDetailEligible(deviceName, commandText) {
        return isJunosDevice(deviceName) && !!parseJunosInterfaceCommand(commandText);
    }

    function buildJunosInterfaceDetailCommand(baseCommand, detailId) {
        var base = String(baseCommand || "").trim();
        if (!detailId) return { ok: true, command: base, message: "" };
        if (JUNOS_INTERFACE_DETAILS.indexOf(detailId) === -1) return { ok: false, command: base, message: "Unsupported interface detail." };
        var parsed = parseJunosInterfaceCommand(base);
        if (!parsed) return { ok: false, command: base, message: "Output Detail is not available for this command." };
        return {
            ok: true,
            command: "show interfaces" + (parsed.subject ? " " + parsed.subject : "") + " " + detailId,
            message: ""
        };
    }

    function isJunosOperationalCommand(deviceName, commandText) {
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        if (!isJunosDevice(deviceName) || command.indexOf("\n") !== -1) return false;
        return /^(?:run\s+)?(?:show|monitor|ping|traceroute|file\s+show|help)(?:\s|$)/i.test(command);
    }

    function validateJunosFilterValue(filterId, rawValue) {
        var value = String(rawValue == null ? "" : rawValue).trim();
        var spec = JUNOS_OUTPUT_FILTERS[filterId];
        if (!spec || spec.input === "none") return { ok: true, value: "", message: "" };

        if (spec.input === "positive") {
            if (!/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(value)) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Enter a positive number of lines." };
            return { ok: true, value: String(parseInt(value, 10)), message: "" };
        }

        if (spec.input === "nonnegative") {
            if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Enter zero or a positive column number." };
            return { ok: true, value: String(parseInt(value, 10)), message: "" };
        }

        if (spec.input === "range") {
            if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Enter refresh seconds from 1 to 604800." };
            var seconds = parseInt(value, 10);
            if (seconds < spec.min || seconds > spec.max) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Enter refresh seconds from 1 to 604800." };
            return { ok: true, value: String(seconds), message: "" };
        }

        if (!value) return { ok: false, value: "", message: "Enter text or a POSIX regular expression." };
        if (value.length > 160) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Keep the filter to 160 characters or fewer." };
        if (/[\r\n";`]/.test(value)) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Remove quotes, semicolons, backticks, or line breaks." };
        return { ok: true, value: value, message: "" };
    }

    function buildJunosFilteredCommand(baseCommand, filterId, rawValue) {
        var base = String(baseCommand || "").trim();
        if (!filterId) return { ok: true, command: base, message: "" };
        var spec = JUNOS_OUTPUT_FILTERS[filterId];
        if (!spec) return { ok: false, command: base, message: "Unsupported filter." };
        var checked = validateJunosFilterValue(filterId, rawValue);
        if (!checked.ok) return { ok: false, command: base, message: checked.message };
        if (spec.input === "none") return { ok: true, command: base + " | " + spec.operator, message: "" };
        if (spec.input === "positive" || spec.input === "nonnegative" || spec.input === "range") {
            return { ok: true, command: base + " | " + spec.operator + " " + checked.value, message: "" };
        }
        return { ok: true, command: base + " | " + spec.operator + " \"" + checked.value + "\"", message: "" };
    }


    function isCiscoDevice(deviceName) {
        return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(CISCO_FILTER_DEVICES, deviceName);
    }

    function isCiscoOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, commandText) {
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        return isCiscoDevice(deviceName) && command.indexOf("\n") === -1 && /^show(?:\s|$)/i.test(command) && command.indexOf("|") === -1;
    }

    function isCiscoOperationalCommand(deviceName, commandText) {
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        return isCiscoDevice(deviceName) && command.indexOf("\n") === -1 && /^show(?:\s|$)/i.test(command);
    }

    function validateCiscoFilterValue(filterId, rawValue) {
        var value = String(rawValue == null ? "" : rawValue).trim();
        var spec = CISCO_OUTPUT_FILTERS[filterId];
        if (!spec) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Unsupported Cisco filter." };
        if (!value) return { ok: false, value: "", message: "Enter case-sensitive text or a Cisco regular expression." };
        if (value.length > 160) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Keep the filter to 160 characters or fewer." };
        if (/[\r\n;"'`]/.test(value)) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Remove quotes, semicolons, backticks, or line breaks." };
        if (/^\||\|$|\|\s+/.test(value)) return { ok: false, value: value, message: "Use | only inside a compact regex such as up|down; chained pipe commands are blocked." };
        return { ok: true, value: value, message: "" };
    }

    function buildCiscoFilteredCommand(baseCommand, filterId, rawValue) {
        var base = String(baseCommand || "").trim();
        if (!filterId) return { ok: true, command: base, message: "" };
        var spec = CISCO_OUTPUT_FILTERS[filterId];
        if (!spec) return { ok: false, command: base, message: "Unsupported Cisco filter." };
        var checked = validateCiscoFilterValue(filterId, rawValue);
        if (!checked.ok) return { ok: false, command: base, message: checked.message };
        return { ok: true, command: base + " | " + spec.operator + " " + checked.value, message: "" };
    }

    function getOutputFilterFamily(deviceName) {
        if (isJunosDevice(deviceName)) return "junos";
        if (isCiscoDevice(deviceName)) return "cisco";
        return "";
    }

    function getOutputFilterSpecs(deviceName) {
        var family = getOutputFilterFamily(deviceName);
        if (family === "junos") return JUNOS_OUTPUT_FILTERS;
        if (family === "cisco") return CISCO_OUTPUT_FILTERS;
        return null;
    }

    function getOutputFilterSpec(deviceName, filterId) {
        var specs = getOutputFilterSpecs(deviceName);
        return specs && specs[filterId] ? specs[filterId] : null;
    }

    function populateOutputFilterOptions(select, deviceName) {
        if (!select) return;
        var family = getOutputFilterFamily(deviceName);
        if (select.dataset.filterFamily === family && select.options.length) return;

        select.innerHTML = "";
        select.appendChild(option("", "No filter"));
        var specs = getOutputFilterSpecs(deviceName);
        if (specs) {
            Object.keys(specs).forEach(function (id) {
                select.appendChild(option(id, "| " + specs[id].operator));
            });
        }
        select.dataset.filterFamily = family;
        select.value = "";
        select.setAttribute("aria-label", (family === "cisco" ? "Cisco" : family === "junos" ? "Junos" : "Device") + " output filter");
    }

    function isDeviceOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, commandText) {
        if (isJunosDevice(deviceName)) return isJunosOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, commandText);
        if (isCiscoDevice(deviceName)) return isCiscoOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, commandText);
        return false;
    }

    function validateDeviceOutputFilterValue(deviceName, filterId, rawValue) {
        if (isJunosDevice(deviceName)) return validateJunosFilterValue(filterId, rawValue);
        if (isCiscoDevice(deviceName)) return validateCiscoFilterValue(filterId, rawValue);
        return { ok: false, value: String(rawValue || ""), message: "Output filters are not available for this device." };
    }

    function buildDeviceOutputFilteredCommand(deviceName, baseCommand, filterId, rawValue) {
        if (isJunosDevice(deviceName)) return buildJunosFilteredCommand(baseCommand, filterId, rawValue);
        if (isCiscoDevice(deviceName)) return buildCiscoFilteredCommand(baseCommand, filterId, rawValue);
        return { ok: false, command: String(baseCommand || "").trim(), message: "Output filters are not available for this device." };
    }

    var CER_BLOCKED_COMMAND_PATTERN = /^(?:configure|edit|set|delete|clear|request|restart|start|test|commit|rollback|protect|unprotect|load|save|insert|rename|activate|deactivate|replace|copy)(?:\s|$)/i;
    var CER_ALLOWED_PIPE_PATTERN = /^(?:match|except|find|count|last|no-more|trim|refresh|hold|include|exclude|begin|section)(?:\s|$)/i;
    var CER_DISALLOWED_SEQUENCE_PATTERN = /(?:;|&&|\|\||`|>|<|\$\(|\$\{|\t)/;
    var CER_CONTROL_CHARACTER_PATTERN = /[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/;

    function isReadOnlyCommandText(commandText) {
        var rawText = String(commandText || "");
        if (CER_DISALLOWED_SEQUENCE_PATTERN.test(rawText) || CER_CONTROL_CHARACTER_PATTERN.test(rawText)) return false;

        var lines = rawText.split(/\r?\n/).map(function (line) { return line.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
        if (!lines.length) return false;
        return lines.every(function (line) {
            var command = line.replace(/^run\s+/i, "");
            if (CER_BLOCKED_COMMAND_PATTERN.test(command)) return false;

            var pipeParts = command.split("|");
            var baseCommand = pipeParts.shift().trim();
            if (!/^(?:show|display|monitor|help|ping|traceroute|file\s+show)(?:\s|$)/i.test(baseCommand)) return false;
            return pipeParts.every(function (pipePart) {
                return CER_ALLOWED_PIPE_PATTERN.test(pipePart.trim());
            });
        });
    }

    function isCerReadOnlyTask(item) {
        var properties = normalizeCommandProperties(item && item.meta);
        return !!item && properties.readOnly && isReadOnlyCommandText(item.cmd);
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_JUNOS_FILTERS = Object.freeze({
        operators: Object.freeze(Object.keys(JUNOS_OUTPUT_FILTERS)),
        isEligible: isJunosOutputFilterEligible,
        build: buildJunosFilteredCommand,
        validate: validateJunosFilterValue
    });

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_JUNOS_DETAILS = Object.freeze({
        options: JUNOS_INTERFACE_DETAILS,
        isEligible: isJunosInterfaceDetailEligible,
        build: buildJunosInterfaceDetailCommand,
        isOperational: isJunosOperationalCommand
    });

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_CISCO_FILTERS = Object.freeze({
        operators: Object.freeze(Object.keys(CISCO_OUTPUT_FILTERS)),
        isEligible: isCiscoOutputFilterEligible,
        isOperational: isCiscoOperationalCommand,
        build: buildCiscoFilteredCommand,
        validate: validateCiscoFilterValue,
        devices: CISCO_FILTER_DEVICES
    });


    window.FOCUS_NOTES_COMMAND_POLICY = Object.freeze({
        isReadOnlyCommandText: isReadOnlyCommandText,
        isCerReadOnlyTask: isCerReadOnlyTask
    });

    /**
     * Sanitizes input to ensure it is safe for terminal pasting.
     * Trims surrounding whitespace and strips pasted line breaks.
     */
    function sanitizeInput(input) {
        if (typeof input !== "string") return "";
        return input.trim().replace(/[\r\n]+/g, "");
    }

    var TERMINAL_UNSAFE_PATTERN = /[\r\n\t|;&<>"'`\\]/;
    var SAFE_VALUE_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9._:\/-]+$/;

    function validateTerminalValue(key, rawValue) {
        var value = sanitizeInput(rawValue);
        var normalizedKey = String(key || "").toUpperCase();

        if (!value) return { ok: false, empty: true, value: "", message: "Enter a value." };
        if (TERMINAL_UNSAFE_PATTERN.test(value)) {
            return { ok: false, empty: false, value: value, message: "Remove terminal control characters." };
        }

        if (normalizedKey === "VLAN") {
            if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) {
                return { ok: false, empty: false, value: value, message: "VLAN must be a number from 1 to 4094." };
            }
            var vlan = parseInt(value, 10);
            if (vlan < 1 || vlan > 4094) {
                return { ok: false, empty: false, value: value, message: "VLAN must be from 1 to 4094." };
            }
            return { ok: true, empty: false, value: String(vlan), message: "" };
        }

        if (!SAFE_VALUE_PATTERN.test(value)) {
            return { ok: false, empty: false, value: value, message: "Use letters, numbers, . _ : / or -." };
        }

        return { ok: true, empty: false, value: value, message: "" };
    }

    window.validateTerminalValue = validateTerminalValue;

    function needsValueMessage(text) {
        return /^Enter .+ above$/i.test((text || "").trim());
    }

    function copyCommandText(text, statusElement, flashMissing) {
        var clean = (text || "").trim();
        if (!clean || /<[^>]+>/.test(clean) || needsValueMessage(clean)) {
            if (typeof flashMissing === "function") flashMissing();
            if (statusElement) {
                statusElement.style.color = "var(--alert)";
                statusElement.textContent = "Fill in value first";
                setTimeout(function () {
                    statusElement.textContent = "";
                    statusElement.style.color = "";
                }, 1800);
            }
            return;
        }

        copyText(clean).then(function (success) {
            if (!success || !statusElement) return;
            statusElement.style.color = "";
            statusElement.textContent = "✓ Command copied";
            setTimeout(function () { statusElement.textContent = ""; }, 1600);
        });
    }

    function createInputField(builderLabel, inputFields, itemField, renderCommand, rememberedValue, rememberValue) {
        var wrap = document.createElement("div");
        wrap.className = "builder-field full";

        var label = document.createElement("label");
        label.textContent = itemField.label;

        var input = document.createElement("input");
        input.className = "nid-input";
        input.dataset.key = itemField.key;
        input.placeholder = itemField.placeholder;
        input.autocomplete = "off";
        input.setAttribute("aria-label", builderLabel + " " + itemField.label);
        input.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "false");
        input.value = rememberedValue || "";

        var validation = document.createElement("div");
        validation.className = "input-validation";
        validation.setAttribute("aria-live", "polite");

        input.addEventListener("input", function () {
            var safeValue = sanitizeInput(input.value);
            if (input.value !== safeValue) input.value = safeValue;
            if (typeof rememberValue === "function") rememberValue(itemField.key, input.value);
            var check = validateTerminalValue(itemField.key, input.value);
            var showError = !check.ok && !check.empty;
            input.classList.toggle("invalid-value", showError);
            input.setAttribute("aria-invalid", showError ? "true" : "false");
            validation.textContent = showError ? check.message : "";
            input.classList.remove("input-flash");
            renderCommand();
        });

        wrap.appendChild(label);
        wrap.appendChild(input);
        wrap.appendChild(validation);
        inputFields.appendChild(wrap);
    }

    function applyInputsToCommand(cmd, inputFields) {
        var missing = [];
        var invalid = [];
        var inputs = inputFields.querySelectorAll(".nid-input");

        inputs.forEach(function (input) {
            var check = validateTerminalValue(input.dataset.key, input.value);
            if (input.value !== check.value) input.value = check.value;
            var fieldWrap = input.closest(".builder-field");
            var fieldLabel = fieldWrap && fieldWrap.querySelector("label");
            var validation = fieldWrap && fieldWrap.querySelector(".input-validation");
            var label = fieldLabel ? fieldLabel.textContent.trim() : input.dataset.key;

            if (check.empty) {
                missing.push(label);
                input.classList.remove("invalid-value");
                input.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "false");
                if (validation) validation.textContent = "";
                return;
            }

            if (!check.ok) {
                invalid.push({ label: label, message: check.message });
                input.classList.add("invalid-value");
                input.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
                if (validation) validation.textContent = check.message;
                return;
            }

            input.classList.remove("invalid-value");
            input.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "false");
            if (validation) validation.textContent = "";
            cmd = cmd.split("{" + input.dataset.key + "}").join(check.value);
        });

        return { cmd: cmd, missing: missing, invalid: invalid };
    }

    function normalizeCommandProperties(properties) {
        properties = properties || {};
        return {
            readOnly: properties.readOnly !== false,
            cliMode: properties.cliMode || "unknown",
            parentContext: properties.parentContext || ""
        };
    }

    function inferCliMode(deviceName, commandText, rawProperties) {
        var properties = normalizeCommandProperties(rawProperties);
        var explicitMode = String(properties.cliMode || "unknown");
        if (explicitMode !== "unknown") return explicitMode;

        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        var firstLine = command.split(/\r?\n/)[0].trim();
        if (!firstLine) return "unknown";if (isJunosDevice(deviceName)) {
            if (/^(?:edit|set|delete|commit|rollback|activate|deactivate|insert|rename|replace|protect|unprotect|load|save)(?:\s|$)/i.test(firstLine)) {
                return "configuration";
            }
            if (isJunosOperationalCommand(deviceName, firstLine) || /^(?:configure|request)(?:\s|$)/i.test(firstLine)) {
                return "operational";
            }
        }

        if (isCiscoDevice(deviceName)) {
            if (/^show(?:\s|$)/i.test(firstLine) || /^configure\s+terminal(?:\s|$)/i.test(firstLine)) {
                return "privileged-exec";
            }
            if (/^interface(?:\s|$)/i.test(firstLine)) return "interface-config";
            if (/^service\s+instance(?:\s|$)/i.test(firstLine)) return "service-instance-config";
        }

        return "unknown";
    }

    function cliModeHint(deviceName, commandText, mode, parentContext) {
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        var isSequence = command.indexOf("\n") !== -1;
        if (mode === "operational") {
            if (isJunosDevice(deviceName)) {
                return isSequence
                    ? "Start from operational mode (>); run the sequence line by line."
                    : "Run from operational mode (>). From configuration mode, add run.";
            }
            return "Run from the operational prompt.";
        }
        if (mode === "configuration") return "Run from Junos configuration mode (#).";
        if (mode === "user-exec") return "Run from user EXEC mode (>).";
        if (mode === "privileged-exec") {
            return isSequence
                ? "Start from privileged EXEC mode (#); run the sequence line by line."
                : "Run from privileged EXEC mode (#).";
        }
        if (mode === "global-config") return "Run from global configuration mode (config)#.";
        if (mode === "interface-config") return "Run from interface configuration mode (config-if)#.";
        if (mode === "service-instance-config") return "Run from service-instance configuration mode (config-if-srv)#.";
        if (mode === "other" && parentContext) return "Run from " + parentContext + ".";
        return "";
    }

    function inferCommandIntelligence(deviceName, commandText, rawProperties) {
        var properties = normalizeCommandProperties(rawProperties);
        var command = String(commandText || "").trim();
        var mode = inferCliMode(deviceName, command, properties);
        var readOnlyByText = isReadOnlyCommandText(command);
        return {
            family: isJunosDevice(deviceName) ? "junos" : (isCiscoDevice(deviceName) ? "cisco" : "other"),
            mode: mode,
            readOnly: properties.readOnly && readOnlyByText,
            stateChanging: properties.readOnly === false || !readOnlyByText,
            filterEligible: isDeviceOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, command),
            hint: cliModeHint(deviceName, command, mode, properties.parentContext)
        };
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_CLI_INTELLIGENCE = Object.freeze({
        infer: inferCommandIntelligence,
        inferMode: inferCliMode,
        modeHint: cliModeHint
    });

    function cleanTaskName(name) {
        return String(name || "").replace(/^\[[^\]]+\]\s*/, "").trim();
    }

        "Route Table": "Check Routes",
        "Running Config": "Check Configuration",
        "Service Instances": "Check Service Instances",
        "Show Ports": "View All Ports",
        "Show VLAN / Attached Ports": "Find VLAN Ports",
        "Spanning Tree Bridge": "Check Spanning Tree Root",
        "Spanning Tree Interfaces": "Check Spanning Tree Ports",
        "Spanning Tree Summary": "Check Spanning Tree",
        "Spanning Tree by VLAN": "Check VLAN Spanning Tree",
        "Speed / Duplex": "Check Speed & Duplex",
        "Status & Description": "Check Status & Description",
        "System Info": "Check System Info",
        "System Resource Overview": "Check System Resources",
        "Transceiver Detail": "Check Optics",
        "Transceiver IDPROM": "Check Optics Details",
        "Transceiver Parser Help": "Find Optics Command",
        "Transceiver Status": "Check Optics Status",
        "Transmit Stats": "Check Transmit Traffic",
        "UDLD Status": "Check UDLD",
        "VLAN Summary": "View VLAN Summary",
        "VLANs": "View VLANs",
        "VPLS Connections": "Check VPLS Connection",
        "VPLS MAC Table": "Find VPLS MACs",
        "Port Traffic + Errors": "Check Port Traffic & Errors",
        "LAG Status": "Check LAG Status",
        "LAG Traffic": "Check LAG Traffic",
        "EFM OAM Status": "Check EFM OAM",
        "EFM OAM Counters": "Check EFM OAM Counters",
        "What's Bound to Port": "Find Port Services",
        "L2CP Counters": "Check L2CP Counters",
        "Service List": "View Services",
        "Service Summary": "Check Service Summary",
        "Service Details": "Check Service Details",
        "What's Bound to LAG": "Find LAG Services",
        "LACP Member Status": "Check LACP Member",
        "LACP Member Counters": "Check LACP Member Counters",
        "VLANs on Bridge": "Find Bridge VLANs"
});

    function simpleTaskName(name) {
        var cleaned = cleanTaskName(name);
        return SIMPLE_TASK_LABELS[cleaned] || cleaned;
    }

    function basicTaskDescription(item) {
        var name = cleanTaskName(item && item.name);
        if (!name) return "";
        if (/^(?:check|view|show|find|locate|identify|test|clear|reset|restart|set|configure|change|enable|disable|save|commit|monitor|display|run)\b/i.test(name)) {
            return name.replace(/[.!?]+$/, "") + ".";
        }
        return "Check " + name + ".";
    }

    function taskDescription(item) {
        var explicit = String(item && item.description || "").trim();
        return explicit || basicTaskDescription(item);
    }

    function renderCommandNote(container, item, validationMessage) {
        if (!container) return;
        container.innerHTML = "";

        if (validationMessage) {
            var validation = document.createElement("div");
            validation.className = "command-validation-message";
            validation.textContent = validationMessage;
            container.appendChild(validation);
        } else {
            var description = document.createElement("div");
            description.className = "task-description";

            var descriptionLabel = document.createElement("span");
            descriptionLabel.className = "task-description-label";
            descriptionLabel.textContent = "What it checks:";

            var descriptionText = document.createElement("span");
            descriptionText.className = "task-description-text";
            descriptionText.textContent = taskDescription(item);

            description.appendChild(descriptionLabel);
            description.appendChild(descriptionText);
            container.appendChild(description);
        }

        var properties = normalizeCommandProperties(item && item.meta);
        if (!properties.readOnly) {
            var caution = document.createElement("div");
            caution.className = "command-caution";
            caution.textContent = "Caution: This command can change the device.";
            container.appendChild(caution);
        }

    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_SIMPLIFIED_BUILDER = Object.freeze({
        cleanTaskName: cleanTaskName,
        simpleTaskName: simpleTaskName,
        labels: SIMPLE_TASK_LABELS,
        basicTaskDescription: basicTaskDescription,
        taskDescription: taskDescription,
        render: renderCommandNote
    });

    function commandTaskGroupLabel(name) {
        name = String(name || "");
        if (name.indexOf("[SYSTEM]") === 0) return "SYSTEM";
        if (name.indexOf("[FIND CIRCUIT / VLAN]") === 0) return "FIND CIRCUIT / VLAN";
        if (name.indexOf("[INTERFACE]") === 0) return "INTERFACE";
        if (name.indexOf("[L1/L2]") === 0) return "Layer 1–2 · OAM";
        if (name.indexOf("[L1]") === 0) return "Layer 1 · Physical";
        if (name.indexOf("[L2]") === 0) return "Layer 2 · Services";
        if (name.indexOf("[QoS]") === 0) return "QoS · Traffic";
        if (name.indexOf("[L3 - Limited]") === 0) return "Layer 3 · Limited";
        if (name.indexOf("[SYS]") === 0) return "System";
        return "Other / Custom";
    }

    function populateGroupedTaskSelect(select, tasks) {
        if (!select) return;
        select.innerHTML = "";
        var groupMap = {};
        var groupOrder = [];

        tasks.forEach(function (item, index) {
            var label = item.preserveLabel ? String(item.group || "") : commandTaskGroupLabel(item.name);
            if (!groupMap[label]) {
                var group = document.createElement("optgroup");
                group.label = label;
                groupMap[label] = group;
                groupOrder.push(label);
            }
            var optionLabel = item.preserveLabel ? String(item.name || item.group || "") : simpleTaskName(item.name);
            groupMap[label].appendChild(option(String(index), optionLabel));
        });

        groupOrder.forEach(function (label) {
            select.appendChild(groupMap[label]);
        });
    }

    function setupCommandBuilder(config) {
        var dataRoot = COMMAND_DB[config.type];
        if (!dataRoot) return;

        var deviceSelect = config.deviceSelectId ? document.getElementById(config.deviceSelectId) : null;
        var taskSelect = document.getElementById(config.taskSelectId);
        var inputFields = document.getElementById(config.inputFieldsId);
        var deviceOutput = document.getElementById(config.deviceOutputId);
        var deviceWrap = document.getElementById(config.deviceWrapId);
        var deviceCopyStatus = document.getElementById(config.deviceCopyStatusId);
        var output = document.getElementById(config.outputId);
        var copyStatus = document.getElementById(config.copyStatusId);
        var outputControls = config.outputControlsId ? document.getElementById(config.outputControlsId) : null;
        var detailWrap = config.detailWrapId ? document.getElementById(config.detailWrapId) : null;
        var detailSelect = config.detailSelectId ? document.getElementById(config.detailSelectId) : null;
        var filterWrap = config.filterWrapId ? document.getElementById(config.filterWrapId) : null;
        var filterSelect = config.filterSelectId ? document.getElementById(config.filterSelectId) : null;
        var filterValue = config.filterValueId ? document.getElementById(config.filterValueId) : null;
        var filterValidation = config.filterValidationId ? document.getElementById(config.filterValidationId) : null;
        var refreshStopNote = config.refreshStopNoteId ? document.getElementById(config.refreshStopNoteId) : null;
        var modeGuide = config.modeGuideId ? document.getElementById(config.modeGuideId) : null;
        var commandNote = document.getElementById(config.commandNoteId);
        var emptyState = config.emptyStateId ? document.getElementById(config.emptyStateId) : null;
        var commandContent = config.commandContentId ? document.getElementById(config.commandContentId) : null;
        var refDevice = config.refDeviceId ? document.getElementById(config.refDeviceId) : null;
        var portLines = config.portLinesId ? document.getElementById(config.portLinesId) : null;
        var portReference = config.portReferenceId ? document.getElementById(config.portReferenceId) : null;
        var radModelWrap = config.radModelWrapId ? document.getElementById(config.radModelWrapId) : null;
        var radModelSelect = config.radModelSelectId ? document.getElementById(config.radModelSelectId) : null;
        var deviceSequenceControls = config.deviceSequenceControlsId ? document.getElementById(config.deviceSequenceControlsId) : null;
        var deviceStepLabel = config.deviceStepLabelId ? document.getElementById(config.deviceStepLabelId) : null;
        var devicePrevButton = config.devicePrevButtonId ? document.getElementById(config.devicePrevButtonId) : null;
        var deviceNextButton = config.deviceNextButtonId ? document.getElementById(config.deviceNextButtonId) : null;
        var commandSequenceControls = config.commandSequenceControlsId ? document.getElementById(config.commandSequenceControlsId) : null;
        var commandStepLabel = config.commandStepLabelId ? document.getElementById(config.commandStepLabelId) : null;
        var commandPrevButton = config.commandPrevButtonId ? document.getElementById(config.commandPrevButtonId) : null;
        var commandNextButton = config.commandNextButtonId ? document.getElementById(config.commandNextButtonId) : null;
        var deviceStepIndex = 0;
        var commandStepIndex = 0;

        if (!taskSelect || !inputFields || !output) return;

        function getDeviceName() {
            return deviceSelect ? (deviceSelect.value || dataRoot.defaultDevice) : dataRoot.defaultDevice;
        }

        function getCaseValueScopeKey() {
            var deviceName = getDeviceName();
            var modelName = deviceName === "RAD ETX-2" && radModelSelect
                ? (radModelSelect.value || "203AX")
                : "";
            return [config.type, deviceName, modelName].join("|");
        }

        function getRememberedCaseValue(key) {
            var scope = caseValueMemory[getCaseValueScopeKey()];
            return scope && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(scope, key) ? scope[key] : "";
        }

        function rememberCaseValue(key, value) {
            var scopeKey = getCaseValueScopeKey();
            var scope = caseValueMemory[scopeKey];
            if (!scope) {
                scope = Object.create(null);
                caseValueMemory[scopeKey] = scope;
            }
            if (value) scope[key] = value;
            else delete scope[key];
            if (!Object.keys(scope).length) delete caseValueMemory[scopeKey];
        }

        function getDeviceData() {
            return dataRoot.devices[getDeviceName()];
        }

        function getSelectedRadModelData() {
            if (getDeviceName() !== "RAD ETX-2" || !radModelSelect) return null;
            var modelKey = radModelSelect.value || "203AX";
            return radModelPorts[modelKey] || radModelPorts["203AX"];
        }

        function getEffectiveDeviceCheck(deviceData) {
            var modelData = getSelectedRadModelData();
            return modelData && modelData.deviceCheck ? modelData.deviceCheck : (deviceData && deviceData.deviceCheck);
        }

        function getAvailableTasks(deviceData) {
            var tasks = deviceData && Array.isArray(deviceData.tasks) ? deviceData.tasks.slice() : [];
            var modelData = getSelectedRadModelData();
            if (modelData && radModelSelect) {
                var modelKey = radModelSelect.value || "203AX";
                tasks = tasks.filter(function (item) {
                    return !Array.isArray(item.radModels) || item.radModels.indexOf(modelKey) !== -1;
                });
            }
            return tasks;
        }

        function getCurrentTask() {
            var tasks = getAvailableTasks(getDeviceData());
            var index = parseInt(taskSelect.value || "0", 10);
            return tasks[index] || null;
        }

        function updateSequenceControls(controls, label, previousButton, nextButton, steps, index) {
            var count = Array.isArray(steps) ? steps.length : 0;
            var visible = config.type === "nid" && count > 1;
            if (controls) controls.hidden = !visible;
            if (label) label.textContent = "Step " + (Math.min(index, Math.max(count - 1, 0)) + 1) + " of " + Math.max(count, 1);
            if (previousButton) previousButton.disabled = !visible || index <= 0;
            if (nextButton) nextButton.disabled = !visible || index >= count - 1;
        }

        function renderDeviceCheck() {
            if (!deviceOutput) return;
            var deviceData = getDeviceData();
            var steps = splitCommandSequence(getEffectiveDeviceCheck(deviceData) || "show version");
            if (!steps.length) steps = ["show version"];
            deviceStepIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(deviceStepIndex, steps.length - 1));
            deviceOutput.textContent = steps[deviceStepIndex];
            updateSequenceControls(deviceSequenceControls, deviceStepLabel, devicePrevButton, deviceNextButton, steps, deviceStepIndex);
            deviceOutput.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");
        }

        function showCommandStatus(statusElement, text) {
            if (!statusElement) return;
            statusElement.style.color = "var(--alert)";
            statusElement.textContent = text;
            setTimeout(function () {
                statusElement.textContent = "";
                statusElement.style.color = "";
            }, 2400);
        }

        function copyReadyCommand(text, statusElement, flashMissing) {
            copyCommandText(text, statusElement, flashMissing);
        }

        function updateDeviceCheckVisibility(cmd) {
            if (!deviceWrap || !deviceOutput) return;
            var deviceSteps = splitCommandSequence(getEffectiveDeviceCheck(getDeviceData()) || "");
            var deviceCmd = (deviceOutput.textContent || "").trim();
            var generatedCmd = (cmd || "").trim();
            deviceWrap.hidden = deviceSteps.length <= 1 && !!deviceCmd && deviceCmd === generatedCmd;
        }

        function updateOutputControlsVisibility() {
            if (!outputControls) return;
            var detailVisible = !!detailWrap && !detailWrap.hidden;
            var filterVisible = !!filterWrap && !filterWrap.hidden;
            outputControls.hidden = !detailVisible && !filterVisible;
        }

        function resetOutputDetail() {
            if (detailSelect) detailSelect.value = "";
            if (detailWrap) detailWrap.hidden = true;
            updateOutputControlsVisibility();
        }

        function resetOutputFilter() {
            if (filterSelect) filterSelect.value = "";
            if (filterValue) {
                filterValue.value = "";
                filterValue.type = "text";
                filterValue.placeholder = "Text or regex";
                filterValue.hidden = true;
                filterValue.disabled = true;
                filterValue.classList.remove("invalid-value", "input-flash");
                filterValue.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
            }
            if (filterValidation) filterValidation.textContent = "";
            if (refreshStopNote) refreshStopNote.hidden = true;
            if (filterWrap) {
                filterWrap.hidden = true;
                filterWrap.classList.remove("filter-error");
            }
            updateOutputControlsVisibility();
        }

        function updateFilterInputControl() {
            if (!filterSelect || !filterValue) return;
            var deviceName = getDeviceName();
            var spec = getOutputFilterSpec(deviceName, filterSelect.value);
            var needsValue = !!spec && spec.input !== "none";
            var numeric = !!spec && ["positive", "nonnegative", "range"].indexOf(spec.input) !== -1;
            filterValue.hidden = !needsValue;
            filterValue.disabled = !needsValue;
            filterValue.type = numeric ? "number" : "text";
            filterValue.placeholder = spec && spec.placeholder ? spec.placeholder : "Text or regex";
            if (numeric) {
                filterValue.min = String(spec.min == null ? 1 : spec.min);
                if (spec.max != null) filterValue.max = String(spec.max);
                else filterValue.removeAttribute("max");
                filterValue.step = "1";
                filterValue.inputMode = "numeric";
            } else {
                filterValue.removeAttribute("min");
                filterValue.removeAttribute("max");
                filterValue.removeAttribute("step");
                filterValue.removeAttribute("inputmode");
            }
            if (refreshStopNote) refreshStopNote.hidden = !(isJunosDevice(deviceName) && filterSelect.value === "refresh");
            if (!needsValue) {
                filterValue.value = "";
                filterValue.classList.remove("invalid-value");
                filterValue.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
                if (filterValidation) filterValidation.textContent = "";
                if (filterWrap) filterWrap.classList.remove("filter-error");
            }
        }

        function setDetailAvailability(deviceName, baseCommand, ready) {
            if (!detailWrap || !detailSelect) return false;
            var eligible = isJunosInterfaceDetailEligible(deviceName, baseCommand);
            detailWrap.hidden = !eligible;
            detailSelect.disabled = !eligible || !ready;
            if (!eligible) resetOutputDetail();
            updateOutputControlsVisibility();
            return eligible;
        }

        function setFilterAvailability(deviceName, baseCommand, ready) {
            if (!filterWrap || !filterSelect) return false;
            populateOutputFilterOptions(filterSelect, deviceName);
            var eligible = isDeviceOutputFilterEligible(deviceName, baseCommand);
            filterWrap.hidden = !eligible;
            filterSelect.disabled = !eligible || !ready;
            if (!eligible) resetOutputFilter();
            updateOutputControlsVisibility();
            return eligible;
        }

        function updateCommandModeGuide(deviceName, commandText, ready, rawProperties) {
            if (!modeGuide) return;
            var intelligence = inferCommandIntelligence(deviceName, commandText, rawProperties);
            var visible = !!ready && !!intelligence.hint;
            modeGuide.hidden = !visible;
            modeGuide.textContent = visible ? intelligence.hint : "";
            modeGuide.removeAttribute("title");
        }

        function flashMissingInputs() {
            output.classList.remove("input-flash");
            void output.offsetWidth;
            output.classList.add("input-flash");

            inputFields.querySelectorAll(".nid-input").forEach(function (input) {
                var check = validateTerminalValue(input.dataset.key, input.value);
                if (!check.ok) {
                    input.classList.remove("input-flash");
                    void input.offsetWidth;
                    input.classList.add("input-flash");
                    setTimeout(function () { input.classList.remove("input-flash"); }, 2100);
                }
            });

            if (filterValue && !filterValue.hidden) {
                var filterCheck = validateDeviceOutputFilterValue(getDeviceName(), filterSelect ? filterSelect.value : "", filterValue.value);
                if (!filterCheck.ok) {
                    filterValue.classList.remove("input-flash");
                    void filterValue.offsetWidth;
                    filterValue.classList.add("input-flash");
                    setTimeout(function () { filterValue.classList.remove("input-flash"); }, 2100);
                }
            }

            setTimeout(function () { output.classList.remove("input-flash"); }, 2100);
        }

        function renderPortRef(deviceName, deviceData) {
            if (!refDevice || !portLines || !deviceData) return;

            var ports = deviceData.ports || [];
            var structuredReference = Array.isArray(deviceData.portReference) ? deviceData.portReference : [];
            var title = deviceName;

            if (radModelWrap && radModelSelect) {
                var isRad = deviceName === "RAD ETX-2";
                radModelWrap.hidden = !isRad;
                var deviceTaskRow = radModelWrap.closest(".builder-row");
                if (deviceTaskRow) deviceTaskRow.classList.toggle("rad-model-active", isRad);

                if (isRad) {
                    var selectedModel = radModelSelect.value || "203AX";
                    var modelData = radModelPorts[selectedModel] || radModelPorts["203AX"];
                    ports = modelData.ports;
                    title = deviceName + " · " + modelData.label;
                }
            }

            var hasReference = structuredReference.length > 0 || ports.length > 0;
            if (portReference) {
                portReference.hidden = !hasReference;
                if (!hasReference) portReference.removeAttribute("open");
            }

            refDevice.textContent = title;
            if (!hasReference) {
                portLines.innerHTML = "";
                return;
            }

            if (structuredReference.length) {
                portLines.innerHTML = "";
                structuredReference.forEach(function (entry) {
                    var item = document.createElement("div");
                    item.className = "qfx-port-entry";

                    var name = document.createElement("div");
                    name.className = "qfx-port-name";
                    name.textContent = entry.name || "";
                    item.appendChild(name);

                    var notes = document.createElement("ul");
                    notes.className = "qfx-port-notes";
                    (entry.notes || []).forEach(function (noteText) {
                        var note = document.createElement("li");
                        note.textContent = noteText;
                        notes.appendChild(note);
                    });
                    item.appendChild(notes);
                    portLines.appendChild(item);
                });
                return;
            }

            portLines.innerHTML = ports.map(function (line) {
                return '<div class="line"><span class="tag ' + line[0] + '">' + line[1] + '</span><span>' + line[2] + '</span></div>';
            }).join("");
        }

        function renderCommand() {
            var item = getCurrentTask();
            if (!item) return;
            var properties = normalizeCommandProperties(item.meta);
            if (item.referenceOnly) {
                inputFields.innerHTML = "";
                output.textContent = "No command is available for this task.";
                output.classList.add("needs-input");
                output.dataset.ready = "false";
                output.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                renderCommandNote(commandNote, item, "");
                updateDeviceCheckVisibility("");
                return;
            }
            var result = applyInputsToCommand(item.cmd, inputFields);
            var deviceName = getDeviceName();

            if (copyStatus) {
                copyStatus.textContent = "";
                copyStatus.style.color = "";
            }

            if (result.invalid.length) {
                setDetailAvailability(deviceName, item.cmd, false);
                setFilterAvailability(deviceName, item.cmd, false);
                updateCommandModeGuide(deviceName, item.cmd, false, properties);
                updateSequenceControls(commandSequenceControls, commandStepLabel, commandPrevButton, commandNextButton, [], 0);
                output.textContent = "Check " + result.invalid.map(function (entry) { return entry.label; }).join(" and ") + " above";
                output.classList.add("needs-input");
                output.dataset.ready = "false";
                output.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                renderCommandNote(commandNote, item, result.invalid[0].message);
                updateDeviceCheckVisibility("");
                return;
            }

            if (result.missing.length) {
                setDetailAvailability(deviceName, item.cmd, false);
                setFilterAvailability(deviceName, item.cmd, false);
                updateCommandModeGuide(deviceName, item.cmd, false, properties);
                updateSequenceControls(commandSequenceControls, commandStepLabel, commandPrevButton, commandNextButton, [], 0);
                output.textContent = "Enter " + result.missing.join(" and ") + " above";
                output.classList.add("needs-input");
                output.dataset.ready = "false";
                output.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                renderCommandNote(commandNote, item, "");
                updateCommandModeGuide(deviceName, item.cmd, false, properties);
                updateDeviceCheckVisibility("");
                return;
            }

            var commandSteps = splitCommandSequence(result.cmd);
            if (!commandSteps.length) commandSteps = [result.cmd.trim()];
            commandStepIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(commandStepIndex, commandSteps.length - 1));
            updateSequenceControls(commandSequenceControls, commandStepLabel, commandPrevButton, commandNextButton, commandSteps, commandStepIndex);
            var baseCommand = commandSteps[commandStepIndex];
            var detailAvailable = setDetailAvailability(deviceName, baseCommand, true);
            var detailed = { ok: true, command: baseCommand, message: "" };
            if (detailAvailable && detailSelect && detailSelect.value) {
                detailed = buildJunosInterfaceDetailCommand(baseCommand, detailSelect.value);
            }

            var commandBeforeFilter = detailed.ok ? detailed.command : baseCommand;
            var filterAvailable = setFilterAvailability(deviceName, commandBeforeFilter, true);
            var built = detailed;

            if (built.ok && filterAvailable && filterSelect && filterSelect.value) {
                built = buildDeviceOutputFilteredCommand(deviceName, commandBeforeFilter, filterSelect.value, filterValue ? filterValue.value : "");
            }

            if (!built.ok) {
                output.textContent = detailAvailable && detailSelect && detailSelect.value && !detailed.ok ? "Choose a supported detail" : ((filterSelect && ["last", "trim", "refresh"].indexOf(filterSelect.value) !== -1) ? "Enter a number" : "Enter filter text");
                output.classList.add("needs-input");
                output.dataset.ready = "false";
                output.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                if (filterValidation) filterValidation.textContent = built.message;
                if (filterValue) {
                    filterValue.classList.add("invalid-value");
                    filterValue.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
                }
                if (filterWrap) filterWrap.classList.add("filter-error");
                renderCommandNote(commandNote, item, built.message || detailed.message || "Check the selected output option.");
                updateCommandModeGuide(deviceName, item.cmd, false, properties);
                updateDeviceCheckVisibility("");
                return;
            }

            if (filterValidation) filterValidation.textContent = "";
            if (filterValue) {
                filterValue.classList.remove("invalid-value");
                filterValue.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
            }
            if (filterWrap) filterWrap.classList.remove("filter-error");

            output.textContent = built.command;
            output.classList.remove("needs-input");
            output.dataset.ready = "true";
            output.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");
            renderCommandNote(commandNote, item, "");
            updateCommandModeGuide(deviceName, commandBeforeFilter, true, properties);
            updateDeviceCheckVisibility(built.command);
        }

        function renderInputs() {
            var item = getCurrentTask();
            if (!item) return;

            commandStepIndex = 0;
            inputFields.innerHTML = "";
            item.fields.forEach(function (itemField) {
                createInputField(
                    config.label,
                    inputFields,
                    itemField,
                    renderCommand,
                    getRememberedCaseValue(itemField.key),
                    rememberCaseValue
                );
            });

            renderCommand();
        }

        function renderDevice() {
            resetOutputDetail();
            resetOutputFilter();
            if (modeGuide) modeGuide.hidden = true;
            var deviceName = getDeviceName();
            var deviceData = getDeviceData();
            if (!deviceData) return;
            var availableTasks = getAvailableTasks(deviceData);

            renderPortRef(deviceName, deviceData);

            if (!availableTasks.length) {
                taskSelect.innerHTML = "";
                taskSelect.appendChild(option("", "No commands added yet"));
                taskSelect.disabled = true;
                inputFields.innerHTML = "";
                if (emptyState) emptyState.hidden = false;
                if (commandContent) commandContent.hidden = true;
                if (output) {
                    output.textContent = "";
                    output.classList.add("needs-input");
                    output.dataset.ready = "false";
                    output.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                }
                if (commandNote) commandNote.innerHTML = "";
                if (copyStatus) copyStatus.textContent = "";
                return;
            }

            taskSelect.disabled = false;
            if (emptyState) emptyState.hidden = true;
            if (commandContent) commandContent.hidden = false;

            deviceStepIndex = 0;
            renderDeviceCheck();

            populateGroupedTaskSelect(taskSelect, availableTasks);
            taskSelect.value = "0";

            renderInputs();
        }

        if (deviceSelect) {
            deviceSelect.innerHTML = "";
            Object.keys(dataRoot.devices).forEach(function (deviceName) {
                deviceSelect.appendChild(option(deviceName));
            });
            deviceSelect.value = dataRoot.defaultDevice;
            deviceSelect.addEventListener("change", renderDevice);
        }

        taskSelect.addEventListener("change", function () {
            resetOutputDetail();
            resetOutputFilter();
            if (modeGuide) modeGuide.hidden = true;
            renderInputs();
        });

        if (radModelSelect) {
            radModelSelect.addEventListener("change", function () {
                renderDevice();
            });
        }

        if (detailSelect) {
            detailSelect.addEventListener("change", renderCommand);
        }

        if (filterSelect) {
            filterSelect.addEventListener("change", function () {
                updateFilterInputControl();
                renderCommand();
                if (filterValue && !filterValue.hidden) filterValue.focus();
            });
        }

        if (filterValue) {
            filterValue.addEventListener("input", renderCommand);
            filterValue.addEventListener("change", renderCommand);
        }


        if (devicePrevButton) {
            devicePrevButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (deviceStepIndex <= 0) return;
                deviceStepIndex -= 1;
                renderDeviceCheck();
                updateDeviceCheckVisibility(output.textContent);
            });
        }

        if (deviceNextButton) {
            deviceNextButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                var steps = splitCommandSequence(getEffectiveDeviceCheck(getDeviceData()) || "");
                if (deviceStepIndex >= steps.length - 1) return;
                deviceStepIndex += 1;
                renderDeviceCheck();
                updateDeviceCheckVisibility(output.textContent);
            });
        }

        if (commandPrevButton) {
            commandPrevButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                if (commandStepIndex <= 0) return;
                commandStepIndex -= 1;
                renderCommand();
            });
        }

        if (commandNextButton) {
            commandNextButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                var item = getCurrentTask();
                if (!item) return;
                var result = applyInputsToCommand(item.cmd, inputFields);
                var steps = splitCommandSequence(result.cmd);
                if (result.missing.length || result.invalid.length || commandStepIndex >= steps.length - 1) return;
                commandStepIndex += 1;
                renderCommand();
            });
        }

        if (deviceOutput) {
            deviceOutput.addEventListener("click", function () {
                copyReadyCommand(deviceOutput.textContent, deviceCopyStatus, flashMissingInputs);
            });
        }

        function copyGeneratedOutput() {
            if (output.dataset.ready !== "true") {
                flashMissingInputs();
                showCommandStatus(copyStatus, "Fill in the command and filter values first");
                return;
            }
            copyReadyCommand(output.textContent, copyStatus, flashMissingInputs);
        }

        output.addEventListener("click", copyGeneratedOutput);

        window[config.resetName] = function () {
            if (deviceSelect) deviceSelect.value = dataRoot.defaultDevice;
            renderDevice();
        };

        renderDevice();
    }

    function initializeCesNidIpLookup() {
        var details = document.getElementById("cesNidIpLookup");
        var deviceSelect = document.getElementById("cesDeviceSelect");
        var taskSelect = document.getElementById("cesTaskSelect");
        var macInput = document.getElementById("cesNidLookupMac");
        var arpCommand = document.getElementById("cesNidLookupArpCommand");
        var validation = document.getElementById("cesNidLookupValidation");
        if (!details || !deviceSelect || !taskSelect || !macInput || !arpCommand || details.dataset.initialized === "true") return;

        function normalizeMac(value) {
            var text = String(value || "").trim().replace(/-/g, ":").toLowerCase();
            return /^(?:[0-9a-f]{2}:){5}[0-9a-f]{2}$/.test(text) ? text : "";
        }

        function buildArpCommand(value) {
            var mac = normalizeMac(value);
            return mac ? "show arp no-resolve | match " + mac : "";
        }

        function updateArpCommand() {
            var raw = String(macInput.value || "").trim();
            var command = buildArpCommand(raw);
            if (command) {
                var normalized = normalizeMac(raw);
                if (macInput.value !== normalized) macInput.value = normalized;
                arpCommand.textContent = command;
                arpCommand.classList.remove("needs-input");
                arpCommand.dataset.ready = "true";
                arpCommand.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");
                validation.textContent = "";
                macInput.classList.remove("invalid-value");
                macInput.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
            } else {
                arpCommand.textContent = "Enter MAC Address above";
                arpCommand.classList.add("needs-input");
                arpCommand.dataset.ready = "false";
                arpCommand.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                validation.textContent = raw ? "Use MAC format 00:20:d2:64:72:ad" : "";
                macInput.classList.toggle("invalid-value", !!raw);
                if (raw) macInput.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
                else macInput.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
            }
        }

        function selectExistingMacTask() {
            if (deviceSelect.value !== "Juniper QFX") return false;
            var options = Array.prototype.slice.call(taskSelect.options);
            var target = options.find(function (optionNode) {
                return String(optionNode.textContent || "").trim() === "Find MACs on Interface";
            });
            if (!target) return false;
            if (taskSelect.value !== target.value) {
                taskSelect.value = target.value;
                taskSelect.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
            }
            return true;
        }

        function clearLookupState() {
            details.open = false;
            macInput.value = "";
            updateArpCommand();
        }

        function syncVisibility() {
            var isQfx = deviceSelect.value === "Juniper QFX";
            details.hidden = !isQfx;
            if (!isQfx) clearLookupState();
        }

        details.dataset.initialized = "true";
        details.addEventListener("toggle", function () {
            if (details.open) selectExistingMacTask();
        });
        macInput.addEventListener("input", updateArpCommand);
        arpCommand.addEventListener("click", function () {
            if (arpCommand.dataset.ready !== "true") {
                macInput.focus();
                return;
            }
            quickCopy(arpCommand.textContent, arpCommand);
        });
        deviceSelect.addEventListener("change", syncVisibility);

        var originalReset = window.resetCesCommandBuilder;
        if (typeof originalReset === "function" && !originalReset._focusNotesNidLookupWrapped) {
            var wrappedReset = function () {
                originalReset();
                clearLookupState();
                syncVisibility();
            };
            wrappedReset._focusNotesNidLookupWrapped = true;
            window.resetCesCommandBuilder = wrappedReset;}

        updateArpCommand();
        syncVisibility();

        window.FOCUS_NOTES_CES_NID_IP_LOOKUP = Object.freeze({
            normalizeMac: normalizeMac,
            buildArpCommand: buildArpCommand,
            selectMacTask: selectExistingMacTask,
            reset: clearLookupState
        });
    }

    function initCommandBuilders() {
        setupCommandBuilder({
            type: "nid",
            label: "NID",
            resetName: "resetNidCommandBuilder",
            deviceSelectId: "nidDeviceSelect",
            taskSelectId: "nidTaskSelect",
            inputFieldsId: "nidInputFields",
            deviceOutputId: "nidDeviceOutput",
            deviceWrapId: "nidDeviceWrap",
            deviceCopyStatusId: "nidDeviceCopyStatus",
            outputId: "nidCommandOutput",
            copyStatusId: "nidCopyStatus",
            outputControlsId: "nidJunosControls",
            detailWrapId: "nidOutputDetailWrap",
            detailSelectId: "nidOutputDetailSelect",
            filterWrapId: "nidOutputFilterWrap",
            filterSelectId: "nidOutputFilterSelect",
            filterValueId: "nidOutputFilterValue",
            filterValidationId: "nidOutputFilterValidation",
            refreshStopNoteId: "nidRefreshStopNote",
            modeGuideId: "nidJunosModeGuide",
            commandNoteId: "nidCommandNote",
            portReferenceId: "nidPortReference",
            refDeviceId: "nidRefDevice",
            portLinesId: "nidPortLines",
            radModelWrapId: "nidRadModelWrap",
            radModelSelectId: "nidRadModelSelect",
            deviceSequenceControlsId: "nidDeviceSequenceControls",
            deviceStepLabelId: "nidDeviceStepLabel",
            devicePrevButtonId: "nidDevicePrevButton",
            deviceNextButtonId: "nidDeviceNextButton",
            commandSequenceControlsId: "nidCommandSequenceControls",
            commandStepLabelId: "nidCommandStepLabel",
            commandPrevButtonId: "nidCommandPrevButton",
            commandNextButtonId: "nidCommandNextButton"
        });

        setupCommandBuilder({
            type: "cer",
            label: "CER",
            resetName: "resetCerCommandBuilder",
            deviceSelectId: "cerDeviceSelect",
            taskSelectId: "cerTaskSelect",
            inputFieldsId: "cerInputFields",
            deviceOutputId: "cerDeviceOutput",
            deviceWrapId: "cerDeviceWrap",
            deviceCopyStatusId: "cerDeviceCopyStatus",
            outputId: "cerCommandOutput",
            copyStatusId: "cerCopyStatus",
            outputControlsId: "cerJunosControls",
            detailWrapId: "cerOutputDetailWrap",
            detailSelectId: "cerOutputDetailSelect",
            filterWrapId: "cerOutputFilterWrap",
            filterSelectId: "cerOutputFilterSelect",
            filterValueId: "cerOutputFilterValue",
            filterValidationId: "cerOutputFilterValidation",
            refreshStopNoteId: "cerRefreshStopNote",
            modeGuideId: "cerJunosModeGuide",
            commandNoteId: "cerCommandNote"
        });

        setupCommandBuilder({
            type: "ces",
            label: "CES",
            resetName: "resetCesCommandBuilder",
            deviceSelectId: "cesDeviceSelect",
            taskSelectId: "cesTaskSelect",
            inputFieldsId: "cesInputFields",
            outputId: "cesCommandOutput",
            copyStatusId: "cesCopyStatus",
            modeGuideId: "cesJunosModeGuide",
            commandNoteId: "cesCommandNote",
            emptyStateId: "cesEmptyState",
            commandContentId: "cesCommandContent",
            portReferenceId: "cesPortReference",
            refDeviceId: "cesRefDevice",
            portLinesId: "cesPortLines"
        });
    }

    function initializeCommandTooling() {
        initCommandBuilders();
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initializeCommandTooling);
    } else {
        initializeCommandTooling();
    }
})();
</script>


<script>
(function () {
    "use strict";

    var APP_VERSION = String(document.documentElement.getAttribute("data-app-version") || "").trim();
    var APP_BUILD_DATE = String(document.documentElement.getAttribute("data-build-date") || "").trim();
    window.FOCUS_NOTES_BUILD = { version: APP_VERSION, buildDate: APP_BUILD_DATE };

    function formatBuildDate(dateValue) {
        var parts = String(dateValue || "").split("-");
        var months = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
        if (parts.length !== 3) return String(dateValue || "");
        var monthIndex = Number(parts[1]) - 1;
        var day = Number(parts[2]);
        if (monthIndex < 0 || monthIndex > 11 || !day) return String(dateValue || "");
        return months[monthIndex] + " " + day + ", " + parts[0];
    }

    function expectedBuildMetaText() {
        return "Focus Notes v" + APP_VERSION + " · Updated " + formatBuildDate(APP_BUILD_DATE);
    }

    function syncBuildMetadata(target) {
        var root = target && target.nodeType === 1
            ? target
            : (target && target.documentElement ? target.documentElement : document.documentElement);
        if (!root) return;

        root.setAttribute("data-app-version", APP_VERSION);
        root.setAttribute("data-build-date", APP_BUILD_DATE);

        var titleNode = root.querySelector("title");
        if (titleNode) titleNode.textContent = "Focus Notes v" + APP_VERSION + " - SecureCRT Toolkit";

        var buildMeta = root.querySelector("#buildMeta");
        if (buildMeta) buildMeta.textContent = expectedBuildMetaText();
    }

    syncBuildMetadata(document);

    function findById(root, id) {
        if (!root) return null;
        if (typeof root.getElementById === "function") return root.getElementById(id);
        return root.querySelector("#" + id);
    }

    function resetCloneForSaving(clone) {
        clone.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(function (input) {
            var type = (input.getAttribute("type") || "text").toLowerCase();
            if (type === "checkbox") {
                input.removeAttribute("checked");
                input.checked = false;
            }
            if (input.id === "speedInput") input.setAttribute("value", "200");
            else if (type === "text" || type === "email" || type === "tel" || type === "search" || type === "number") {
                input.setAttribute("value", "");
                input.removeAttribute("data-key");
            }
            input.classList.remove("input-flash", "invalid-value");
            input.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
        });

        clone.querySelectorAll("textarea").forEach(function (textarea) {
            textarea.textContent = "";
            textarea.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
            textarea.style.removeProperty("height");
            textarea.style.removeProperty("min-height");
            textarea.removeAttribute("data-focus-notes-default-height");
        });

        ["issuePreset", "nextStepPreset"].forEach(function (id) {
            var savedPreset = findById(clone, id);
            if (!savedPreset) return;
            savedPreset.value = "";
            Array.prototype.forEach.call(savedPreset.options, function (option) {
                if (option.value === "") option.setAttribute("selected", "");
                else option.removeAttribute("selected");
            });
        });

        var savedAccountCount = findById(clone, "accountNumCount");
        if (savedAccountCount) {
            savedAccountCount.textContent = "0";
            savedAccountCount.setAttribute("aria-label", "0 letters and numbers entered");
        }

        var savedLiveStatus = findById(clone, "status");
        if (savedLiveStatus) {
            savedLiveStatus.textContent = "";
            savedLiveStatus.removeAttribute("style");
        }

        var savedScratchZone = clone.querySelector(".scratch-zone");
        if (savedScratchZone) {
            savedScratchZone.style.removeProperty("height");
            savedScratchZone.style.removeProperty("min-height");
        }
        var savedContainer = clone.querySelector(".container");
        if (savedContainer) savedContainer.style.removeProperty("scroll-padding-top");

        ["nidInputFields", "cerInputFields", "cesInputFields", "selfTestResults", "fullDiagnosticResults"].forEach(function (id) {
            var node = findById(clone, id);
            if (node) node.innerHTML = "";
        });

        ["nidDeviceSelect", "nidTaskSelect", "cerTaskSelect", "cesDeviceSelect", "cesTaskSelect"].forEach(function (id) {
            var select = findById(clone, id);
            if (select) select.innerHTML = "";
        });

        ["nidOutputFilterSelect", "cerOutputFilterSelect"].forEach(function (id) {
            var select = findById(clone, id);
            if (select) {
                select.innerHTML = '<option value="" selected>No filter</option>';
                select.value = "";
                select.removeAttribute("data-filter-family");
            }
        });

        ["nidOutputDetailSelect", "cerOutputDetailSelect"].forEach(function (id) {
            var select = findById(clone, id);
            if (select) {
                select.value = "";
                Array.prototype.forEach.call(select.options, function (option, index) {
                    if (index === 0) option.setAttribute("selected", "");
                    else option.removeAttribute("selected");
                });
            }
        });

        ["nidJunosControls", "cerJunosControls", "nidOutputDetailWrap", "cerOutputDetailWrap", "nidOutputFilterWrap", "cerOutputFilterWrap", "nidJunosModeGuide", "cerJunosModeGuide", "cesJunosModeGuide", "nidRefreshStopNote", "cerRefreshStopNote", "nidDeviceSequenceControls", "nidCommandSequenceControls"].forEach(function (id) {
            var wrap = findById(clone, id);
            if (wrap) {
                wrap.setAttribute("hidden", "");
                wrap.classList.remove("filter-error");
            }
        });

        ["nidOutputFilterValue", "cerOutputFilterValue"].forEach(function (id) {
            var input = findById(clone, id);
            if (input) {
                input.setAttribute("value", "");
                input.setAttribute("hidden", "");
                input.setAttribute("disabled", "");
                input.setAttribute("type", "text");
                input.setAttribute("placeholder", "Text or regex");
                input.classList.remove("invalid-value", "input-flash");
                input.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
            }
        });

        ["nidOutputFilterValidation", "cerOutputFilterValidation"].forEach(function (id) {
            var validation = findById(clone, id);
            if (validation) validation.textContent = "";
        });

        var manualCopyOverlay = findById(clone, "manualCopyFallback");
        if (manualCopyOverlay && manualCopyOverlay.parentNode) manualCopyOverlay.parentNode.removeChild(manualCopyOverlay);

        ["regionalSupportDirectory", "rocContactDetails", "nfsContactDetails", "cesNidIpLookup", "cesPortReference"].forEach(function (id) {
            var details = findById(clone, id);
            if (details) details.removeAttribute("open");
        });

        var savedNidLookupHelper = findById(clone, "cesNidIpLookup");
        if (savedNidLookupHelper) savedNidLookupHelper.removeAttribute("data-initialized");

        var savedNidLookupMac = findById(clone, "cesNidLookupMac");
        if (savedNidLookupMac) {
            savedNidLookupMac.setAttribute("value", "");
            savedNidLookupMac.classList.remove("invalid-value");
            savedNidLookupMac.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
        }
        var savedNidLookupCommand = findById(clone, "cesNidLookupArpCommand");
        if (savedNidLookupCommand) {
            savedNidLookupCommand.textContent = "Enter MAC Address above";
            savedNidLookupCommand.classList.add("needs-input");
            savedNidLookupCommand.setAttribute("data-ready", "false");
            savedNidLookupCommand.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
        }
        var savedNidLookupValidation = findById(clone, "cesNidLookupValidation");
        if (savedNidLookupValidation) savedNidLookupValidation.textContent = "";

        [["cerCommandBuilder", "cerBuilderBody", "cerBuilderToggle"],
         ["cesCommandBuilder", "cesBuilderBody", "cesBuilderToggle"],
         ["nidCommandBuilder", "nidBuilderBody", "nidBuilderToggle"]].forEach(function (group) {
            var panel = findById(clone, group[0]);
            var body = findById(clone, group[1]);
            var toggle = findById(clone, group[2]);
            if (panel) panel.classList.remove("is-open");
            if (body) body.setAttribute("hidden", "");
            if (toggle) {
                toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
                toggle.textContent = "Show";
            }
        });

        var regionalSelect = findById(clone, "regionalSupportRegion");
        if (regionalSelect) {
            regionalSelect.value = "";
            regionalSelect.classList.remove("has-selection", "regional-support-select-prompt");
            Array.prototype.forEach.call(regionalSelect.options, function (option) {
                if (option.value === "") option.setAttribute("selected", "");
                else option.removeAttribute("selected");
            });
        }

        var regionalSummaryRegion = findById(clone, "regionalSupportSummaryRegion");
        if (regionalSummaryRegion) {
            regionalSummaryRegion.textContent = "";
            regionalSummaryRegion.setAttribute("hidden", "");
        }
        var regionalShared = findById(clone, "regionalSupportShared");
        if (regionalShared) regionalShared.setAttribute("hidden", "");
        var regionalArea = findById(clone, "regionalSupportManagementArea");
        if (regionalArea) regionalArea.textContent = "";

        [["rocContactDetails", "rocContactPurpose"], ["nfsContactDetails", "nfsContactPurpose"]].forEach(function (item) {
            var details = findById(clone, item[0]);
            var purpose = findById(clone, item[1]);
            if (details) {
                details.classList.add("is-unavailable");
                details.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
                details.removeAttribute("data-regional-directory-initialized");
            }
            if (purpose) purpose.textContent = "Select Region";
        });

        var exportedRocBody = findById(clone, "rocContactBody");
        if (exportedRocBody) exportedRocBody.innerHTML = "";
        var exportedNfsBody = findById(clone, "nfsContactBody");
        if (exportedNfsBody) exportedNfsBody.innerHTML = "";

        var interfaceProfile = findById(clone, "interfaceProfile");
        if (interfaceProfile) {
            Array.prototype.forEach.call(interfaceProfile.options, function (option) {
                if (option.value === "auto") option.setAttribute("selected", "");
                else option.removeAttribute("selected");
            });
        }

        var calcResult = findById(clone, "calcResult");
        if (calcResult) {
            calcResult.innerHTML = "";
            calcResult.removeAttribute("style");
        }

        var nidDeviceOutput = findById(clone, "nidDeviceOutput");
        if (nidDeviceOutput) {
            nidDeviceOutput.textContent = "show system";
            nidDeviceOutput.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");
            nidDeviceOutput.removeAttribute("title");
        }

        [["nidDeviceStepLabel", "Step 1 of 1"], ["nidCommandStepLabel", "Step 1 of 1"]].forEach(function (item) {
            var label = findById(clone, item[0]);
            if (label) label.textContent = item[1];
        });
        ["nidDevicePrevButton", "nidDeviceNextButton", "nidCommandPrevButton", "nidCommandNextButton"].forEach(function (id) {
            var button = findById(clone, id);
            if (button) button.setAttribute("disabled", "");
        });

        var nidOutput = findById(clone, "nidCommandOutput");
        if (nidOutput) {
            nidOutput.textContent = "show system";
            nidOutput.classList.remove("needs-input", "input-flash");
            nidOutput.setAttribute("data-ready", "true");
            nidOutput.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");
            nidOutput.removeAttribute("title");
        }

        var cerOutput = findById(clone, "cerCommandOutput");
        if (cerOutput) {
            cerOutput.textContent = "Enter Circuit ID above";
            cerOutput.classList.add("needs-input");
            cerOutput.classList.remove("input-flash");
            cerOutput.setAttribute("data-ready", "false");
        }

        var cesOutput = findById(clone, "cesCommandOutput");
        if (cesOutput) {
            cesOutput.textContent = "show system uptime";
            cesOutput.classList.remove("needs-input", "input-flash");
            cesOutput.setAttribute("data-ready", "true");
            cesOutput.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");
        }

        var cesCommandContent = findById(clone, "cesCommandContent");
        if (cesCommandContent) cesCommandContent.removeAttribute("hidden");
        var cesEmptyState = findById(clone, "cesEmptyState");
        if (cesEmptyState) cesEmptyState.setAttribute("hidden", "");
        var cesPortReference = findById(clone, "cesPortReference");
        if (cesPortReference) cesPortReference.removeAttribute("hidden");

        ["nidCommandNote", "cerCommandNote", "cesCommandNote"].forEach(function (id) {
            var note = findById(clone, id);
            if (note) note.textContent = "";
        });


        var selfTestCard = findById(clone, "selfTestCard");
        var selfTestBody = findById(clone, "selfTestBody");
        var selfTestToggle = findById(clone, "selfTestToggle");
        if (selfTestCard) selfTestCard.classList.remove("is-expanded");
        if (selfTestBody) selfTestBody.setAttribute("hidden", "");
        if (selfTestToggle) {
            selfTestToggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
            selfTestToggle.textContent = "Show";
        }
        var diagnosticHealth = findById(clone, "diagnosticHealth");
        if (diagnosticHealth) {
            diagnosticHealth.textContent = "Not run yet";
            diagnosticHealth.classList.remove("is-pass", "is-review", "is-error");
        }

        clone.querySelectorAll(".copy-status").forEach(function (node) {
            node.textContent = "";
