            node.removeAttribute("style");
        });
    }

    function buildUpdatedHtml() {
        var clone = document.documentElement.cloneNode(true);
        resetCloneForSaving(clone);
        syncBuildMetadata(clone);
        return "<!DOCTYPE html>\n" + clone.outerHTML;
    }

    function exportCheck(section, name, pass, detail) {
        return {
            section: section,
            name: name,
            pass: !!pass,
            detail: pass ? "" : (detail || "Check failed.")
        };
    }

    function validateGeneratedHtml(fullHtml) {
        var checks = [];
        var parsed = null;

        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Document",
            "DOCTYPE is present",
            /^<!DOCTYPE html>/i.test(String(fullHtml || "")),
            "The generated code does not begin with <!DOCTYPE html>."
        ));

        try {
            parsed = new DOMParser().parseFromString(String(fullHtml || ""), "text/html");
            checks.push(exportCheck(
                "Document",
                "Generated HTML can be parsed",
                !!(parsed && parsed.documentElement),
                "The browser could not parse the generated HTML."
            ));
        } catch (error) {
            checks.push(exportCheck(
                "Document",
                "Generated HTML can be parsed",
                false,
                error && error.message ? error.message : "DOMParser failed."
            ));
        }

        if (!parsed || !parsed.documentElement) {
            return { ok: false, checks: checks, failed: checks.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }) };
        }

        var root = parsed.documentElement;
        var parsedTitle = parsed.querySelector("title");
        var parsedBuildMeta = parsed.getElementById("buildMeta");
        var parsedMain = parsed.querySelector("main");
        var parsedHeading = parsed.querySelector("h1");
        var parsedLiveStatus = parsed.getElementById("status");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Accessibility",
            "Main landmark and page heading are preserved",
            parsed.querySelectorAll("main").length === 1 && parsed.querySelectorAll("h1").length === 1 &&
                !!parsedMain && !!parsedHeading && parsedMain.contains(parsedHeading) && /Focus Notes/i.test(parsedHeading.textContent),
            "The exported HTML is missing its single main landmark or page heading."
        ));
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Accessibility",
            "Primary live status starts blank",
            !!parsedLiveStatus && parsedLiveStatus.textContent.trim() === "",
            "The exported primary live status contains a stale message."
        ));
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Version",
            "Version and build date are synchronized",
            root.getAttribute("data-app-version") === APP_VERSION &&
                root.getAttribute("data-build-date") === APP_BUILD_DATE &&
                !!parsedTitle && parsedTitle.textContent === "Focus Notes v" + APP_VERSION + " - SecureCRT Toolkit" &&
                !!parsedBuildMeta && parsedBuildMeta.textContent === expectedBuildMetaText(),
            "Visible or embedded version/build metadata does not match v" + APP_VERSION + "."
        ));

        var exportedDiagnosticButton = parsed.getElementById("copySelfTestDiagnosticsButton");
        var exportedFullDiagnosticButton = parsed.getElementById("runFullDiagnosticsButton");
        var exportedDiagnosticHealth = parsed.getElementById("diagnosticHealth");
        var exportedDiagnosticHeading = parsed.querySelector("#selfTestCard h2");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Diagnostics",
            "Focus Notes Diagnostics controls are preserved",
            !!exportedDiagnosticButton && !!exportedFullDiagnosticButton && !!exportedDiagnosticHealth &&
                !!exportedDiagnosticHeading && exportedDiagnosticHeading.textContent.trim() === "Focus Notes Diagnostics" &&
                /FOCUS_NOTES_DIAGNOSTICS/.test(String(fullHtml || "")) && /FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS/.test(String(fullHtml || "")),
            "The exported file is missing Focus Notes Diagnostics controls or diagnostic runtime support."
        ));

        var exportedNextStep = parsed.getElementById("nextStep");
        var exportedNextStepPreset = parsed.getElementById("nextStepPreset");
        var exportedIssue = parsed.getElementById("issue");
        var exportedIssuePreset = parsed.getElementById("issuePreset");
        var nextStepPresetValues = exportedNextStepPreset ? Array.prototype.map.call(exportedNextStepPreset.options, function (option) {
            return option.value;
        }).filter(Boolean) : [];
        var issuePresetValues = exportedIssuePreset ? Array.prototype.map.call(exportedIssuePreset.options, function (option) {
            return option.value;
        }).filter(Boolean) : [];
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Tracker Fields",
            "Issue and Next Step preserve matching preset-plus-multiline workflows",
            !!exportedNextStep && exportedNextStep.tagName === "TEXTAREA" &&
                !!exportedNextStepPreset && exportedNextStepPreset.tagName === "SELECT" &&
                nextStepPresetValues.join("|") === "Pending Technician Visit|Pending Engineer Response|Pending Layer 1 Troubleshooting|Pending Resolution Confirmation|Pending Customer Response" &&
                !!exportedIssue && exportedIssue.tagName === "TEXTAREA" &&
                !!exportedIssuePreset && exportedIssuePreset.tagName === "SELECT" &&
                issuePresetValues.join("|") === "No Service|Intermittent Service|Slow Performance|Packet Loss / Latency|Port / Interface Down|Errors / Drops|Configuration / VLAN Issue|Equipment Issue|Customer Requesting Ticket Update",
            "The exported Issue or Next Step preset-plus-multiline workflow is missing or has changed presets."
        ));


        var builderStack = parsed.querySelector(".directory-tools-stack");
        var builderOrder = builderStack ? Array.prototype.filter.call(builderStack.children, function (node) {
            return ["cerCommandBuilder", "cesCommandBuilder", "nidCommandBuilder"].indexOf(node.id) !== -1;
        }).map(function (node) { return node.id; }) : [];
        var exportedBuilderDefaults = ["cer", "ces", "nid"].every(function (prefix) {
            var body = parsed.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderBody");
            var toggle = parsed.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderToggle");
            return !!body && body.hasAttribute("hidden") && !!toggle && toggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") === "false";
        });
        var exportedCes = parsed.getElementById("cesCommandBuilder");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Command Builders",
            "CES stays active while builders export collapsed in CER/CES/NID order",
            builderOrder.join("|") === "cerCommandBuilder|cesCommandBuilder|nidCommandBuilder" &&
                exportedBuilderDefaults && !!exportedCes && !exportedCes.hasAttribute("hidden") &&
                !!exportedCes.querySelector("#cesDeviceSelect") && !!exportedCes.querySelector("#cesTaskSelect") &&
                !!exportedCes.querySelector('#cesCommandOutput') &&
                exportedCes.querySelector("#cesCommandOutput").tagName === "BUTTON",
            "Builder order, default collapsed state, or active CES controls changed in the exported HTML."
        ));

        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "CES NID Lookup",
            "NID IP helper exports collapsed with no temporary MAC data",
            (function () {
                var helper = parsed.getElementById("cesNidIpLookup");
                var mac = parsed.getElementById("cesNidLookupMac");
                var command = parsed.getElementById("cesNidLookupArpCommand");
                var validation = parsed.getElementById("cesNidLookupValidation");
                return !!helper && !helper.hasAttribute("open") && !!mac && !mac.value &&
                    !!command && command.textContent.trim() === "Enter MAC Address above" &&
                    command.getAttribute("aria-disabled") === "true" &&
                    (!validation || !validation.textContent.trim());
            })(),
            "The exported CES NID IP helper retained temporary state."
        ));

        var exportedResources = parsed.querySelector(".dir-head-resources");
        var exportedDiagnostics = parsed.getElementById("selfTestCard");
        var exportedSupport = parsed.querySelector(".support-lines-box");
        var exportedBuild = parsed.getElementById("buildMeta");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Layout",
            "Tools, reference/support, and Diagnostics preserve the approved hierarchy",
            !!builderStack && !!exportedResources && !!exportedDiagnostics && !!exportedSupport && !!exportedBuild &&
                !!(builderStack.compareDocumentPosition(exportedResources) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) &&
                !!(exportedSupport.compareDocumentPosition(exportedDiagnostics) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING) &&
                !!(exportedDiagnostics.compareDocumentPosition(exportedBuild) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING),
            "The exported tools/reference/Diagnostics hierarchy changed."
        ));

        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Command Builders",
            "Builder headers stay simple",
            [
                ["cerCommandBuilder", "CER"],
                ["cesCommandBuilder", "CES"],
                ["nidCommandBuilder", "Fiber NID&MTU"]
            ].every(function (entry) {
                var panel = parsed.getElementById(entry[0]);
                var heading = panel ? panel.querySelector("h2") : null;
                return !!heading && heading.textContent.trim() === entry[1];
            }),
            "A builder heading no longer matches the expected family label."
        ));

        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Cleanup",
            "Command review and older-widget import workflow is absent",
            typeof window.PENDING_COMMANDS === "undefined" &&
                !parsed.getElementById("pendingCommandData") &&
                !parsed.getElementById("pendingCommandCard") &&
                !parsed.getElementById("savePendingCommandButton") &&
                !parsed.getElementById("importOlderWidgetButton"),
            "The retired review queue or older-widget import workflow remains in the generated HTML."
        ));

        var temporaryIds = [
            "brainDump", "callerName", "itCompany", "accountNum",
            "callbackNum", "email", "address", "issue", "issuePreset", "actions", "nextStep", "nextStepPreset"
        ];
        var uncleared = temporaryIds.filter(function (id) {
            var node = parsed.getElementById(id);
            if (!node) return false;
            var value = "value" in node ? node.value : node.textContent;
            return String(value || "").trim() !== "";
        });
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Privacy",
            "Temporary form values are removed",
            uncleared.length === 0,
            uncleared.length ? "Values remain in: " + uncleared.join(", ") + "." : "Temporary values remain."
        ));

        var savedAccountCount = parsed.getElementById("accountNumCount");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Privacy",
            "Account number counter resets to zero",
            !!savedAccountCount && savedAccountCount.textContent.trim() === "0",
            "The exported account number counter did not reset to zero."
        ));

        var regionalDirectory = parsed.getElementById("regionalSupportDirectory");
        var regionalSelect = parsed.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
        var regionalSummaryRegion = parsed.getElementById("regionalSupportSummaryRegion");
        var regionalShared = parsed.getElementById("regionalSupportShared");
        var regionalManagementArea = parsed.getElementById("regionalSupportManagementArea");
        var exportedRocDetails = parsed.getElementById("rocContactDetails");
        var exportedNfsDetails = parsed.getElementById("nfsContactDetails");
        var exportedRocBody = parsed.getElementById("rocContactBody");
        var exportedNfsBody = parsed.getElementById("nfsContactBody");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Regional Support",
            "Regional selector and Field Group cards export collapsed and neutral",
            !!regionalDirectory && !regionalDirectory.hasAttribute("open") &&
                !!regionalSelect && regionalSelect.value === "" && !regionalSelect.classList.contains("has-selection") &&
                !!regionalSummaryRegion && regionalSummaryRegion.hasAttribute("hidden") && !regionalSummaryRegion.textContent.trim() &&
                !!regionalShared && regionalShared.hasAttribute("hidden") &&
                !!regionalManagementArea && !regionalManagementArea.textContent.trim() &&
                !!exportedRocDetails && !exportedRocDetails.hasAttribute("open") && exportedRocDetails.getAttribute("aria-disabled") === "true" &&
                !!exportedNfsDetails && !exportedNfsDetails.hasAttribute("open") && exportedNfsDetails.getAttribute("aria-disabled") === "true" &&
                !!exportedRocBody && !exportedRocBody.querySelector("[data-copy-value]") &&
                !!exportedNfsBody && !exportedNfsBody.querySelector("[data-copy-value]"),
            "The exported Field Support Directory retained a selection, open Field Group, or copyable contact."
        ));

        var speed = parsed.getElementById("speedInput");
        var profile = parsed.getElementById("interfaceProfile");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Calculator",
            "Calculator defaults are preserved",
            !!speed && String(speed.value) === "200" && !!profile && profile.value === "auto",
            "Speed/CIR should reopen at 200 and Service / Interface Type should reopen at Standard Service."
        ));

        var dynamicContainers = ["nidInputFields", "cerInputFields", "cesInputFields", "selfTestResults"];
        var populatedContainers = dynamicContainers.filter(function (id) {
            var node = parsed.getElementById(id);
            return !!node && node.children.length > 0;
        });
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Runtime State",
            "Temporary generated controls and test results are removed",
            populatedContainers.length === 0,
            populatedContainers.length ? "Runtime content remains in: " + populatedContainers.join(", ") + "." : "Runtime content remains."
        ));

        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Runtime State",
            "Exported HTML excludes the manual-copy overlay",
            !parsed.getElementById("manualCopyFallback"),
            "The temporary manual-copy overlay remains in the generated HTML."
        ));

        var nidOutput = parsed.getElementById("nidCommandOutput");
        var cerOutput = parsed.getElementById("cerCommandOutput");
        var cesOutput = parsed.getElementById("cesCommandOutput");
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Command Builders",
            "Generated command outputs are reset",
            !!nidOutput && nidOutput.textContent.trim() === "show system" &&
                !!cerOutput && cerOutput.textContent.trim() === "Enter Circuit ID above" &&
                !!cesOutput && cesOutput.textContent.trim() === "show system uptime",
            "A temporary NID, CER, or CES command may still be present in the generated code."
        ));

        var cleanOutputRuntime = [
            "nidJunosControls", "cerJunosControls", "nidOutputDetailWrap", "cerOutputDetailWrap",
            "nidOutputFilterWrap", "cerOutputFilterWrap", "nidJunosModeGuide", "cerJunosModeGuide", "cesJunosModeGuide",
            "nidRefreshStopNote", "cerRefreshStopNote", "nidDeviceSequenceControls", "nidCommandSequenceControls"
        ].every(function (id) {
            var node = parsed.getElementById(id);
            return !!node && node.hasAttribute("hidden");
        });
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Command Builders",
            "Device-aware detail, filter, and mode controls reset cleanly",
            cleanOutputRuntime,
            "One or more device-aware runtime controls remain active in the exported HTML."
        ));

        var seen = {};
        var duplicates = [];
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(parsed.querySelectorAll("[id]"), function (node) {
            if (seen[node.id]) duplicates.push(node.id);
            seen[node.id] = true;
        });
        checks.push(exportCheck(
            "Document",
            "No duplicate element IDs",
            duplicates.length === 0,
            duplicates.length ? "Duplicate IDs: " + duplicates.join(", ") + "." : "Duplicate element IDs were found."
        ));

        var failed = checks.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; });
        return { ok: failed.length === 0, checks: checks, failed: failed };
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_EXPORT = Object.freeze({
        buildUpdatedHtml: buildUpdatedHtml,
        validateGeneratedHtml: validateGeneratedHtml
    });

    function runSelfTest() {
        var results = [];

        function test(section, name, fn, failureDetail) {
            try {
                var outcome = fn();
                if (outcome && typeof outcome === "object" && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(outcome, "pass")) {
                    results.push({
                        section: section,
                        name: name,
                        pass: !!outcome.pass,
                        detail: outcome.pass ? "" : (outcome.detail || failureDetail || "Check failed.")
                    });
                } else {
                    results.push({
                        section: section,
                        name: name,
                        pass: !!outcome,
                        detail: outcome ? "" : (failureDetail || "Check failed.")
                    });
                }
            } catch (error) {
                results.push({
                    section: section,
                    name: name,
                    pass: false,
                    detail: error && error.message ? error.message : (failureDetail || "Unexpected error.")
                });
            }
        }

        function exerciseTrackerEntry(field, values) {
            if (!field) return false;
            var previousValue = field.value;
            var accepted = true;
            try {
                values.forEach(function (value) {
                    field.value = value;
                    field.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
                    field.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                    if (field.value !== value) accepted = false;
                });
            } finally {
                field.value = previousValue;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
            return accepted;
        }

        function captureCopiedNotes(values) {
            var fieldIds = [
                "callerName", "itCompany", "accountNum", "callbackNum", "email",
                "address", "issue", "actions", "nextStep"
            ];
            var savedValues = {};
            var captured = null;
            var originalForceCopy = window.forceCopy;
            var originalShowStatus = window.showStatus;

            fieldIds.forEach(function (id) {
                var field = document.getElementById(id);
                if (!field) return;
                savedValues[id] = field.value;
                field.value = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(values, id) ? values[id] : "";
            });

            try {
                window.forceCopy = function (text) {
                    captured = String(text);
                    return {
                        then: function (callback) {
                            callback(true);
                            return this;
                        }
                    };
                };
                window.showStatus = function () {};
                copyNotes();
            } finally {
                window.forceCopy = originalForceCopy;
                window.showStatus = originalShowStatus;
                fieldIds.forEach(function (id) {
                    var field = document.getElementById(id);
                    if (field && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(savedValues, id)) field.value = savedValues[id];
                });
                updateAccountNumCount();
            }

            return captured;
        }

        function trackerFieldsFitNarrowWidth(width) {
            var issueCard = document.querySelector(".card.issue");
            var actionsCard = document.querySelector(".card.actions");
            var nextStepCard = document.querySelector(".card.next-step");
            if (!issueCard || !actionsCard || !nextStepCard) return false;

            var probe = document.createElement("div");
            probe.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
            probe.style.cssText = "position:absolute;left:-10000px;top:0;width:" + width + "px;max-width:" + width + "px;visibility:hidden;overflow:visible;";
            probe.appendChild(issueCard.cloneNode(true));
            probe.appendChild(actionsCard.cloneNode(true));
            probe.appendChild(nextStepCard.cloneNode(true));
            document.body.appendChild(probe);

            var fields = probe.querySelectorAll("input, textarea, select");
            var fits = probe.scrollWidth <= probe.clientWidth + 1 && Array.prototype.every.call(fields, function (field) {
                var fieldRect = field.getBoundingClientRect();
                var probeRect = probe.getBoundingClientRect();
                return fieldRect.left >= probeRect.left - 1 && fieldRect.right <= probeRect.right + 1;
            });
            document.body.removeChild(probe);
            return fits;
        }

        function trackerWorkflowSizingIsUniform(width) {
            var cards = [
                document.querySelector(".card.issue"),
                document.querySelector(".card.actions"),
                document.querySelector(".card.next-step")
            ];
            if (cards.some(function (card) { return !card; })) return false;

            var probe = document.createElement("div");
            probe.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
            probe.style.cssText = "position:absolute;left:-10000px;top:0;width:" + width + "px;max-width:" + width + "px;visibility:hidden;overflow:visible;";
            cards.forEach(function (card) {
                var clone = card.cloneNode(true);
                var field = clone.querySelector("#issue, #actions, #nextStep");
                if (field) {
                    field.removeAttribute("style");
                    field.value = "";
                }
                probe.appendChild(clone);
            });
            document.body.appendChild(probe);

            var fields = probe.querySelectorAll("#issue, #actions, #nextStep");
            var probeCards = probe.querySelectorAll(".card");
            var fieldHeights = Array.prototype.map.call(fields, function (field) { return field.getBoundingClientRect().height; });
            var labelsFit = Array.prototype.every.call(probe.querySelectorAll(".zone-label"), function (label) {
                return label.scrollWidth <= label.clientWidth + 3 && label.scrollHeight <= label.clientHeight + 3;
            });
            var uniform = fields.length === 3 && probeCards.length === 3 &&
                Math.max.apply(null, fieldHeights) - Math.min.apply(null, fieldHeights) <= 2 &&
                Math.min.apply(null, fieldHeights) >= 88 && labelsFit;
            document.body.removeChild(probe);
            return uniform;
        }

        test("Next Step", "Multiline field and all five presets are available", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("nextStep");
            var preset = document.getElementById("nextStepPreset");
            var expected = [
                "Pending Technician Visit",
                "Pending Engineer Response",
                "Pending Layer 1 Troubleshooting",
                "Pending Resolution Confirmation",
                "Pending Customer Response"
            ];
            var values = preset ? Array.prototype.map.call(preset.options, function (option) {
                return option.value;
            }).filter(Boolean) : [];
            return !!field && field.tagName === "TEXTAREA" &&
                !!preset && preset.tagName === "SELECT" &&
                values.join("\n") === expected.join("\n");
        }, "The multiline Next Step field or exact five approved presets are missing.");

        test("Next Step", "Preset fills an empty field and resets the selector", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("nextStep");
            var preset = document.getElementById("nextStepPreset");
            if (!field || !preset) return false;
            var oldValue = field.value;
            var oldPreset = preset.value;
            try {
                field.value = "";
                delete field._focusNotesNextStepSelectionStart;
                delete field._focusNotesNextStepSelectionEnd;
                preset.value = "Pending Technician Visit";
                preset.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                return field.value === "Pending Technician Visit" && preset.value === "";
            } finally {
                field.value = oldValue;
                preset.value = oldPreset;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
        }, "Selecting a Next Step preset did not fill an empty field or reset the selector.");

        test("Next Step", "Preset preserves existing multiline notes instead of overwriting them", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("nextStep");
            var preset = document.getElementById("nextStepPreset");
            if (!field || !preset) return false;
            var oldValue = field.value;
            var oldPreset = preset.value;
            try {
                field.value = "Customer maintenance window is tonight.";
                field._focusNotesNextStepSelectionStart = field.value.length;
                field._focusNotesNextStepSelectionEnd = field.value.length;
                preset.value = "Pending Customer Response";
                preset.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                return field.value === "Customer maintenance window is tonight.\nPending Customer Response" && preset.value === "";
            } finally {
                field.value = oldValue;
                preset.value = oldPreset;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
        }, "Selecting a Next Step preset overwrote existing notes or failed to insert on a new line.");

        test("Next Step", "Custom multiline text is retained", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("nextStep");
            return exerciseTrackerEntry(field, [
                "Pending Engineer Response\nEscalation sent at 14:10.",
                "Waiting for customer maintenance window\nFollow up tomorrow morning."
            ]);
        }, "The Next Step textarea did not retain directly typed multiline notes.");

        test("Next Step", "Copy Notes preserves multiline text", function () {
            return captureCopiedNotes({
                nextStep: "Pending Customer Response\nFollow up tomorrow morning."
            }) === "NEXT STEP: Pending Customer Response\nFollow up tomorrow morning.";
        }, "Copy Notes did not preserve multiline Next Step text.");

        test("Next Step", "Start New clearing removes text and preset state", function () {
            var fixture = document.createElement("div");
            fixture.innerHTML = '<textarea id="nextStep">Pending Technician Visit</textarea><select id="nextStepPreset"><option value=""></option><option value="Pending Customer Response" selected>Pending Customer Response</option></select>';
            clearTemporaryFieldValues(fixture);
            return fixture.querySelector("#nextStep").value === "" && fixture.querySelector("#nextStepPreset").value === "";
        }, "The Start New clearing path did not clear Next Step or reset its preset selector.");

        test("Issue", "Multiline field and all nine presets are available", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("issue");
            var preset = document.getElementById("issuePreset");
            var expected = [
                "No Service",
                "Intermittent Service",
                "Slow Performance",
                "Packet Loss / Latency",
                "Port / Interface Down",
                "Errors / Drops",
                "Configuration / VLAN Issue",
                "Equipment Issue",
                "Customer Requesting Ticket Update"
            ];
            var values = preset ? Array.prototype.map.call(preset.options, function (option) {
                return option.value;
            }).filter(Boolean) : [];
            return !!field && field.tagName === "TEXTAREA" &&
                !!preset && preset.tagName === "SELECT" &&
                values.join("\n") === expected.join("\n");
        }, "The multiline Issue field or exact nine approved Issue presets are missing.");

        test("Issue", "Preset fills an empty Issue field and resets the selector", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("issue");
            var preset = document.getElementById("issuePreset");
            if (!field || !preset) return false;
            var oldValue = field.value;
            var oldPreset = preset.value;
            try {
                field.value = "";
                delete field._focusNotesIssueSelectionStart;
                delete field._focusNotesIssueSelectionEnd;
                preset.value = "No Service";
                preset.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                return field.value === "No Service" && preset.value === "";
            } finally {
                field.value = oldValue;
                preset.value = oldPreset;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
        }, "Selecting a preset did not fill an empty Issue field or reset the selector.");

        test("Issue", "Preset preserves existing multiline notes instead of overwriting them", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("issue");
            var preset = document.getElementById("issuePreset");
            if (!field || !preset) return false;
            var oldValue = field.value;
            var oldPreset = preset.value;
            try {
                field.value = "Customer reports intermittent drops.";
                field._focusNotesIssueSelectionStart = field.value.length;
                field._focusNotesIssueSelectionEnd = field.value.length;
                preset.value = "Intermittent Service";
                preset.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                return field.value === "Customer reports intermittent drops.\nIntermittent Service" && preset.value === "";
            } finally {
                field.value = oldValue;
                preset.value = oldPreset;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
        }, "Selecting a preset overwrote existing Issue notes or failed to insert on a new line.");

        test("Issue", "Preset can insert at the remembered cursor without losing surrounding text", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("issue");
            var preset = document.getElementById("issuePreset");
            if (!field || !preset) return false;
            var oldValue = field.value;
            var oldPreset = preset.value;try {
                field.value = "First note\nLast note";
                field._focusNotesIssueSelectionStart = 10;
                field._focusNotesIssueSelectionEnd = 10;
                preset.value = "No Service";
                preset.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                return field.value === "First note\nNo Service\nLast note" && preset.value === "";
            } finally {
                field.value = oldValue;
                preset.value = oldPreset;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
        }, "The Issue preset did not insert at the remembered cursor cleanly.");

        test("Issue", "Preset insertion never deletes a selected range", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("issue");
            var preset = document.getElementById("issuePreset");
            if (!field || !preset) return false;
            var oldValue = field.value;
            var oldPreset = preset.value;
            try {
                field.value = "Alpha IMPORTANT Omega";
                field._focusNotesIssueSelectionStart = 6;
                field._focusNotesIssueSelectionEnd = 15;
                preset.value = "No Service";
                preset.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
                return field.value === "Alpha \nNo Service\nIMPORTANT Omega" && preset.value === "";
            } finally {
                field.value = oldValue;
                preset.value = oldPreset;
                resizeAutoGrowTextarea(field, false);
            }
        }, "Selecting an Issue preset deleted or replaced highlighted Issue text.");

        test("Issue", "Custom multiline Issue text is retained", function () {
            var field = document.getElementById("issue");
            return exerciseTrackerEntry(field, [
                "No Service\nCustomer reports all locations impacted.\nStarted after maintenance.",
                "BGP session resets every few minutes\nObserved at 10:15 and 10:22."
            ]);
        }, "The Issue textarea did not retain directly typed multiline notes.");

        test("Issue", "Copy Notes preserves multiline Issue text", function () {
            return captureCopiedNotes({
                issue: "Port / Interface Down\nCustomer reports link light off."
            }) === "ISSUE: Port / Interface Down\nCustomer reports link light off.";
        }, "Copy Notes did not preserve multiline Issue text.");

        test("Issue", "Start New clearing removes Issue text and preset state", function () {
            var fixture = document.createElement("div");
            fixture.innerHTML = '<textarea id="issue">Equipment Issue</textarea><select id="issuePreset"><option value=""></option><option value="No Service" selected>No Service</option></select>';
            clearTemporaryFieldValues(fixture);
            return fixture.querySelector("#issue").value === "" && fixture.querySelector("#issuePreset").value === "";
        }, "The Start New clearing path did not clear Issue or reset the Issue preset selector.");

        test("Tracker Fields", "Issue and Next Step preset-plus-multiline controls remain keyboard-operable", function () {
            var controls = [
                [document.getElementById("issue"), "TEXTAREA"],
                [document.getElementById("issuePreset"), "SELECT"],
                [document.getElementById("nextStep"), "TEXTAREA"],
                [document.getElementById("nextStepPreset"), "SELECT"]
            ];
            var previousActive = document.activeElement;
            var pass = controls.every(function (entry) {
                var field = entry[0];
                if (!field || field.tagName !== entry[1] || field.disabled || field.tabIndex < 0) return false;
                try {
                    field.focus({ preventScroll: true });
                } catch (error) {
                    field.focus();
                }
                return document.activeElement === field;
            });
            if (previousActive && typeof previousActive.focus === "function") {
                try {
                    previousActive.focus({ preventScroll: true });
                } catch (error) {
                    previousActive.focus();
                }
            }
            return pass;
        }, "An Issue or Next Step preset-plus-multiline control is not reachable as a native keyboard control.");

        test("Narrow Layout", "Tracker combo fields fit at 320px without horizontal overflow", function () {
            return trackerFieldsFitNarrowWidth(320);
        }, "Issue, Troubleshooting, or Next Step overflows a narrow SecureCRT-width layout.");

        test("Tracker Fields", "Issue, Troubleshooting, and Next Step primary entry boxes use uniform large sizing", function () {
            return trackerWorkflowSizingIsUniform(320);
        }, "The three primary tracker entry boxes are not uniformly sized, fully visible, and large enough to scan quickly.");

        test("Notes", "Existing Copy Notes line order and separators remain intact", function () {
            var copied = captureCopiedNotes({
                callerName: "Alex Smith",
                issue: "No Service",
                actions: "Checked handoff",
                nextStep: "Pending Technician Visit"
            });
            return copied === "CALLER: Alex Smith\n-------------------\nISSUE: No Service\n-------------------\nACTIONS: Checked handoff\n-------------------\nNEXT STEP: Pending Technician Visit";
        }, "Existing Copy Notes formatting or line order changed.");

        test("Command Builders", "NID, CER, and CES builders are available", function () {
            return !!(window.COMMAND_DB && window.COMMAND_DB.nid && window.COMMAND_DB.cer && window.COMMAND_DB.ces &&
                document.getElementById("nidCommandOutput") && document.getElementById("cerCommandOutput") && document.getElementById("cesCommandOutput"));
        }, "The NID, CER, or CES command database/output is missing.");

        test("Command Integrity", "Every command placeholder has an input field", function () {
            return ["nid", "ces", "cer"].every(function (family) {
                return Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices).every(function (deviceName) {
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        var placeholders = Array.from(String(item.cmd || "").matchAll(/\{([A-Z0-9_]+)\}/g)).map(function (m) { return m[1]; });
                        var fields = (item.fields || []).map(function (field) { return field.key; });
                        return placeholders.every(function (key) { return fields.indexOf(key) !== -1; });
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "At least one generated command has a variable with no input field.");

        test("Command Integrity", "Pipe filters are complete", function () {
            var incomplete = /\|\s*(?:match|include|exclude|except|grep|i|find|begin|count)?\s*$/i;
            return ["nid", "ces", "cer"].every(function (family) {
                return Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices).every(function (deviceName) {
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        return !incomplete.test(String(item.cmd || "").trim());
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "At least one command still ends with an incomplete pipe/filter operator.");


        test("Command Integrity", "Worked-example placeholders are not executable command text", function () {
            var rawExample = /(?:\bx(?:\/x){2,4}\b|\bxxxx\b|\bsrvid\b)/i;
            return ["nid", "ces", "cer"].every(function (family) {
                return Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices).every(function (deviceName) {
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        return !rawExample.test(String(item.cmd || ""));
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "A worked-example placeholder is still embedded directly in a command.");

        test("Command Integrity", "Command templates have balanced quotes", function () {
            return ["nid", "ces", "cer"].every(function (family) {
                return Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices).every(function (deviceName) {
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        var command = String(item.cmd || "");
                        return (command.match(/"/g) || []).length % 2 === 0;
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "At least one command still contains a truncated quoted value.");

        test("Command Integrity", "Source and selector continuations are complete", function () {
            var incompleteEnd = /\bsource\s*$/i;
            var knownMissingArgument = /^(?:show arp no-resolve interface|show ipv6 neighbors interface|show route output interface|show bridge domain interface|show vpls connections instance|show vpls mac-table instance|show vpls mac-table interface|show policy-map interface|show running-config interface|show l2vpn xconnect interface|show lldp neighbors interface|show ethernet-switching table vlan-id|info configure qos interface)$/i;
            return ["nid", "ces", "cer"].every(function (family) {
                return Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices).every(function (deviceName) {
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        var command = String(item.cmd || "").trim();
                        return !incompleteEnd.test(command) && !knownMissingArgument.test(command);
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "At least one command still ends before a required selector/value.");


        test("Command Integrity", "No generic VALUE placeholder remains", function () {
            return ["nid","ces","cer"].every(function (family) {
                return Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices).every(function (deviceName) {
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        return String(item.cmd || "").indexOf("{VALUE}") === -1 &&
                            !(item.fields || []).some(function (field) { return field.key === "VALUE"; });
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "A generic VALUE placeholder still remains.");

        test("RAD ETX-2", "Port syntax uses eth and never ethernet", function () {

        test("CER Juniper", "Circuit lookup and gateway helpers remain available", function () {

        test("Command Integrity", "No exact duplicate command appears twice in one workflow group", function () {
            return ["nid", "ces", "cer"].every(function (family) {

                    var seen = Object.create(null);
                    return window.COMMAND_DB[family].devices[deviceName].tasks.every(function (item) {
                        var key = String(item.group || "") + "\n" + String(item.cmd || "").trim();
                        if (seen[key]) return false;
                        seen[key] = true;
                        return true;
                    });
                });
            });
        }, "An exact duplicate command remains inside the same workflow group.");

        test("Command Builders", "Whole builder headers are clickable with centered labels", function () {
            return ["cer", "ces", "nid"].every(function (prefix) {
                var panel = document.getElementById(prefix + "CommandBuilder");
                var header = panel ? panel.querySelector(":scope > .header") : null;
                var toggle = document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderToggle");
                var heading = header ? header.querySelector("h2") : null;
                if (!header || !toggle || !heading) return false;
                var headerRect = header.getBoundingClientRect();
                var toggleRect = toggle.getBoundingClientRect();
                var headingRect = heading.getBoundingClientRect();
                var disclosure = window.getComputedStyle(header, "::after");
                var fillsHeader = Math.abs(headerRect.left - toggleRect.left) <= 1 &&
                    Math.abs(headerRect.top - toggleRect.top) <= 1 &&
                    Math.abs(headerRect.width - toggleRect.width) <= 1 &&
                    Math.abs(headerRect.height - toggleRect.height) <= 1;
                var centered = Math.abs((headingRect.left + headingRect.width / 2) - (headerRect.left + headerRect.width / 2)) <= 1.5 &&
                    Math.abs((headingRect.top + headingRect.height / 2) - (headerRect.top + headerRect.height / 2)) <= 1.5;
                var hasVisibleDisclosure = !!disclosure && disclosure.content !== "none" &&
                    disclosure.borderRightWidth !== "0px" && disclosure.borderBottomWidth !== "0px";
                return fillsHeader && centered && hasVisibleDisclosure && /commands$/i.test(toggle.getAttribute("aria-label") || "");
            });
        }, "A builder header is not a full click target, has no clear disclosure cue, or its label is not centered.");

        test("Diagnostics", "Self-test diagnostic copy control is available", function () {
            var button = document.getElementById("copySelfTestDiagnosticsButton");
            var api = window.FOCUS_NOTES_DIAGNOSTICS;
            var runtime = window.FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS;
            return !!button && !!api && typeof api.buildReport === "function" && typeof api.copy === "function" &&
                !!runtime && typeof runtime.list === "function";
        }, "Diagnostic copy support is missing or incomplete.");

        test("Command Readiness", "Missing and invalid required values remain blocked", function () {
            if (typeof window.validateTerminalValue !== "function") return false;
            var missing = window.validateTerminalValue("VLAN", "");
            var invalid = window.validateTerminalValue("VLAN", "4095");
            var ready = window.validateTerminalValue("VLAN", "100");
            return !missing.ok && missing.empty === true &&
                !invalid.ok && invalid.empty === false &&
                ready.ok === true && ready.value === "100";
        }, "Required-value validation no longer distinguishes missing, invalid, and ready commands.");

        test("Command Readiness", "No approval state is exposed or stored", function () {

        test("Command Library", "Fiber NID&MTU device list matches the expected order", function () {
            var expected = ["Adva 114","Adva 114 PRO","ADVA 116","ADVA 120","Adva 404","Adva 825","ADVA XG108","ADVA GO102","Ciena 5171","ME3400","ME3600","ASR920","Juniper EX","NOKIA 7210","NOKIA 7705","RAD ETX-2"].join("|");
            var actual = Object.keys(window.COMMAND_DB.nid.devices).join("|");

        test("Command Library", "Command placeholders generate separate input fields", function () {
            var task = window.COMMAND_DB.nid.devices["Ciena 5171"].tasks.find(function (item) {

            var keys = task ? task.fields.map(function (field) { return field.key; }) : [];
            return keys.length === 2 && keys.indexOf("DATE") !== -1 && keys.indexOf("FILTER") !== -1;
        }, "A command with two placeholders did not generate both input fields.");
        test("Terminal Safety", "Unsafe command characters are blocked", function () {
            var validator = window.validateTerminalValue;
            return typeof validator === "function" && validator("PORT", "ge-0/0/1").ok && !validator("PORT", "ge-0/0/1|match").ok;
        }, "The Port validator did not correctly accept a normal interface or block a pipe character.");

        test("Terminal Safety", "VLAN allows only 1 through 4094", function () {
            var validator = window.validateTerminalValue;
            return typeof validator === "function" && validator("VLAN", "1").ok && validator("VLAN", "4094").ok && !validator("VLAN", "0").ok && !validator("VLAN", "4095").ok;
        }, "VLAN boundaries are not being enforced correctly.");

        test("Case Values", "Remembered values stay in memory and expose a defensive snapshot", function () {
            var api = window.FOCUS_NOTES_CASE_MEMORY;
            if (!api || typeof api.clear !== "function" || typeof api.snapshot !== "function") return false;
            var before = JSON.stringify(api.snapshot());
            var copy = api.snapshot();
            copy.__selfTestMutation = { PORT: "must-not-leak" };
            var after = JSON.stringify(api.snapshot());
            var resetSource = typeof resetForm === "function" ? resetForm.toString() : "";
            return before === after && resetSource.indexOf("FOCUS_NOTES_CASE_MEMORY.clear") !== -1 &&
                resetSource.indexOf("resetCesCommandBuilder") !== -1;
        }, "Case memory is unavailable, externally mutable, or not connected to Start New.");

        test("Cisco Filters", "Safe Cisco filters are scoped to eligible show commands", function () {
            var api = window.FOCUS_NOTES_CISCO_FILTERS;
            return !!api &&
                api.operators.join("|") === "include|exclude|begin|section" &&
                api.isEligible("ASR920", "show interfaces Te0/0/5") &&
                api.isEligible("ME3400", "show vlan brief") &&
                api.isEligible("ME3600", "show policy-map interface Te0/1") &&
                !api.isEligible("Juniper EX", "show route") &&
                !api.isEligible("ASR920", "clear counters") &&
                !api.isEligible("ASR920", "show interfaces | include up") &&
                !api.isEligible("ME3600", "show version\nshow inventory");
        }, "Cisco filter eligibility is missing, unsafe, or too broad.");

        test("Cisco Filters", "Cisco filter syntax and safety validation are correct", function () {
            var api = window.FOCUS_NOTES_CISCO_FILTERS;
            if (!api) return false;
            var include = api.build("show interfaces", "include", "line protocol is up");
            var exclude = api.build("show logging", "exclude", "LINK-3-UPDOWN");
            var begin = api.build("show running-config", "begin", "interface Te0/0/5");
            var section = api.build("show running-config", "section", "^interface");
            var alternation = api.build("show interfaces status", "include", "up|down");
            var chained = api.build("show version", "include", "IOS | tee flash:test.txt");
            return include.ok && include.command === "show interfaces | include line protocol is up" &&
                exclude.ok && exclude.command === "show logging | exclude LINK-3-UPDOWN" &&
                begin.ok && begin.command === "show running-config | begin interface Te0/0/5" &&
                section.ok && section.command === "show running-config | section ^interface" &&
                alternation.ok && alternation.command === "show interfaces status | include up|down" &&
                !chained.ok && !api.validate("include", "").ok;
        }, "Cisco filter composition or injection blocking is incorrect.");

                hold.ok && hold.command === "show log messages | hold" &&

            var previousValue = field.value;
            var liveValue = "";
            try {
                field.value = "granite telecommunications";
                field.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
                liveValue = field.value;

                field.value = previousValue;

                liveValue === "Granite Telecommunications" &&

        test("Reset", "Expandable sections collapse without a runtime error", function () {
            var targets = [
                ["cerCommandBuilder", "cerBuilderBody", "cerBuilderToggle", "is-open"],
                ["cesCommandBuilder", "cesBuilderBody", "cesBuilderToggle", "is-open"],
                ["nidCommandBuilder", "nidBuilderBody", "nidBuilderToggle", "is-open"],
                ["selfTestCard", "selfTestBody", "selfTestToggle", "is-expanded"]
            ];
            var original = targets.map(function (target) {
                var panel = target[0] ? document.getElementById(target[0]) : null;
                var body = document.getElementById(target[1]);
                var toggle = document.getElementById(target[2]);
                return {
                    panel: panel,
                    body: body,
                    toggle: toggle,
                    panelClass: panel && target[3] ? panel.classList.contains(target[3]) : false,
                    bodyHidden: body ? body.hidden : true,
                    toggleExpanded: toggle ? toggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") : null,
                    toggleText: toggle ? toggle.textContent : ""
                };
            });

            var errorMessage = "";
            var collapsed = false;
            try {
                targets.forEach(function (target) {
                    var panel = target[0] ? document.getElementById(target[0]) : null;
                    var body = document.getElementById(target[1]);
                    var toggle = document.getElementById(target[2]);
                    if (panel && target[3]) panel.classList.add(target[3]);
                    if (body) body.hidden = false;
                    if (toggle) {
                        toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
                        toggle.textContent = "Hide";
                    }
                });
                collapseExpandableSections();
                collapsed = targets.every(function (target) {
                    var panel = target[0] ? document.getElementById(target[0]) : null;
                    var body = document.getElementById(target[1]);
                    var toggle = document.getElementById(target[2]);
                    return (!panel || !target[3] || !panel.classList.contains(target[3])) &&
                        (!body || body.hidden) &&
                        (!toggle || toggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") === "false");
                });
            } catch (error) {
                errorMessage = error && error.message ? error.message : String(error);
            } finally {
                original.forEach(function (state, index) {
                    var target = targets[index];
                    if (state.panel && target[3]) state.panel.classList.toggle(target[3], state.panelClass);
                    if (state.body) state.body.hidden = state.bodyHidden;
                    if (state.toggle) {
                        if (state.toggleExpanded == null) state.toggle.removeAttribute("aria-expanded");
                        else state.toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", state.toggleExpanded);
                        state.toggle.textContent = state.toggleText;
                    }
                });
            }
            return {
                pass: !errorMessage && collapsed,
                detail: errorMessage || (collapsed ? "" : "One or more expandable sections stayed open.")
            };
        }, "Start New section-collapse behavior threw an error or left a section open.");

        test("Fixed Actions", "Start New and Copy Notes stay fixed with full native hit targets", function () {
            var row = document.querySelector(".btn-row");
            var resetButton = document.querySelector('[data-action="reset-form"]');
            var copyButton = document.querySelector('[data-action="copy-notes"]');
            if (!row || !resetButton || !copyButton) return false;
            var rowStyle = window.getComputedStyle(row);
            var resetStyle = window.getComputedStyle(resetButton);
            var copyStyle = window.getComputedStyle(copyButton);
            var resetRect = resetButton.getBoundingClientRect();
            var copyRect = copyButton.getBoundingClientRect();

            function nativeHitTargetAt(button, x, y) {
                var hit = document.elementFromPoint(x, y);
                return hit === button || button.contains(hit);
            }

            var hitInset = 8;
            var resetHits = [
                [resetRect.left + hitInset, resetRect.top + hitInset],
                [resetRect.right - hitInset, resetRect.top + hitInset],
                [resetRect.left + resetRect.width / 2, resetRect.top + resetRect.height / 2],
                [resetRect.left + hitInset, resetRect.bottom - hitInset],
                [resetRect.right - hitInset, resetRect.bottom - hitInset]
            ].every(function (point) { return nativeHitTargetAt(resetButton, point[0], point[1]); });
            var copyHits = [
                [copyRect.left + hitInset, copyRect.top + hitInset],
                [copyRect.right - hitInset, copyRect.top + hitInset],
                [copyRect.left + copyRect.width / 2, copyRect.top + copyRect.height / 2],
                [copyRect.left + hitInset, copyRect.bottom - hitInset],
                [copyRect.right - hitInset, copyRect.bottom - hitInset]
            ].every(function (point) { return nativeHitTargetAt(copyButton, point[0], point[1]); });

            return rowStyle.position === "fixed" &&
                rowStyle.pointerEvents === "auto" &&
                resetStyle.pointerEvents === "auto" && copyStyle.pointerEvents === "auto" &&
                resetRect.height >= 44 && copyRect.height >= 44 &&
                rowStyle.gridTemplateColumns.split(" ").length >= 2 &&
                Math.abs(resetRect.top - copyRect.top) <= 2 &&
                row.getBoundingClientRect().bottom <= window.innerHeight + 3 &&
                resetHits && copyHits;
        }, "The permanent action buttons are not full-size native hit targets or are outside the fixed bar.");

        test("Fixed Actions", "Bottom actions use direct single-fire click bindings", function () {
            var resetButton = document.querySelector('[data-action="reset-form"]');
            var copyButton = document.querySelector('[data-action="copy-notes"]');
            var api = window.FOCUS_NOTES_BOTTOM_ACTIONS;
            if (!resetButton || !copyButton || !api || typeof api.run !== "function") return false;

            var originalReset = window.resetForm;
            var originalCopy = window.copyNotes;
            var resetCalls = 0;
            var copyCalls = 0;
            try {
                window.resetForm = function () { resetCalls += 1; };
                window.copyNotes = function () { copyCalls += 1; };
                resetButton.click();
                copyButton.click();
            } finally {
                window.resetForm = originalReset;
                window.copyNotes = originalCopy;
            }
            return resetCalls === 1 && copyCalls === 1;
        }, "Start New or Copy Notes did not fire exactly once through its direct button binding.");

        test("Fixed Actions", "Visible action-bar gaps cannot click through to content", function () {
            var row = document.querySelector(".btn-row");
            if (!row) return false;
            var rect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
            var hit = document.elementFromPoint(rect.left + rect.width / 2, rect.top + 2);
            return window.getComputedStyle(row).pointerEvents !== "none" && !!hit && (hit === row || row.contains(hit));
        }, "The fixed bottom action bar allows clicks through its visible padding or gap.");

        test("Fixed Actions", "Scroll padding keeps keyboard focus above the fixed action bar", function () {

        test("Regional Support", "Directory typography and acronym-first Field Group titles use the approved CLI treatment", function () {
            var parentSummary = document.querySelector("#regionalSupportDirectory > summary");
            var rocSummary = document.querySelector("#rocContactDetails > summary");
            var nfsSummary = document.querySelector("#nfsContactDetails > summary");
            var select = document.getElementById("regionalSupportRegion");
            var value = document.getElementById("regionalSupportManagementArea");
            if (!parentSummary || !rocSummary || !nfsSummary || !select || !value) return false;
            var parentStyle = window.getComputedStyle(parentSummary);
            var selectStyle = window.getComputedStyle(select);
            var valueStyle = window.getComputedStyle(value);
            return /ROC\s*·\s*Regional Operations Centers/.test(rocSummary.textContent) &&
                /NFS\s*·\s*National Field Support/.test(nfsSummary.textContent) &&
                parentStyle.fontFamily.toLowerCase().indexOf("mono") !== -1 &&
                parentStyle.fontVariantLigatures === "none" &&
                parseFloat(parentStyle.letterSpacing) >= 0.5 &&
                selectStyle.fontFamily.toLowerCase().indexOf("mono") !== -1 &&

        }, "The directory lost its CLI typography, controlled chevrons, or acronym-first Field Group titles.");

        }, "Duplicate element IDs were found.");

        test("Accessibility", "A single main landmark contains the page heading", function () {
