        test("Accessibility", "Workflow section headings keep readable focused-state contrast", function () {
            function parseRgb(value) {
                var match = String(value || "").match(/rgba?\((\d+(?:\.\d+)?)[,\s]+(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)[,\s]+(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
                return match ? [Number(match[1]), Number(match[2]), Number(match[3])] : null;
            }
            function luminance(rgb) {
                return rgb.map(function (channel) {
                    var value = channel / 255;
                    return value <= 0.04045 ? value / 12.92 : Math.pow((value + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
                }).reduce(function (sum, value, index) {
                    return sum + value * [0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722][index];
                }, 0);
            }
            function ratio(foreground, background) {
                var fg = parseRgb(foreground);
                var bg = parseRgb(background);
                if (!fg || !bg) return 0;
                var a = luminance(fg);
                var b = luminance(bg);
                return (Math.max(a, b) + 0.05) / (Math.min(a, b) + 0.05);
            }
            var pairs = [
                [".card.identity", "#callerName"],
                [".card.issue", "#issue"],
                [".card.actions", "#actions"],
                [".card.next-step", "#nextStep"]
            ];
            var previousActive = document.activeElement;
            var pass = pairs.every(function (pair) {
                var card = document.querySelector(pair[0]);
                var field = document.querySelector(pair[1]);
                if (!card || !field) return false;
                try { field.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (error) { field.focus(); }
                var label = card.querySelector(".zone-label");
                return !!label && ratio(window.getComputedStyle(label).color, window.getComputedStyle(card).backgroundColor) >= 4.5;
            });
            if (previousActive && typeof previousActive.focus === "function") {
                try { previousActive.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (error) { previousActive.focus(); }
            }
            return pass;
        }, "A workflow section heading falls below 4.5:1 contrast in its focused card state.");

        test("Clipboard", "Compatibility copy fallback is available", function () {
            return typeof fallbackCopy === "function" && typeof copyText === "function";
        }, "The clipboard compatibility fallback is unavailable.");

        test("Privacy", "No browser storage is used", function () {
            var storageNames = ["local" + "Storage", "session" + "Storage", "indexed" + "DB"];
            var source = document.documentElement.outerHTML;
            return storageNames.every(function (name) { return source.indexOf(name) === -1; });
        }, "A browser-storage API name was found in the HTML source.");

        test("Address", "Ambiguous city and street-suffix names format correctly", function () {
            var cases = [
                ["500 Main St Temple Terrace FL 33617", "500 Main St, Temple Terrace, FL 33617"],
                ["500 Main St Green Cove Springs FL 32043", "500 Main St, Green Cove Springs, FL 32043"],
                ["500 Palm Cove Loop Orlando FL 32819", "500 Palm Cove Loop, Orlando, FL 32819"],
                ["242 Near Water Dr Maitland FL 32751", "242 Near Water Dr, Maitland, FL 32751"],
                ["500 Terrace Dr Temple Terrace FL 33617", "500 Terrace Dr, Temple Terrace, FL 33617"],
                ["123 State Road 50 Clermont FL 34711", "123 State Rd 50, Clermont, FL 34711"],
                ["242 N Orlando Ave Apt 4 Maitland FL 32751 407-555-1212", "242 N Orlando Ave, Maitland, FL 32751"],
                ["1600 Pennsylvania Avenue Northwest Washington DC 20500", "1600 Pennsylvania Ave NW, Washington, DC 20500"]
            ];
            var failures = cases.filter(function (item) {
                return normalizeServiceAddress(item[0]) !== item[1];
            });
            return {
                pass: failures.length === 0,
                detail: failures.length ? "Failed: " + failures.map(function (item) { return item[0]; }).join(" | ") : ""
            };
        }, "One or more address regression cases failed.");

        var exportReport = validateGeneratedHtml(buildUpdatedHtml());
        exportReport.checks.forEach(function (check) {
            results.push({
                section: "Export · " + check.section,
                name: check.name,
                pass: check.pass,
                detail: check.detail
            });
        });

        var container = document.getElementById("selfTestResults");
        if (!container) return;
        container.innerHTML = "";
        var passed = 0;
        results.forEach(function (result) {
            if (result.pass) passed += 1;

            var row = document.createElement("div");
            row.className = "self-test-row " + (result.pass ? "pass" : "fail");

            var icon = document.createElement("span");
            icon.className = "self-test-icon";
            icon.textContent = result.pass ? "✓" : "✕";

            var copy = document.createElement("div");
            copy.className = "self-test-copy";

            var section = document.createElement("span");
            section.className = "self-test-section";
            section.textContent = result.section;

            var name = document.createElement("span");
            name.className = "self-test-name";
            name.textContent = result.name;

            copy.appendChild(section);
            copy.appendChild(name);

            if (!result.pass && result.detail) {
                var detail = document.createElement("span");
                detail.className = "self-test-detail";
                detail.textContent = result.detail;
                copy.appendChild(detail);
            }

            row.appendChild(icon);
            row.appendChild(copy);
            container.appendChild(row);
        });

        var summary = document.createElement("div");
        summary.className = "self-test-summary";
        summary.textContent = passed + " of " + results.length + " checks passed." + (passed === results.length ? " Tool checks are clear." : " Review the failed section(s) above.");
        container.appendChild(summary);

        return {
            passed: passed,
            total: results.length,
            summary: summary.textContent,
            results: results.slice()
        };
    }

    var DIAGNOSTIC_VERSION = "1";
    var DIAGNOSTIC_BUILD_MARKER = "FN-" + APP_VERSION + "-D1";

    function redactDiagnosticReport(text) {
        var output = String(text == null ? "" : text);
        ["callerName", "itCompany", "accountNum", "callbackNum", "email", "address", "issue", "actions", "nextStep"].forEach(function (id) {
            var field = document.getElementById(id);
            var value = field && typeof field.value === "string" ? field.value.trim() : "";
            if (value.length >= 3) output = output.split(value).join("[REDACTED]");
        });
        return output;
    }

    function diagnosticFailureLines(items) {
        var failures = (items || []).filter(function (item) { return item && item.pass === false; });
        if (!failures.length) return ["- None"];
        return failures.map(function (item) {
            var detail = String(item.detail || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
            return "- [FAIL] " + (item.section ? item.section + " — " : "") + item.name + (detail ? " :: " + detail : "");
        });
    }

    function diagnosticAllLines(items) {
        if (!(items || []).length) return ["- None"];
        return items.map(function (item) {
            var detail = String(item.detail || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
            return "- [" + (item.pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL") + "] " + (item.section ? item.section + " — " : "") + item.name + (detail ? " :: " + detail : "");
        });
    }

    function diagnosticCheck(name, pass, detail, section) {
        return { section: section || "Diagnostics", name: name, pass: !!pass, detail: pass ? "" : String(detail || "Check failed.") };
    }

    function browserVersionLabel() {
        var ua = String(navigator.userAgent || "");
        var edge = ua.match(/Edg\/(\d+(?:\.\d+)*)/);
        var chrome = ua.match(/Chrome\/(\d+(?:\.\d+)*)/);
        var firefox = ua.match(/Firefox\/(\d+(?:\.\d+)*)/);
        if (edge) return "Microsoft Edge " + edge[1];
        if (chrome) return "Google Chrome " + chrome[1];
        if (firefox) return "Mozilla Firefox " + firefox[1];
        return "Browser version unavailable";
    }

    function probeCalculatorStartup() {
        var speedInput = document.getElementById("speedInput");
        var profile = document.getElementById("interfaceProfile");
        var originalResult = document.getElementById("calcResult");
        if (!speedInput || !profile || !originalResult || !originalResult.parentNode || typeof window.initializePolicerCalculator !== "function") {
            return { pass: false, targetCount: 0, heights: [], detail: "Calculator controls or startup function are unavailable." };
        }

        var originalSpeed = speedInput.value;
        var originalProfile = profile.value;
        var temporaryResult = originalResult.cloneNode(false);
        originalResult.parentNode.replaceChild(temporaryResult, originalResult);
        try {
            speedInput.value = "200";
            profile.value = "auto";
            window.initializePolicerCalculator();
            var targets = Array.prototype.slice.call(temporaryResult.querySelectorAll(".calc-copy-value"));
            var heights = targets.map(function (target) {
                return Math.round(target.getBoundingClientRect().height * 10) / 10;
            });
            var pass = temporaryResult.style.display === "block" && /CIR/.test(temporaryResult.textContent) && targets.length === 6;
            return {
                pass: pass,
                targetCount: targets.length,
                heights: heights,
                detail: pass ? "" : "Generated " + targets.length + " of 6 calculator copy targets."
            };
        } catch (error) {
            return { pass: false, targetCount: 0, heights: [], detail: error && error.message ? error.message : "Calculator probe failed." };
        } finally {
            speedInput.value = originalSpeed;
            profile.value = originalProfile;
            if (temporaryResult.parentNode) temporaryResult.parentNode.replaceChild(originalResult, temporaryResult);
        }
    }

    function collectStartupDiagnostics() {
        var db = window.COMMAND_DB || {};
        var exportApi = window.FOCUS_NOTES_EXPORT;
        var runtimeApi = window.FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS;
        return [
            diagnosticCheck("Tracker", !!document.getElementById("issue") && !!document.getElementById("actions") && !!document.getElementById("nextStep"), "Tracker fields are unavailable.", "Startup"),
            (function () {
                var probe = probeCalculatorStartup();
                return diagnosticCheck("Calculator", probe.pass, probe.detail || "Calculator startup/result is unavailable.", "Startup");
            })(),
            diagnosticCheck("CER", !!db.cer && !!document.getElementById("cerBuilderToggle") && !!document.getElementById("cerBuilderBody"), "CER controls or command data are unavailable.", "Startup"),
            diagnosticCheck("CES", !!db.ces && !!document.getElementById("cesBuilderToggle") && !!document.getElementById("cesBuilderBody"), "CES controls or command data are unavailable.", "Startup"),
            diagnosticCheck("NID", !!db.nid && !!document.getElementById("nidBuilderToggle") && !!document.getElementById("nidBuilderBody"), "NID controls or command data are unavailable.", "Startup"),
            diagnosticCheck("Copy Notes", typeof window.copyNotes === "function" && typeof window.forceCopy === "function", "Copy Notes or clipboard compatibility helper is unavailable.", "Startup"),
            diagnosticCheck("Start New", typeof window.resetForm === "function", "Start New reset function is unavailable.", "Startup"),
            diagnosticCheck("Export", !!exportApi && typeof exportApi.buildUpdatedHtml === "function" && typeof exportApi.validateGeneratedHtml === "function", "Export validation API is unavailable.", "Startup"),
            diagnosticCheck("Runtime Error Capture", !!runtimeApi && typeof runtimeApi.list === "function", "Runtime error capture API is unavailable.", "Startup")
        ];
    }

    function px(value) {
        var number = Number(value);
        return isFinite(number) ? Math.round(number * 10) / 10 : 0;
    }

    function elementHeight(id) {
        var node = document.getElementById(id);
        return node ? px(node.getBoundingClientRect().height) : 0;
    }

    function configuredMinHeight(selector) {
        var node = document.querySelector(selector);
        if (!node) return 0;
        var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
        return px(parseFloat(style.minHeight) || node.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0);
    }

    function collectLayoutMeasurements() {
        var container = document.querySelector(".container");
        var actionBar = document.querySelector(".btn-row");
        var calcProbe = probeCalculatorStartup();
        var calcTargets = calcProbe.heights || [];
        var calcMin = calcTargets.length ? Math.min.apply(Math, calcTargets) : 0;
        return [
            { name: "Viewport", value: window.innerWidth + " x " + window.innerHeight + " px" },
            { name: "Page horizontal overflow", value: container ? Math.max(0, Math.round(container.scrollWidth - container.clientWidth)) + " px" : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Main content width", value: container ? px(container.getBoundingClientRect().width) + " px" : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Main content max width", value: container ? getComputedStyle(container).maxWidth : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Top focus clearance", value: container ? getComputedStyle(container).scrollPaddingTop : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Quick Scratch height", value: document.querySelector(".scratch-zone") ? px(document.querySelector(".scratch-zone").getBoundingClientRect().height) + " px" : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Caller Identity height", value: document.getElementById("callerIdentitySection") ? px(document.getElementById("callerIdentitySection").getBoundingClientRect().height) + " px" : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Issue field height", value: elementHeight("issue") + " px" },
            { name: "Troubleshooting field height", value: elementHeight("actions") + " px" },
            { name: "Action bar height", value: actionBar ? px(actionBar.getBoundingClientRect().height) + " px" : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "CER header height", value: elementHeight("cerBuilderToggle") + " px" },
            { name: "CES header height", value: elementHeight("cesBuilderToggle") + " px" },
            { name: "NID header height", value: elementHeight("nidBuilderToggle") + " px" },
            { name: "Diagnostics header height", value: document.querySelector("#selfTestCard .self-test-head") ? px(document.querySelector("#selfTestCard .self-test-head").getBoundingClientRect().height) + " px" : "Unavailable" },
            { name: "Smallest calculator copy target", value: px(calcMin) + " px" },
            { name: "CES port-reference minimum target", value: configuredMinHeight("#cesPortReference > summary") + " px" },
            { name: "NID port-reference minimum target", value: configuredMinHeight("#nidPortReference > summary") + " px" }
        ];
    }

    function snapshotBuilderState() {
        return ["cer", "ces", "nid"].map(function (prefix) {
            var body = document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderBody");
            var toggle = document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderToggle");
            return { prefix: prefix, open: !!body && !body.hidden, expanded: toggle ? toggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") : "false" };
        });
    }

    function restoreBuilderState(snapshot) {
        var accordion = window.FOCUS_NOTES_COMMAND_ACCORDION;
        if (!accordion) return false;
        accordion.collapseAll();
        (snapshot || []).forEach(function (item) {
            if (item.open) accordion.setExpanded(item.prefix + "CommandBuilder", true);
        });
        return true;
    }

    function runSafeFullChecks() {
        var checks = [];
        var accordion = window.FOCUS_NOTES_COMMAND_ACCORDION;
        var originalBuilderState = snapshotBuilderState();
        try {
            var accordionPass = !!accordion && typeof accordion.setExpanded === "function" && typeof accordion.collapseAll === "function";
            if (accordionPass) {
                ["cer", "ces", "nid"].forEach(function (prefix) {
                    accordion.collapseAll();
                    accordion.setExpanded(prefix + "CommandBuilder", true);
                    var body = document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderBody");
                    var toggle = document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderToggle");
                    if (!body || body.hidden || !toggle || toggle.getAttribute("aria-expanded") !== "true") accordionPass = false;
                });
            }
            checks.push(diagnosticCheck("CER/CES/NID accordion transitions", accordionPass, "One of the command sections did not transition correctly.", "Full"));
        } catch (error) {
            checks.push(diagnosticCheck("CER/CES/NID accordion transitions", false, error && error.message ? error.message : "Accordion check failed.", "Full"));
        } finally {
            restoreBuilderState(originalBuilderState);
        }

        try {
            var calculatorProbe = probeCalculatorStartup();
            checks.push(diagnosticCheck("Calculator isolated startup probe", calculatorProbe.pass, calculatorProbe.detail || "Calculator did not generate all six copy values in the isolated probe.", "Full"));
        } catch (error) {
            checks.push(diagnosticCheck("Calculator isolated startup probe", false, error && error.message ? error.message : "Calculator probe failed.", "Full"));
        }

        try {
            var exportApi = window.FOCUS_NOTES_EXPORT;
            var exportReport = exportApi && exportApi.validateGeneratedHtml(exportApi.buildUpdatedHtml());
            checks.push(diagnosticCheck("Export/reopen compatibility", !!exportReport && exportReport.ok === true, exportReport && exportReport.failed ? exportReport.failed.length + " export check(s) failed." : "Export validation could not run.", "Full"));
        } catch (error) {
            checks.push(diagnosticCheck("Export/reopen compatibility", false, error && error.message ? error.message : "Export validation failed.", "Full"));
        }

        checks.push(diagnosticCheck("Diagnostic controls", !!document.getElementById("runSelfTestButton") && !!document.getElementById("runFullDiagnosticsButton") && !!document.getElementById("copySelfTestDiagnosticsButton"), "One or more diagnostic controls are unavailable.", "Full"));
        checks.push(diagnosticCheck("Runtime error capture", !!window.FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS && typeof window.FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS.list === "function", "Runtime error capture is unavailable.", "Full"));
        return checks;
    }

    function getExportReport() {
        try {
            return { report: validateGeneratedHtml(buildUpdatedHtml()), error: "" };
        } catch (error) {
            return { report: null, error: error && error.message ? error.message : String(error || "Unknown export validation error") };
        }
    }

    function collectEnvironmentWarnings() {
        var warnings = [];
        if (!window.isSecureContext) warnings.push("Secure Context is unavailable; clipboard fallback may be used.");
        if (!(navigator.clipboard && typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText === "function")) warnings.push("Clipboard API is unavailable; the compatibility copy fallback is required.");
        return warnings;
    }

    function diagnosticStatus(result) {
        if (!result || !result.quick) return "ERROR";
        var quickFailed = result.quick.total - result.quick.passed;
        var startupFailed = result.startup.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }).length;
        var fullFailed = result.fullChecks.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }).length;
        var exportFailed = result.exportReport && result.exportReport.failed ? result.exportReport.failed.length : 1;
        return quickFailed === 0 && startupFailed === 0 && fullFailed === 0 && exportFailed === 0 && result.runtimeErrors.length === 0 ? "PASS" : "REVIEW";
    }

    function setDiagnosticHealth(status, text) {
        var node = document.getElementById("diagnosticHealth");
        if (!node) return;
        node.classList.remove("is-pass", "is-review", "is-error");
        node.classList.add(status === "PASS" ? "is-pass" : status === "REVIEW" ? "is-review" : "is-error");
        node.textContent = text;
    }

    function renderFullDiagnosticSummary(result) {
        var node = document.getElementById("fullDiagnosticResults");
        if (!node || !result) return;
        var startupPassed = result.startup.filter(function (item) { return item.pass; }).length;
        var fullPassed = result.fullChecks.filter(function (item) { return item.pass; }).length;
        var exportChecks = result.exportReport && result.exportReport.checks ? result.exportReport.checks : [];
        var exportPassed = exportChecks.filter(function (item) { return item.pass; }).length;
        node.textContent = "Startup " + startupPassed + "/" + result.startup.length + " · Full " + fullPassed + "/" + result.fullChecks.length + " · Export " + exportPassed + "/" + exportChecks.length + " · Runtime errors " + result.runtimeErrors.length;
    }

    function runDiagnostics() {
        var quick = runSelfTest();
        var status = quick && quick.passed === quick.total ? "PASS" : "REVIEW";
        setDiagnosticHealth(status, status + " · " + (quick ? quick.passed + "/" + quick.total : "Unable to run"));
        var extra = document.getElementById("fullDiagnosticResults");
        if (extra) extra.textContent = "";
        return quick;
    }

    function runFullDiagnostics() {
        var quick = runSelfTest();
        var startup = collectStartupDiagnostics();
        var measurements = collectLayoutMeasurements();
        var fullChecks = runSafeFullChecks();
        var exportBundle = getExportReport();
        var runtimeApi = window.FOCUS_NOTES_RUNTIME_DIAGNOSTICS;
        var runtimeErrors = runtimeApi && typeof runtimeApi.list === "function" ? runtimeApi.list() : [];
        var result = {
            version: DIAGNOSTIC_VERSION,
            buildMarker: DIAGNOSTIC_BUILD_MARKER,
            quick: quick,
            startup: startup,
            measurements: measurements,
            fullChecks: fullChecks,
            exportReport: exportBundle.report,
            exportError: exportBundle.error,
            runtimeErrors: runtimeErrors,
            warnings: collectEnvironmentWarnings()
        };
        result.status = diagnosticStatus(result);
        setDiagnosticHealth(result.status, result.status + " · " + quick.passed + "/" + quick.total + " built-in checks");
        renderFullDiagnosticSummary(result);
        return result;
    }

    function buildDiagnosticReport(diagnosticResult) {
        var result = diagnosticResult && diagnosticResult.quick ? diagnosticResult : runFullDiagnostics();
        var testResult = result.quick || { passed: 0, total: 0, summary: "Diagnostics did not run.", results: [] };
        var exportReport = result.exportReport;
        var exportChecks = exportReport && exportReport.checks ? exportReport.checks : [];
        var exportPassed = exportChecks.filter(function (item) { return item.pass; }).length;
        var allFailureItems = [];
        allFailureItems = allFailureItems.concat((testResult.results || []).filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }));
        allFailureItems = allFailureItems.concat(result.startup.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }));
        allFailureItems = allFailureItems.concat(result.fullChecks.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }));
        allFailureItems = allFailureItems.concat(exportChecks.filter(function (item) { return !item.pass; }));

        var lines = [
            "Focus Notes Diagnostics",
            "Privacy: Customer and ticket field contents are intentionally excluded.",
            "Scope: Local Focus Notes checks only. No network calls, browser storage, system inspection, or automatic repair.",
            "",
            "Status: " + result.status,
            "App Version: " + APP_VERSION,
            "Build Date: " + APP_BUILD_DATE,
            "Diagnostic Version: " + DIAGNOSTIC_VERSION,
            "Build Marker: " + DIAGNOSTIC_BUILD_MARKER,
            "Generated: " + new Date().toISOString(),
            "Page Protocol: " + String(window.location && window.location.protocol || "unknown"),
            "Browser: " + browserVersionLabel(),
            "Language: " + String(navigator.language || "unknown"),
            "Secure Context: " + (window.isSecureContext ? "Yes" : "No"),
            "Clipboard API: " + (navigator.clipboard && typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText === "function" ? "Available" : "Unavailable / fallback required"),
            "Device Pixel Ratio: " + String(window.devicePixelRatio || 1),
            "",
            "Failures First"
        ];
        lines = lines.concat(diagnosticFailureLines(allFailureItems));
        if (result.runtimeErrors.length) {
            result.runtimeErrors.forEach(function (item) {
                var location = item.source ? " (" + item.source + (item.line ? ":" + item.line : "") + (item.column ? ":" + item.column : "") + ")" : "";
                lines.push("- [FAIL] Runtime — " + item.type + ": " + item.message + location);
            });
        }

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("Warnings");
        if (!result.warnings.length) lines.push("- None");
        else result.warnings.forEach(function (warning) { lines.push("- " + warning); });

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("Startup Diagnostics");
        lines = lines.concat(diagnosticAllLines(result.startup));

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("Layout Measurements");
        result.measurements.forEach(function (item) { lines.push("- " + item.name + ": " + item.value); });

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("Full Diagnostic Checks");
        lines = lines.concat(diagnosticAllLines(result.fullChecks));

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("All Built-in Checks");
        lines.push("- Summary: " + testResult.summary);
        lines = lines.concat(diagnosticAllLines(testResult.results));

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("Export Validation");
        if (exportReport) {
            lines.push("- Summary: " + exportPassed + " of " + exportChecks.length + " checks passed.");
            lines = lines.concat(diagnosticAllLines(exportChecks));
        } else {
            lines.push("- [FAIL] Could not run: " + String(result.exportError || "Unknown export error").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim());
        }

        lines.push("");
        lines.push("Runtime Errors Captured: " + result.runtimeErrors.length);
        if (!result.runtimeErrors.length) {
            lines.push("- None");
        } else {
            result.runtimeErrors.forEach(function (item) {
                var location = item.source ? " (" + item.source + (item.line ? ":" + item.line : "") + (item.column ? ":" + item.column : "") + ")" : "";
                lines.push("- " + item.type + ": " + item.message + location);
            });
        }

        return redactDiagnosticReport(lines.join("\n"));
    }

    function copySelfTestDiagnostics() {
        var result = runFullDiagnostics();
        var report = buildDiagnosticReport(result);
        var copier = typeof window.forceCopy === "function" ? window.forceCopy : null;
        if (!copier) {
            if (typeof window.showStatus === "function") window.showStatus("⚠ Diagnostic Copy Unavailable");
            return Promise.resolve(false);
        }
        return Promise.resolve(copier(report)).then(function (copied) {
            if (typeof window.showStatus === "function") window.showStatus(copied ? "✓ Diagnostic Results Copied" : "⚠ Copy Manually");
            return copied;
        });
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_DIAGNOSTICS = Object.freeze({
        version: DIAGNOSTIC_VERSION,
        buildMarker: DIAGNOSTIC_BUILD_MARKER,
        runQuick: runDiagnostics,
        runFull: runFullDiagnostics,
        collectStartup: collectStartupDiagnostics,
        collectLayout: collectLayoutMeasurements,
        buildReport: buildDiagnosticReport,
        copy: copySelfTestDiagnostics
    });

    function initSelfTestCard() {
        var card = document.getElementById("selfTestCard");
        var toggle = document.getElementById("selfTestToggle");
        var body = document.getElementById("selfTestBody");
        var runButton = document.getElementById("runSelfTestButton");
        var runFullButton = document.getElementById("runFullDiagnosticsButton");
        var copyDiagnosticsButton = document.getElementById("copySelfTestDiagnosticsButton");
        if (!card || !toggle || !body || !runButton || !runFullButton || !copyDiagnosticsButton) return;

        body.hidden = true;
        card.classList.remove("is-expanded");
        toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
        toggle.textContent = "Show";

        toggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
            var expanded = body.hidden;
            body.hidden = !expanded;
            card.classList.toggle("is-expanded", expanded);
            toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", expanded ? "true" : "false");
            toggle.textContent = expanded ? "Hide" : "Show";
        });
        runButton.addEventListener("click", runDiagnostics);
        runFullButton.addEventListener("click", runFullDiagnostics);
        copyDiagnosticsButton.addEventListener("click", copySelfTestDiagnostics);
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initSelfTestCard, { once: true });
    else initSelfTestCard();
})();
</script>


<script id="commandBuilderAccordionScript">
(function () {
    "use strict";

    var panelIds = ["cerCommandBuilder", "cesCommandBuilder", "nidCommandBuilder"];

    function panelParts(panelId) {
        var prefix = panelId.replace("CommandBuilder", "");
        return {
            panel: document.getElementById(panelId),
            body: document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderBody"),
            toggle: document.getElementById(prefix + "BuilderToggle")
        };
    }

    function setCommandBuilderExpanded(panelId, expanded) {
        var parts = panelParts(panelId);
        if (!parts.panel || !parts.body || !parts.toggle) return false;

        parts.body.hidden = !expanded;
        parts.panel.classList.toggle("is-open", expanded);
        parts.toggle.setAttribute("aria-expanded", expanded ? "true" : "false");
        parts.toggle.textContent = expanded ? "Hide" : "Show";
        parts.toggle.setAttribute("aria-label", (expanded ? "Hide " : "Show ") + panelId.replace("CommandBuilder", "").toUpperCase() + " commands");
        return true;
    }

    function collapseAllCommandBuilders(exceptId) {
        panelIds.forEach(function (panelId) {
            if (panelId !== exceptId) setCommandBuilderExpanded(panelId, false);
        });
    }

    function initCommandBuilderAccordion() {
        panelIds.forEach(function (panelId) {
            var parts = panelParts(panelId);
            if (!parts.panel || !parts.body || !parts.toggle) return;

            setCommandBuilderExpanded(panelId, false);
            parts.toggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
                var shouldOpen = parts.body.hidden;
                collapseAllCommandBuilders(shouldOpen ? panelId : "");
                setCommandBuilderExpanded(panelId, shouldOpen);
            });
        });
    }

    window.collapseAllCommandBuilders = function () {
        collapseAllCommandBuilders("");
    };
    window.FOCUS_NOTES_COMMAND_ACCORDION = {
        panelIds: panelIds.slice(),
        setExpanded: setCommandBuilderExpanded,
        collapseAll: window.collapseAllCommandBuilders
    };

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initCommandBuilderAccordion, { once: true });
    } else {
        initCommandBuilderAccordion();
    }
})();
</script>

<script id="declarativeEventBootstrap">
(function () {
    "use strict";

    function runBottomAction(action) {
        if (action === "reset-form" && typeof window.resetForm === "function") {
            window.resetForm();
            return true;
        }
        if (action === "copy-notes" && typeof window.copyNotes === "function") {
            window.copyNotes();
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    function initializeBottomActionButtons() {
        var resetButton = document.querySelector('[data-action="reset-form"]');
        var copyButton = document.querySelector('[data-action="copy-notes"]');

        if (resetButton) {
            resetButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                runBottomAction("reset-form");
            });
        }
        if (copyButton) {
            copyButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
                runBottomAction("copy-notes");
            });
        }
    }

    window.FOCUS_NOTES_BOTTOM_ACTIONS = Object.freeze({
        run: runBottomAction
    });

    function initializeDeclarativeEvents() {
        updateAccountNumCount();
        initializeAutoGrowTextareas();
        initializeRegionalSupportDirectory();
        initializeBottomActionButtons();
        document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
            var actionElement = event.target.closest("[data-action]");
            if (actionElement) {
                var action = actionElement.getAttribute("data-action");
                if (action === "reset-form" || action === "copy-notes") return;
                return;
            }

            var copyElement = event.target.closest("[data-copy-value]");
            if (copyElement) quickCopy(copyElement.getAttribute("data-copy-value"), copyElement);
        });

        document.addEventListener("focusout", function (event) {
            if (event.target.matches("#brainDump")) clearQuickScratchScrollPosition(event.target);
            if (event.target.matches("#issue")) rememberIssueSelection(event.target);
            if (event.target.matches("#nextStep")) rememberNextStepSelection(event.target);
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-name="normalize"]')) formatCallerNameField(event.target);
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-company="normalize"]')) formatITCompanyField(event.target, false);
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-address="normalize"]')) formatAddressField(event.target);
        });

        document.addEventListener("keydown", function (event) {
            if (event.target.matches("#brainDump") && event.key !== "Tab") rememberQuickScratchScrollPosition(event.target);

            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-address="normalize"]') && event.key === "Enter") {
                event.preventDefault();
                formatAddressField(event.target);
                event.target.blur();
                return;
            }

        });

        document.addEventListener("beforeinput", function (event) {
            if (event.target.matches("#brainDump")) rememberQuickScratchScrollPosition(event.target);
        });

        document.addEventListener("keyup", function (event) {
            if (event.target.matches("#brainDump") && event.key !== "Tab") restoreQuickScratchScrollPosition(event.target);
        });

        document.addEventListener("input", function (event) {
            if (event.target.matches("#brainDump, #issue, #actions, #nextStep")) resizeAutoGrowTextarea(event.target, true);
            if (event.target.matches("#accountNum")) {
                normalizeAccountNumberField(event.target);
                updateAccountNumCount();
            }
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-company="normalize"]')) formatITCompanyField(event.target, true);
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-input="calculate-policer"]')) calculatePolicer();
        });

        document.addEventListener("change", function (event) {
            if (event.target.matches("#accountNum")) {
                normalizeAccountNumberField(event.target);
                updateAccountNumCount();
            }
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-change="calculate-policer"]')) calculatePolicer();
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-change="regional-support"]')) renderRegionalSupportContact();
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-change="issue-preset"]')) applyIssuePreset(event.target);
            if (event.target.matches('[data-action-change="next-step-preset"]')) applyNextStepPreset(event.target);
        });
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", initializeDeclarativeEvents, { once: true });
    } else {
        initializeDeclarativeEvents();
    }
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
